Method: 在真实rPPG数据集COHFACE与UBFC-rPPG上构造高保真深度伪造样本，分析伪造对自然生理信号与面部行为的影响；提出双向共注意力融合检测器，将rPPG与面部行为表示为token并联合建模，显式捕捉脉搏动态与面部运动间的跨层级依赖，学习鲁棒的联合真实性表征。评估采用受试者不相交的5折协议。

Result: 在换脸构造数据集上取得92.80% AUC，在动作迁移上取得96.78% AUC，优于仅rPPG单模态基线与最佳特征级融合方法；在固定两个特征提取器的情况下迁移至Celeb-DF-v2，达到91.20%准确率和86.08% AUC。

Conclusion: 联合建模rPPG与面部行为的跨层级融合检测显著优于单模态与晚期融合方案，并展现出在目标域适应场景下的可迁移性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent deepfake detection studies increasingly suggest remote photoplethysmography (rPPG) signals as an authenticity cue. However, existing benchmarks lack physiological ground truth, and current detectors underexplore the cross-level relationship between facial features and physiological dynamics, often relying on late fusion or rPPG features alone. In this paper, we construct high-fidelity deepfake manipulations on established real rPPG datasets (COHFACE and UBFC-rPPG) to investigate how forgeries disrupt natural physiological signals and facial behavior at the same time. Building on this analysis, we propose a bidirectional co-attention fusion detector that jointly models rPPG and facial behavior tokens. This mechanism explicitly captures the cross-level dependencies between pulse dynamics and facial motion to learn a robust, joint authenticity representation. Extensive experiments using a subject-disjoint 5-fold evaluation demonstrate the superiority of our approach. Achieving a 92.80\% AUC on constructed datasets using face swapping and 96.78\% AUC on motion transfer, our model outperforms both the rPPG-only single modality baseline and the best feature-level fusion methods. Furthermore, transfer-learning result of the fusion detector on Celeb-DF-v2 while keeping both feature extractors fixed achieves 91.20\% accuracy and 86.08\% AUC, which suggests applicability under target-domain adaptation.

</details>


### [108] [Detecting and Explaining Fake News Short Videos with Multimodal Content and Real-World Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12678)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Ming Tang,Jianxiong Guo,Liang Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究假新闻视频检测与解释（FNVDE）任务，提出NVKE-CEI统一系统，结合新闻视频关键帧提取（NVKE）与融合内容与证据信息的检测解释框架（CEI），性能超越现有最优基线并生成高质量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短视频平台已成为公众主要新闻来源，导致假新闻视频广泛传播。现有方法存在两大局限：一是常用帧选择策略可能遗漏真实性相关线索，或提供的时序上下文不足以理解新闻视频；二是先前方法忽视多模态理解或证据检索。

Method: 提出NVKE-CEI统一系统，包含两部分：NVKE关键帧提取方法，基于视觉与OCR文本相似度组合的时序变化选择关键帧；CEI检测解释框架，采用两个基于LLM的专门事实核查器（基于内容与基于证据），其输出由轻量级判决模型融合。

Result: 大量实验表明，NVKE-CEI优于当前最优基线，同时能生成高质量的、基于内容的解释。

Conclusion: NVKE-CEI通过关键帧提取与内容证据融合，有效解决现有FNVDE方法的局限，在检测性能与解释质量上均达到领先水平。

Abstract: Short-video platforms have become a primary news source for the public, which has also enabled the widespread dissemination of fake news videos. We study the task of fake news video detection and explanation (FNVDE). Existing methods face two critical limitations. First, commonly used frame selection strategies may omit veracity-relevant cues or provide insufficient temporal context for understanding news videos. Second, prior methods neglect either multimodal understanding or evidence retrieval. To address these limitations, we propose NVKE-CEI, a unified system that integrates a news video keyframes extraction method (NVKE) and an FNVDE framework leveraging both content and evidence information (CEI). NVKE selects keyframes based on chronological changes in combined visual and OCR-text similarity. CEI employs two specialized LLM-based fact checkers (content-based and evidence-based) whose outputs are fused by a lightweight judge model. Extensive experiments show that NVKE-CEI outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while generating high-quality content-grounded explanations.

</details>


### [109] [NOVA-GS: Noise-Aware View-Consistent Gaussian Splatting for Low-Light Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12682)
*Shaurya Pavan A,Vemunuri Divya Madhuri,Yash Pradeep Gawande,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NOVA-GS：面向低光照3D高斯泼溅的统一噪声感知框架，用VGGT前馈估计替代SfM获得位姿与几何，并耦合增强、自监督去噪与一致性优化，在真实低光数据集上提升几何保真度与颜色一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实低光场景重建面临严重传感器噪声、低信噪比与光度一致性退化，导致几何估计与新视角合成不稳定。现有方法或依赖良好光照参考数据做SfM初始化，或采用逐视角增强而引入跨视角不一致。

Method: 基于VGGT的前馈估计直接从退化输入获取稳健相机位姿与几何，免除SfM；在此初始化上集成三个耦合模块：结构感知曝光校正增强模块、带盲点掩码的自监督伪监督去噪模块、以及强制跨视角几何一致性的高斯泼溅优化；并提出噪声引导的球谐正则化以抑制噪声区域的视角相关伪影。

Result: 在多样真实低光数据集上的实验表明，几何保真度、颜色一致性和鲁棒性均有提升，且无需成对监督或良好光照参考。

Conclusion: NOVA-GS将增强、去噪与几何优化统一为单过程，为真实低光3D重建提供了不依赖SfM与参考数据的可行方案。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes under real-world low-light conditions remains challenging due to severe sensor noise, low signal-to-noise ratios, and degraded photometric consistency, which destabilize geometry estimation and novel view synthesis. Existing approaches often rely on well-lit reference data for reliable Structure-from-Motion (SfM) initialization under degraded inputs or apply per-view enhancement methods that introduce cross-view inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{NOVA-GS}, a unified noise-aware framework for low-light 3D Gaussian Splatting that subsumes enhancement, denoising, and geometry optimization within a single process. Our method leverages VGGT-based feed-forward estimation to obtain robust camera poses and geometry directly from degraded inputs, eliminating the need for SfM. Building on this initialization, NOVA-GS integrates three coupled components: a structure-aware enhancement module for exposure correction, a self-supervised denoising module with blind-spot masking for pseudo-supervision, and a consistency-driven Gaussian Splatting optimization enforcing cross-view geometric coherence. We further introduce a noise-guided spherical harmonic regularization to suppress view-dependent artifacts in noisy regions. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world low-light datasets demonstrate improved geometric fidelity, color consistency, and robustness without requiring paired supervision or well-lit references. https://shaurya2524.github.io/nova-gs/

</details>


### [110] [Semantically Aligned Gradient-Driven Context-Preserving Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12691)
*Chiranjeev Chiranjeev,Muskan Dosi,Mayank Vatsa,Richa Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IABEdit 将可微的语义验证嵌入训练过程，用冻结 VLM 的空间感知描述符与生成输出的对齐残差作为梯度，教生成器“改什么、改哪里”，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升指令图像编辑的结构保真与定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导的图像编辑存在训练时盲点：生成式编辑器从不被要求语义验证输出是否真的满足指令，监督仅止于重建与输入文本层面的条件化，导致编辑不完整、空间溢出和定位差。

Method: 提出模型无关框架 IABEdit，把可微的语义验证注入训练。冻结的视觉语言模型从真实编辑结果中提取空间感知描述符，可训练的对齐器再从生成输出中复现这些描述符，两者的残差作为梯度，指导生成器同时学习编辑内容与编辑位置，且推理阶段无 VLM 开销。该框架兼容多种骨干（U-Net/Stable Diffusion、MMDiT/FLUX），无需改动其推理流程。

Result: 在 MagicBrush 上，结构保真度较最佳扩散基线提升 +3.49 DINO-I，较最佳总体基线提升 +1.26，指令对齐仍具竞争力；在 RealEdit 和 EMU Edit 上基于嵌入的指标达到最优指令遵循性能；在 D-LORD 监控基准上，于严重遮挡条件下比专有 Gemini agent 高出 +5.13 DINO-P；人类评估与 GPT-4o 评估均确认编辑感知精确、定位良好。

Conclusion: 梯度对齐的 VLM 蒸馏在接近真实世界的监控与遮挡条件下依然有效，表明该训练时语义验证范式能显著提升指令图像编辑的保真度与定位精度，且不牺牲推理效率。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a training-time blind spot. Generative editors are never required to semantically verify whether their outputs actually satisfy the instruction. Supervision stops at reconstruction and input textual-level conditioning. This produces incomplete edits, spatial spillover, and poor localization. We present IABEdit, a model-agnostic framework that embeds differentiable semantic verification into training. A frozen vision-language model extracts spatially-aware descriptors from the ground-truth edit. A trainable aligner then reproduces them from the generated output. The residual between the two becomes a gradient that teaches the generator both what to edit and where, with no inference-time VLM cost. IABEdit is compatible with diverse backbones, including U-Net (Stable Diffusion) and MMDiT (FLUX), without altering their inference pipelines. On MagicBrush, it improves structural fidelity by +3.49 DINO-I over the best diffusion baseline and +1.26 over the best overall baseline, while remaining competitive on instruction alignment. It also achieves state-of-the-art instruction adherence performance on RealEdit and EMU Edit benchmarks based on embedding-based metrics. Most consequentially, on the D-LORD surveillance benchmark, it surpasses the proprietary Gemini agent by +5.13 DINO-P under heavy occlusion, where preserving identity is hardest. This shows that gradient-aligned VLM distillation holds up under real-world-like surveillance and occlusion conditions. Human and GPT-4o evaluations confirm perceptually precise, well-localized edits.

</details>


### [111] [ExpertHTR: Unified Handwritten Text Recognition with Multi-Task Learning and Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12705)
*Dang Hoai Nam,Nguyen Duy Hieu,Quang Huu Hieu,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ExpertHTR统一视觉-语言框架，通过互补监督与条件化模型容量实现异构手写文本识别资源的联合页面级训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写文本识别资源规模小，且分散在语言、文字系统、文档结构和标注格式各异的多个集合中，导致难以进行联合的页面级训练。

Method: 将异构数据集的结构标注统一组织为Page-Region-Line表示，构建四个相关训练任务（完整转写、物理行覆盖、文本定位、局部识别），无需额外人工标注；在联合训练的稠密模型基础上引入稀疏混合专家架构，包含常激活共享分支与条件路由的全MLP专家；Sparsegen使激活的路由专家数量随隐表示变化，路由正则化抑制对少数专家的持续集中。

Result: 在七个异构手写基准上，互补监督较仅页面转写训练持续提升；联合多源训练在多数数据集上进一步增益；稀疏专家模型在七个来源中的六个上优于稠密基线；最终统一模型在多数基准上大幅超越所评估的通用OCR与视觉-语言系统，并在IAM段落级基准上达到最优性能，但在若干困难集合上专用HTR系统仍更强。

Conclusion: 互补监督与条件化模型容量可有效缓解异构手写资源联合训练的困难，统一模型在多数基准上优于通用OCR与视觉-语言系统，但专用HTR系统在部分困难数据集上仍具优势。

Abstract: Handwritten text recognition resources are often small and distributed across collections that differ in language, script, document structure, and annotation format, making joint page-level training difficult. We propose ExpertHTR, a unified vision-language framework that addresses this problem through complementary supervision and conditional model capacity. Structural annotations from heterogeneous datasets are first organized through a common Page-Region-Line representation and used to construct four related training tasks for complete transcription, physical-line coverage, text localization, and localized recognition, without requiring additional manual labels. Building on a jointly trained dense model, ExpertHTR introduces a sparse Mixture-of-Experts architecture with an always-active shared branch and conditionally routed full-MLP experts. Sparsegen allows the number of active routed experts to vary with the hidden representation, while routing regularization reduces persistent concentration on a small subset of experts. Experiments on seven heterogeneous handwriting benchmarks show that complementary supervision consistently improves training with page transcription alone, while joint multi-source training provides further gains on most datasets. The proposed sparse expert model further improves the dense baseline on six of the seven sources. The final unified model also substantially outperforms the evaluated general-purpose OCR and vision-language systems on most benchmarks and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the IAM paragraph-level benchmark, while specialized HTR systems remain stronger on several challenging collections.

</details>


### [112] [Multimodal Floorplan Encoding: Learning Dense Modality-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12723)
*Xavier Anadón,Rémi Pautrat,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态平面图编码器（MMFE），将多种2D室内表示映射到共享致密潜网格，实现跨模态密集匹配、几何对齐与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面图表示形式多样（矢量CAD、栅格渲染、传感器密度图等），这种异质性导致难以构建可跨模态迁移并支持几何中心任务（如对齐、检索）的学习系统。

Method: MMFE 结合冻结的 DINOv3 骨干与可训练的 DPT 头；采用逐单元 InfoNCE 目标，对齐跨模态空间对应区域并将其他单元作为负样本；引入受控相似变换，并通过特征网格变形施加几何一致性约束。

Result: 在 Structured3D 及留出的域外数据集上，MMFE 提升了跨模态密集匹配，结合 RANSAC 实现稳健相似对齐，并配合学习聚合取得强检索性能。

Conclusion: 利用冻结视觉基础模型与致密对比学习，MMFE 可作为统一的多模态平面图编码器，支持几何中心任务并在跨域场景中保持有效性。

Abstract: Floorplans arise in many forms, from vector CAD drawings to raster renderings and sensor-derived density maps. This heterogeneity makes it difficult to build learning systems that transfer across modalities and support geometry-centric tasks such as alignment and retrieval. We introduce the Multimodal Floorplan Encoder (MMFE), which maps diverse 2D indoor representations into a shared dense latent grid. MMFE combines a frozen DINOv3 backbone with a trainable Dense Prediction Transformer (DPT) head, and is trained with a per-cell Information Noise-Contrastive Estimation (InfoNCE) objective that aligns spatially corresponding regions across modalities while using all other cells as negatives. To improve robustness to geometric distortions, we incorporate controlled similarity transformations and enforce geometric consistency through feature-grid warping. On Structured3D, a held-out out-of-domain dataset, MMFE improves cross-modal dense matching, enables robust similarity alignment with RANSAC, and yields strong retrieval when paired with learned aggregation.

</details>


### [113] [GRACE: Adaptive Concept Erasure with Geometry-Guided Retention in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12731)
*Qinghui Gong,Yihuai Liang,Yuanlun Xie,Deepak Kumar Jain,Vitomir Štruc,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRACE框架，实现T2I扩散模型中局部化、选择性的概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法缺乏对参数更新的显式约束，易导致过度干预和语义漂移；且依赖人工构造的反事实监督（如替代提示），数据构建成本高，难以扩展到新概念。

Method: 1）语义加权敏感子空间估计，精确锁定干预方向；2）轻量级子空间约束适配器，防止全局语义扰动；3）自动解耦安全锚机制，消除对人工提示工程的依赖；4）能量驱动动态门控机制，在推理时自适应控制干预时机与强度。

Result: 相比五种SOTA概念擦除方法的平均性能，细粒度NSFW降低率提升17.86%，宏观平均目标CLIP Score和面向保真度的FID分别降低4.75%和50.58%。

Conclusion: GRACE在擦除有效性与生成保真度之间取得更优平衡，实现更强概念抑制的同时显著提升原模型生成能力的保持。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models inevitably internalize sensitive or non-compliant concepts from large-scale pretraining data, necessitating post-hoc concept erasure. However, existing erasure methods often lack explicit constraints on parameter updates, leading to over-intervention and unintended semantic drift. In addition, many methods rely on manually crafted counterfactual supervision, such as surrogate prompts, which incurs substantial data construction costs that limit scalability to new concepts. To address these limitations, we propose GRACE, a structured concept erasure framework designed to enable localized and selective intervention. Specifically, we introduce a semantically weighted sensitive subspace estimation to precisely lock intervention directions, and employ lightweight subspace-constrained adapters to prevent global semantic disturbance. To eliminate the dependency on manual prompt engineering, we design an automatically decoupled safe-anchor mechanism. To mitigate semantic drift induced by excessive intervention, we introduce an energy-driven dynamic gating mechanism that adaptively controls the timing and strength of intervention at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a superior balance between erasure effectiveness and generation fidelity. Compared with the average performance of five state-of-the-art (SOTA) concept erasure methods, our method improves the fine-grained NSFW reduction rate by $17.86\%$, while reducing the macro-averaged target CLIP Score and preservation-oriented Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) by $4.75\%$ and $50.58\%$, respectively, indicating stronger concept suppression with substantially improved preservation of the original model's generative utility.

</details>


### [114] [AquaCubeAI-Powered Monitoring Turbidity on-board Φsat-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12744)
*Pietro Di Stasio,Francesca Razzano,Elisa Liparulo,Gabriele Meoni,Nicolas Longépé,Deodato Tapete,Paolo Gamba,Gilda Schirinzi,Silvia Liberata Ullo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AquaCubeAI 提出一种轻量级机器学习方法，在 Φsat-2 多光谱影像上实现星上沿海水体浊度估计，将推理从地面段迁移至卫星端，以降低时延并适配星载算力与带宽约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卫星工作流依赖下行链路与地面处理，引入的时延限制了对快速演变浊度事件的响应能力；需在星载严格算力与带宽约束下实现低时延、更具操作性的浊度监测。

Method: 采用与 CMEMS HR-OC 浊度产品空间对齐的模拟 Φsat-2 采集数据（覆盖四个欧洲海洋大区局部沿海站点）训练模型；使用空间分块划分协议抑制训练与评估子集间的数据泄漏；模型为紧凑型 MLP 浊度回归器，并通过参数共享重构实现密集空间预测，支持浊度制图与基于阈值的异常掩膜；在 Intel Myriad VPU 上完成嵌入式部署验证。

Result: 在 Intel Myriad VPU 上的嵌入式部署证实了低功耗硬件的可行性，并支持从多光谱输入进行低时延推理；空间分块划分提供了考虑空间相关性下的泛化评估。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了星上轻量级浊度估计在嵌入式硬件上的可行性，可在星载约束下提供更低时延、更及时的沿海水质监测决策支持。

Abstract: Timely monitoring of coastal water quality is critical for environmental protection, yet conventional satellite workflows rely on downlink and ground processing, introducing latency that can limit responsiveness to rapidly evolving turbidity events. To address this limitation, we propose AquaCubeAI, a lightweight machine-learning approach for onboard estimation of coastal water turbidity from Φsat-2 multispectral imagery. By shifting inference from the ground segment to the satellite, AquaCubeAI aims to enable lower-latency, more responsive, and more operationally useful turbidity monitoring under the strict compute and bandwidth constraints of spaceborne platforms. The model is trained on simulated Φsat-2 acquisitions spatially aligned with Copernicus Marine Service (CMEMS) High-Resolution Ocean Color (HR-OC) turbidity products over selected localized coastal sites spanning four European marine macro-regions. To provide a realistic evaluation of generalization in the presence of spatial correlation, we adopt a spatial block splitting protocol that mitigates data leakage between training and evaluation subsets. The main contributions of this work are: (i) a scalable dataset generation pipeline pairing simulated Φsat-2 multispectral patches with CMEMS HR-OC turbidity labels across selected localized European coastal sites; (ii) a compact Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-based turbidity regressor trained under a leakage-aware geospatial split and tailored to embedded constraints; and (iii) a reformulation for dense spatial prediction via parameter sharing, enabling turbidity mapping and simple threshold-based anomaly masks for onboard decision logic. Embedded deployment on an Intel Myriad Vision Processing Unit (VPU) further confirms the feasibility of low-power hardware and supports low-latency inference from multispectral inputs.

</details>


### [115] [Same Encoder, Different Winner: A Paired-View Framework for Cell Painting Encoder Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12761)
*Tim Treis,Nikita Moshkov,Johan Fredin Haslum,Shantanu Singh,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CP-BG-Bench配对视图评测框架，固定中心细胞、消融或增强周围像素，在3个数据集、3个编码器和4种协议上评测，发现单指标排序对协议高度敏感，分歧可分解为三条轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cell Painting视觉编码器通常仅用单一评测（常见为replicate mAP）排序，这种单指标排名可能掩盖编码器在不同任务上的真实差异。作者希望用受控干预揭示背景与上下文对评测结论的影响。

Method: 提出CP-BG-Bench配对视图评测框架：保持中心细胞固定，构造四种匹配视图（原始裁剪C、分割S、密度增强变体CD与SD），对周围像素做受控消融或增强。在JUMP-CP、RxRx1、RxRx3-core三个数据集与DINOv3 ViT-B/16、OpenPhenom、SubCell三个编码器上，按四种社区标准协议（replicate mAP、scIB批次整合、CellProfiler特征预测、跨批次扰动召回）进行评测。

Result: 四种协议对同一批编码器的排序系统性不同，分歧沿三条轴分解：细胞与背景、形态与上下文、研究内与跨批次。最大效应：RxRx3-core上SubCell分割输入保留94%裁剪replicate mAP但仅保留32%裁剪R@10，研究内信号基本不可迁移；密度增强恢复84%研究内C-to-S差距但仅恢复8%跨批次差距。分割视图在三个数据集中两个上对CellProfiler特征预测不劣于甚至优于裁剪，反转replicate mAP排序；C-to-S差距跨数据集相差一个数量级，而跨编码器相近，说明背景增益由实验设计而非编码器决定。

Conclusion: Cell Painting编码器的单指标排名对所用协议敏感，协议间分歧可解释为配对视图设计所暴露的三条轴上的投影。作者将公开配对视图数据集、重建流程、36个训练检查点、聚合嵌入及完整评测套件。

Abstract: Vision encoders for Cell Painting are typically ranked by a single evaluation, commonly replicate mean average precision (mAP). We introduce CP-BG-Bench, a paired-view evaluation framework that holds the central cell fixed across four matched views (raw crop C, segmented S, and density-augmented variants CD and SD), ablating or augmenting surrounding pixels as a controlled intervention. Instantiating the framework on three datasets (JUMP-CP, RxRx1, RxRx3-core) and three encoders (DINOv3 ViT-B/16, OpenPhenom, SubCell) under four community-standard protocols (replicate mAP, scIB batch integration, CellProfiler feature prediction, cross-batch perturbation recall), we find that the four protocols rank the same encoders systematically differently, with disagreements decomposing along three axes: cell versus background, morphology versus context, and within-study versus across-batch. The largest effect: on RxRx3-core, SubCell with segmented inputs retains 94% of crop replicate mAP but only 32% of crop R@10, so the within-study signal preserved under segmentation is largely non-transferable; density augmentation recovers 84% of the within-study C-to-S gap but only 8% of the cross-batch gap. Segmented views predict CellProfiler features as well as or better than crops on two of three datasets, inverting the replicate-mAP ranking, and the C-to-S gap varies by an order of magnitude across datasets while remaining similar across encoders, indicating that background-driven gain is set by experimental design rather than by the encoder. Single-metric ranking of Cell Painting encoders is therefore sensitive to the protocol used, and protocol disagreements are interpretable as projections onto the three axes the paired-view design exposes. We will release the paired-view datasets, reconstruction pipelines, 36 trained checkpoints, aggregated embeddings, and the full evaluation suite.

</details>


### [116] [LG-PF: Lightweight Confidence-Guided Polarization Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12787)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Zhenyu Kuang,Gao Wang,Yang Liu,Haishu Tan,Xiaosong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量级置信度引导的偏振图像融合框架LG-PF，将偏振融合建模为选择性残差迁移过程，并构建多场景数据集MSP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏振图像融合需结合S0图像的稳定亮度与结构信息，以及线偏振度（DoLP）图像中材料敏感的细节。但DoLP的可靠性具有空间差异性，不加区分地迁移偏振信息可能放大不稳定响应，并破坏由S0主导的结构外观。因此需要一种能够空间选择性地利用偏振信息的融合方法。

Method: 提出LG-PF框架，将偏振融合表述为选择性残差迁移：1）偏振置信先验（Polarization Confidence Prior）估计空间上可靠的偏振响应；2）掩膜引导的多尺度融合模块（Mask-guided Multi-scale Fusion）在三个特征尺度上调控偏振信息迁移；3）轻量级上下文感知有界校正头（Lightweight Context-aware Bounded Correction Head）稳定局部光度与结构过渡。置信度引导同时融入优化目标，以保留可靠偏振细节并抑制无支撑响应。此外构建MSP数据集，包含17类室内外场景的1000对像素对齐图像。

Result: 在MSP数据集上，LG-PF在全部六项评价指标上均取得最优结果；在PIF和GAND数据集上的子集评估显示其在无需微调的情况下具有良好迁移性。模型仅0.2936 M参数量，单张图像推理时间21.712 ms，以低计算代价实现有竞争力的融合质量。

Conclusion: LG-PF通过置信度引导的选择性偏振残差迁移，有效解决DoLP空间可靠性差异问题，兼顾融合质量与轻量化效率；MSP数据集为多场景偏振融合提供基准。源代码、数据集及官方数据划分将于发表后公开。

Abstract: Polarization image fusion combines the stable luminance and structural information of the total- intensity image S0 with the material-sensitive details of the degree of linear polarization (DoLP) image. However, the reliability of DoLP varies spatially, and indiscriminate polarization transfer may amplify unstable responses or disturb the structural appearance anchored by S0. We therefore propose LG-PF, a lightweight confidence-guided framework that formulates polarization fusion as a selective residual transfer process. A Polarization Confidence Prior estimates spatially reliable polarization responses, a Mask-guided Multi-scale Fusion module regulates their transfer across three feature scales, and a Lightweight Context-aware Bounded Correction Head stabilizes local photometric and structural transitions. Confidence guidance is also incorporated into the optimization objectives to preserve reliable polarization details while suppressing unsupported responses. We also construct MSP, a multi-scene polarization fusion dataset containing 1000 pixel-aligned image pairs from 17 indoor and outdoor scene categories. LG-PF achieves the best results across all six evaluated metrics on MSP, while subset-based evaluations on PIF and GAND show promising transferability without fine-tuning. With only 0.2936 M parameters and an inference time of 21.712 ms per image, LG-PF achieves competitive fusion quality with low computational cost. The source code, dataset, and official data splits will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [117] [LGFN: Lightweight Gated RGB-Polarization Fusion with Modality-Availability Conditioning for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12798)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Xiaosong Li,Yang Liu,Tao Ye,Haishu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出轻量级门控RGB-偏振融合框架LGFN，通过模态路由器与门控机制实现可分离优化的RGB单模态与偏振辅助配置，在伪装目标检测中兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测中目标与背景高度相似，偏振成像可提供互补物理线索，但现有方法通常假设固定的多模态输入配置，并将偏振内部协同与RGB-偏振交互耦合在一起，缺乏灵活性且计算开销大。

Method: 提出LGFN框架，包含：确定性模态路由器（Modality Router）根据偏振可用性选择配置；多模态配置下，可用性条件模态门（Modality Gate）校准可用偏振分支，门控偏振中枢（Gated Polarization Hub）协调学习的DoLP与AoP表征和显式偏振线索，RGB-偏振交叉融合（RGB-Polarization Cross Fusion）通过受控残差交互将协调表征引入RGB层级。推理时无需样本相关统计或手工质量描述子。

Result: 在完整230幅PCOD_1200测试集上，RGB单模态配置取得MAE 0.0090、Dice 0.8806、IoU 0.8144，在参评RGB方法中六项指标均最优；在通用局部重评估协议下，多模态配置六项指标全面优于PolarNet与IPNet；相较IPNet，参数量、浮点运算量与延迟分别降低53.1%、73.6%、63.0%。

Conclusion: LGFN通过解耦偏振内部协同与RGB-偏振交互，实现了灵活、轻量且高性能的伪装目标检测，在精度与效率上均优于现有多模态方法。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) is an important engineering task in intelligent optical perception, but it remains challenging when targets closely resemble their surroundings. Polarization imaging provides complementary physical cues, whereas existing methods typically assume fixed multimodal input configurations and entangle intra-polarization coordination with interaction between red-green-blue (RGB) and polarization representations. We propose LGFN, a lightweight gated RGB-polarization fusion framework supporting separately optimized RGB-only and polarization-assisted configurations. A deterministic Modality Router selects the appropriate configuration according to polarization availability. In the multimodal configuration, an availability-conditioned Modality Gate calibrates the available polarization branches; the Gated Polarization Hub coordinates learned degree of linear polarization (DoLP) and angle of polarization (AoP) representations with explicit polarization cues; and RGB-Polarization Cross Fusion introduces the coordinated representation into the RGB hierarchy through controlled residual interaction. The multimodal configuration requires neither sample-dependent statistics nor handcrafted quality descriptors during inference. On the complete 230-image PCOD_1200 test set, the RGB-only configuration achieves a mean absolute error of 0.0090, a Dice score of 0.8806, and an intersection over union of 0.8144, obtaining the best results on all six metrics among the evaluated RGB-based methods. Under a common local reevaluation protocol, the multimodal configuration outperforms PolarNet and IPNet on all six metrics. Relative to IPNet, it reduces the parameter count, floating-point operations, and latency by 53.1%, 73.6%, and 63.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [118] [Online Video Agent Harness for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12818)
*Sen Yang,Boqiang Duan,Jing Yang,Weihao Bo,Jie Liu,Boyuan Tong,Ze Feng,Wenkang Zhang,Jingdong Wang,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoXAgent 是一个面向长视频理解的纯在线视频智能体框架，从视频文件和用户查询出发，动态规划、按需调用异构专家工具并聚合多模态证据以生成答案，在多个长视频基准上以约 50k token 的上下文占用达到与前沿 LMM 及视频智能体相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解类似"大海捞针"：与查询相关的证据在长时间跨度上稀疏分布，而将密集帧塞入单一 VLM 上下文会带来上下文腐化（context rot）和高昂成本。现有视频智能体依赖与查询无关的离线预处理或临时拼凑的工具集，容易遗漏查询相关细节并浪费算力。

Method: 提出 VideoXAgent 纯在线视频智能体工具链：从给定视频与用户查询出发，进行任务规划与分解，按需调用专用专家工具，聚合多模态证据并解决观测冲突以输出最终答案。设计了基于数据驱动的原子能力分类指导的异构专家工具体系，涵盖脚本、VLM 及领域模型（如检测、OCR、ASR、人脸识别）；并通过客观证据提示与预算感知控制抑制幻觉和无法终止的问题。

Result: 在 Video-MME-Long、LongVideoBench-Long、LVBench 和 MINERVA 上，VideoXAgent 以更小的上下文占用（每样本约 50k token，即使对时长一小时的视频）与前沿 LMM 和视频智能体性能相当。在 MINERVA 等复杂视频推理基准上，其仅用约 15% 于 1024 帧密集打包基线的上下文即达到同等水平。此外，即使使用视觉能力较弱甚至纯文本的编排器，该框架仍然有效。

Conclusion: 强有力的长视频理解可以来自渐进式的智能体证据搜寻，而非将完整视频打包进单一上下文；按需调用异构专家工具并以小上下文足迹运行，是长视频理解的有效范式。

Abstract: Long video understanding often behaves like a visual needle-in-a-haystack problem: query-relevant evidence is sparsely distributed across long temporal spans, while packing dense frames into a single VLM context incurs \textit{context rot} and high cost. Existing video agents often rely on query-agnostic offline preprocessing or ad hoc tool sets, which can miss query-specific details and waste computation. In this work, we present VideoXAgent, a purely online video-agent harness for long video understanding that starts from the given video file and user query, plans and decomposes the task, invokes specialized expert tools on demand, and aggregates multimodal evidence to produce a final answer while resolving conflicts among observations. To support this on-demand invocation, we design a suite of heterogeneous expert tools guided by a data-driven taxonomy of atomic capabilities, spanning scripts, VLMs, and domain models (e.g., detection, OCR, ASR, face recognition). The harness further enforces objective evidence prompting and budget-aware control to curb hallucination and non-termination. Across Video-MME-Long, LongVideoBench-Long, LVBench, and MINERVA, VideoXAgent is competitive with frontier LMMs and video agents under a smaller context footprint---about 50k tokens of agent context per sample, even on hour-long videos. In particular, on complex video-reasoning benchmarks such as MINERVA, it matches this level while using only about 15\% of the context of a 1,024-frame dense-packing baseline. Notably, the harness remains effective with a visually weak or even text-only orchestrator, suggesting that strong long-video understanding can emerge from progressive agentic evidence seeking rather than from packing the full video into a single context. Project page: https://go-agent-x.github.io/video_agent_harness/

</details>


### [119] [SCDM: Spatial-Contextual Disentanglement Mamba via Differential Inference for Efficient Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12825)
*Mustafa Bora Çelik,Hayriye Aktaş Dinçer,Ayse Keles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SCDM（Spatial-Contextual Differential Mamba），一种非对称双分支状态空间模型架构，通过正分支提取病变判别特征、负分支建模并抑制正常解剖上下文，实现表征解耦，在RSNA肺炎数据集上以更少参数和计算量取得竞争性分类与精准定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于状态空间模型（如VMamba）的医学图像方法难以将细微病理特征与视觉相似的健康解剖背景区分开来，其表征往往是纠缠的，缺乏将疾病信号与正常解剖结构显式分离的机制。

Method: 提出SCDM非对称双分支架构：Positive Branch提取判别性特征，Negative Branch主动建模并抑制正常解剖上下文；通过相似度驱动的排斥门（similarity-driven repulsion gate）和差分推理规则（differential inference rule）实现竞争性特征学习，无需额外分支标签，且不增加模型容量。

Result: 在RSNA Pneumonia数据集上，SCDM取得具有竞争力的分类性能（AUC 0.858），同时参数量（29.4M）和FLOPs（1.44G）显著低于标准VMamba与视觉Transformer基线；激活分析表明其差分机制可实现高精度定位，通过抑制无关解剖干扰有效分离病灶。

Conclusion: SCDM通过差分式双分支表征解耦，在保持轻量高效的同时实现了竞争性分类性能与精准病灶定位，为医学图像中病理信号与正常解剖背景的分离提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: State Space Models (SSMs), particularly VMamba, have emerged as efficient alternatives for modeling long-range dependencies in medical image analysis. However, distinguishing subtle pathological features from visually similar anatomical backgrounds remains a significant challenge. Existing SSM architectures often learn entangled representations, lacking explicit mechanisms to separate disease-specific signals from normal anatomy. To address this limitation, we propose Spatial-Contextual Differential Mamba (SCDM), an asymmetric dual-branch architecture designed for selective representational disentanglement. SCDM introduces a Positive Branch for extracting discriminative features and a Negative Branch that actively models and suppresses normal anatomical context. This separation is achieved through a similarity-driven repulsion gate and a differential inference rule, which promote competitive feature learning without requiring additional branch labels or increasing model capacity. Evaluated on the RSNA Pneumonia dataset, SCDM achieves competitive classification performance (AUC of 0.858) while requiring significantly fewer parameters (29.4M) and FLOPs (1.44G) compared to standard VMamba and vision transformer baselines. Furthermore, activation analyses demonstrate that our differential mechanism yields highly precise localization, effectively isolating lesions by inhibiting irrelevant anatomical distractors.

</details>


### [120] [CoralscapesV2: Panoptic and Fine-Grained Visual Scene Understanding in Coral Reefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12826)
*Jonathan Sauder,Thomas Ruckli,Gabrielė Strodomskytė,Ibrahim Souleiman Abdallah,Rahma Hassan Abdi,Djama Goumaneh Awaleh,Mohamed Houssein Farah,Moustapha Nour,Osama Sharhubil Saad,Mustafa Mohammed Khalafallah Altaib,Maysoon Kteifan,Farah Alsoqi,Eyad Zgool,Jafar Al-Omari,Temesgen Gebremeskel Gebreluel,Zekaria Zekeria Abdulkerim,Meron Ghirmay,Teklehaimanot Beraki,Devis Tuia,Guilhem Banc-Prandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoralscapesV2是珊瑚礁通用视觉场景理解数据集，扩展至95个细粒度类别，并提供65k鱼实例掩码标注，首次实现珊瑚礁全景分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球珊瑚礁衰退需要大幅扩大生态监测规模，而数据采集范式已从高度标准化的调查图像转向可扩展平台上的非受限影像，因此亟需设计能从通用礁石影像中获得细粒度理解的机器学习方法，现有数据集的规模、范围和标注完整性不足。

Method: 构建CoralscapesV2数据集：扩大规模与范围，提升语义分割标注的完整性与质量；将类别从39类扩展至95个细粒度视觉类别；借助视频对鱼进行完整标注，提供65k个穷尽式鱼实例掩码标注；覆盖野外多种场景，构建语义与实例分割基准。

Result: CoralscapesV2成为珊瑚礁领域首个全景分割数据集，构成对当代语义分割与实例分割模型的挑战性基准；同时表明仅基于静态图像进行鱼类标注是不充分的。

Conclusion: CoralscapesV2是迈向珊瑚礁通用全景分割的重要一步，可应用于从机器人或手持视频的底栖覆盖制图，到自动量化与理解鱼类行为及鱼-礁相互作用等广泛场景，对扩大珊瑚礁监测规模具有重要意义。

Abstract: In order to design conservation and restoration strategies to counter the global decline of coral reefs, ecological monitoring of reefs needs to be scaled up dramatically. Computer vision methods are increasingly used to tackle the vast amount of data: as the paradigm of data collection in reefs shifts from highly standardized and constrained survey images to unconstrained imagery on scalable platforms, it is necessary to design machine learning methods that help to get a fine-grained understanding of reefs from general-purpose reef imagery. This paper provides CoralscapesV2, an extension of the Coralscapes dataset for general-purpose visual scene understanding in reefs. CoralscapesV2 increases the dataset size, scope, label completeness and quality for semantic segmentation, and extends the number of classes from 39 to 95 fine-grained visual categories. Furthermore, CoralscapesV2 provides 65k exhaustive fish instance mask annotations, meticulously annotated to completeness by using the video, revealing that annotation of fish based on only static images is insufficient. CoralscapesV2 is the first dataset for panoptic segmentation in coral reefs, capturing a wide range of scenarios in the wild, posing a challenging benchmark for contemporary semantic segmentation and instance segmentation models. CoralscapesV2 is an important step towards general-purpose panoptic segmentation in coral reefs, which has substantial implications for scaling up coral reef monitoring, as it can be employed in a wide range of applications from benthic cover mapping from robot or handheld videos to designing methods for automated quantification and understanding of fish behavior and fish-reef interactions.

</details>


### [121] [A Dual Cross-Attention Framework for Colposcopic CIN Grading and Swede Score Prediction Using a New Multi-Center Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12827)
*Dania Khan,Nuzhat Aisha Shaikh,Asfina Hassan Juicy,Raiyun Kabir,S M Shahida,Taufiq Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出双流交叉注意力深度学习框架，用于宫颈上皮内瘤变(CIN)自动分级和Swede评分预测，并发布BUET多中心阴道镜数据集；CIN三分类准确率71.85%、AUC-ROC 86.23%，Swede评分各分量AUC-ROC为75.7%–88.4%，总分MAE为1.489。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈癌是全球重大健康挑战，疾病负担在低中收入国家(LMICs)尤为突出，原因是缺乏受训专科医生，且基于阴道镜的筛查具有主观性。因此需要自动化、客观、可辅助风险分层的AI筛查工具。

Method: 1) 构建双流交叉注意力(dual-stream cross-attention)架构，显式融合成对的对比性宫颈图像(cervigrams)，模拟专家阴道镜医师比较性评估组织反应的视觉推理过程；2) 设计复合损失函数(custom composite loss)，以缓解五类Swede评分分量中严重的类别不平衡与评分不一致问题；3) 构建并标注BUET多中心阴道镜数据集，用于Swede评分预测与CIN分级。

Result: CIN三分类：准确率71.85%，AUC-ROC 86.23%，优于现有方法；Swede评分分量预测：AUC-ROC介于75.7%至88.4%，复合损失函数带来F1-score的持续提升；Swede总分(0–10)预测：平均绝对误差(MAE)为1.489。数据集与源代码已公开。

Conclusion: 所提方法可为资源受限医疗环境下开发AI辅助阴道镜筛查工具、支持基于风险的分诊(triage)奠定基础。

Abstract: Cervical cancer is a major global health challenge, with disease burden falling disproportionately on low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) due to a shortage of trained specialists and the subjective nature of colposcopy-based screening. To address this challenge, we propose a novel deep learning framework for the automated grading of Cervical Intraepithelial Neoplasia (CIN) and the prediction of clinical Swede scores. We also introduce the BUET Multi-Center Colposcopy Dataset, a novel, multi-center cohort designed and annotated for Swede score prediction and CIN grading. Our proposed dual-stream cross-attention architecture mimics the visual reasoning of an expert colposcopist by explicitly fusing paired multimodal cervigrams to evaluate comparative tissue responses. Furthermore, we introduce a custom composite loss function to address severe class imbalances and scoring inconsistencies across the five Swede score components. The proposed framework achieved 71.85% accuracy and an 86.23% AUC-ROC for three-class CIN grading, outperforming existing methods. For Swede score component prediction, the architecture achieved AUC-ROC values ranging from 75.7% to 88.4%, with the composite loss function yielding consistent F1-score improvements. Finally, the total predicted Swede Score, which ranges between 0 and 10, shows a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 1.489. The results show that the proposed method can pave the way towards developing AI-assisted colposcopy screening tools to support risk-based triage in resource-limited healthcare settings. The dataset and source code are publicly available(url: https://github.com/mHealthBuet/BUET-colposcopy)

</details>


### [122] [Balancing Emotional Alignment and Semantic Consistency in Image Generation via Reinforcement Learning with Valence-Arousal Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12830)
*Jisheng Dang,Zhenxuan Wang,Bin Li,Ronghao Lin,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出anchor-regularized Flow-GRPO框架，将连续VA条件、GRPO与中性语义锚结合，通过marginal-preserving SDE实现轨迹采样与策略比率估计，在情感对齐与语义一致性间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图生成中的连续情感控制要求在不改变提示所描述的对象、布局或场景的前提下提升情感对齐。现有监督式情感注入方法多优化特征空间代理目标，易出现"情感-语义漂移"，即情感条件增强时伴随非预期内容变化。

Method: 采用flow-matching图像生成框架，组合连续valence-arousal（VA）条件、Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）与中性语义锚。将确定性概率流ODE转换为保边缘分布的SDE，得到非退化的转移密度以支持轨迹采样与策略比率估计。以冻结的CLIP-based VA回归器提供终端奖励（预测VA与目标VA距离），并以同一提示在零VA条件下生成的图像作为特征空间语义保持参考。在线RL采样使用缩减的去噪调度，推理时保留原始调度。

Result: 在3,300组提示-情感组合上，valence与arousal误差显著低于VA条件基线，CLIPScore相对EmotiCrafter有所提升，但无参考图像质量存在可测量的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，anchor-regularized Flow-GRPO是连续情感图像合成中平衡情感对齐与语义一致性的可行方案。

Abstract: Continuous emotion control in text-to-image generation requires a model to improve affective alignment without changing the objects, layout, or scene described by the prompt. Existing supervised emotion-injection methods often optimize feature-space proxies and may therefore exhibit emotion-semantic drift, in which stronger emotional conditioning is accompanied by unintended content changes. We address this problem with a flow-matching image-generation framework that combines continuous valence-arousal (VA) conditioning, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), and a neutral semantic anchor. The deterministic probability-flow ODE is converted into a marginal-preserving SDE, yielding non-degenerate transition densities for trajectory sampling and policy-ratio estimation. A frozen CLIP-based VA regressor supplies a terminal reward measuring the distance between the predicted and target VA coordinates, while an image generated from the same prompt under zero VA conditioning provides a feature-space reference for semantic preservation. A reduced denoising schedule is used for online RL sampling, whereas the original schedule is retained at inference. Experiments on 3,300 prompt-emotion combinations show substantially lower valence and arousal errors than the VA-conditioned baseline and an improved CLIPScore relative to EmotiCrafter, with a measurable trade-off in reference-free image quality. The results support anchor-regularized Flow-GRPO as a practical approach to balancing emotional alignment and semantic consistency in continuous-affect image synthesis.

</details>


### [123] [Self-supervised Pre-training Helps Retinal Disease Progression Modelling Most When Data Is Scarce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12834)
*Ifeoma Veronica Nwabufo,Julius Gervelmeyer,Sarah Müller,Philipp Berens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨在纵向数据稀缺时如何利用大规模横断面数据进行自监督预训练以建模疾病进展，以AMD为例比较多种预训练策略并给出实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病进展建模需要纵向影像队列，但此类数据稀缺且规模小，而横断面数据（每人一张图像）丰富。自监督预训练可弥合这一差距，但哪种预训练策略最有利于进展建模、其效果如何依赖有标签纵向数据量，尚不清楚。

Method: 以年龄相关性黄斑变性（AMD）为研究对象，在大型横断面NAKO队列上预训练编码器，并在纵向AREDS数据集上预测进展为晚期AMD的时间。比较自研自监督编码器与通用基础模型（DINOv2）和领域专用基础模型（RETFound），涵盖对比学习、掩码自编码和自蒸馏目标，在冻结与微调两种协议下，以及从100到32,250例不等的有标签训练集上进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型取决于编码器的使用方式。当编码器冻结且标签较少（纵向队列的典型情形）时，预训练表示仅用数百个有标签样本即可达到临床上合理的判别性能，而从零训练的模型无法做到；这一优势在微调下减弱。迁移效果由自监督目标决定，而非语料规模或领域匹配度，因此在适度规模横断面队列上预训练的编码器可匹敌甚至超越更大规模的同领域基础模型。

Conclusion: 为纵向数据稀缺场景下的进展建模提供了实用方案：采用冻结的自监督编码器搭配轻量级生存分析头。

Abstract: Modelling how a disease progresses over time requires longitudinal imaging cohorts, which are scarce and small, whereas cross-sectional data -- one image per participant -- is abundant. Self-supervised pre-training on such data offers a way to bridge this gap, but it is unclear which strategy best supports progression modelling, or how that answer depends on the amount of labelled longitudinal data. We study this for age-related macular degeneration (AMD), pre-training encoders on the large cross-sectional NAKO cohort and predicting time to late AMD on the longitudinal AREDS dataset. We compare in-house self-supervised encoders against a general-purpose (DINOv2) and a domain-specific (RETFound) foundation model, across contrastive, masked-autoencoding, and self-distillation objectives, under frozen and fine-tuned protocols, and across labelled training sets from 100 to 32,250 examples. Which model performs best depends on how the encoder is used. When the encoder is frozen and labels are few -- the regime typical of longitudinal cohorts -- pre-trained representations reach clinically reasonable discrimination from a few hundred labelled samples, while models trained from scratch do not; this advantage fades under fine-tuning. Transfer is governed by the self-supervision objective rather than corpus scale or domain match, so that an encoder pre-trained on a modest cross-sectional cohort matches or exceeds a far larger in-domain foundation model. Together, these results offer a practical recipe for building progression models where longitudinal data is scarce: a frozen self-supervised encoder with a lightweight survival head.

</details>


### [124] [HemaHier: Chain-Conditioned Ordinal Hierarchies for Lineage-Aware Bone-Marrow Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12835)
*Afshin Bozorgpour,Peter Schüffler,Edgar Jost,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出HemaHier，一种面向骨髓细胞学的有序-层次预测头，通过在冻结或轻量适配的细胞学基础模型上引入链条件成熟度评分，实现细分类、谱系与成熟度的层次一致预测，在三个骨髓数据集上减少生物学严重错误并补充谱系内成熟度排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨髓细胞学本身具有结构化特征：每个细胞归属于特定造血谱系，且多种细胞类型位于有序的成熟轨迹上。然而标准扁平分类器忽略这种结构，将同谱系内的轻微混淆与跨谱系的严重错误等同对待，并且只能输出离散标签，无法刻画成熟度。

Method: 提出HemaHier，一个用于冻结或轻量适配的细胞学基础模型的有序-层次预测头。核心是链条件成熟度评分：在每条链的查询下读取单一成熟度数值，仅用生物学上有效的健康链进行监督，而发育异常与链外细胞类型仍作为类别但排除在成熟度监督之外。细分类与谱系预测通过共享后验耦合，保证层次一致性；采用分阶段目标，先稳定识别，再逐步加入谱系与成熟度监督。

Result: 在共享本体下的三个骨髓数据集上，HemaHier取得了有竞争力的识别性能，同时减少了生物学上严重的错误，并新增了扁平分类器所不具备的谱系内成熟度排序能力。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了将造血谱系层次与成熟度有序结构显式建模到预测头中的有效性，在保持识别精度的同时提升了错误的生物学合理性，并扩展了输出的结构化信息。代码已开源。

Abstract: Bone-marrow cytology is inherently structured: each cell belongs to a hematopoietic lineage, and many cell types lie on ordered maturation trajectories. Standard flat classifiers ignore this structure, treating a mild same-lineage confusion the same as a severe cross-lineage mistake and predicting only discrete labels. We propose HemaHier, an ordinal-hierarchical prediction head for a frozen or lightly adapted cytology foundation model. Its central component is a chain-conditioned maturity score that reads a single maturity value under a per-chain query, supervised only on biologically valid healthy chains, while dysplastic and off-chain cell types remain classes but are excluded from maturity supervision. Fine and lineage predictions are coupled through a shared posterior that guarantees hierarchical consistency, and a staged objective first stabilizes recognition, then adds lineage and maturity supervision. On three bone-marrow datasets under a shared ontology, HemaHier achieves competitive recognition while reducing biologically severe errors and adding a within-lineage maturity ordering that flat classifiers lack. Code is available at https://github.com/xmindflow/HemaHier.

</details>


### [125] [Pre-Trained Low-Rank Tensor Decomposition for Multi-Dimensional Image Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12843)
*Bing-Zhang Fu,Zhi-Long Han,Ting-Zhu Huang,Xi-Le Zhao,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个预训练低秩张量分解（PLTD）框架，将预训练大视觉模型（DINOv3）与经典张量分解结合，通过"固定预训练潜张量+可学习低秩潜张量"的互补结构，在多维图像恢复任务上同时实现更高恢复保真度、更少可学习参数和更小碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量分解方法从零学习每幅图像的实例特定结构，忽略了不同图像间的共性结构，导致语义建模能力受限、计算开销高、可学习参数多。作者希望在保持张量分解框架优势的同时，引入预训练大模型的先验来弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出PLTD框架：将目标张量分解为潜张量与一个可学习变换（映射潜张量回原始数据域）。潜张量由两部分组成——从预训练大视觉模型DINOv3中蒸馏得到的固定预训练潜张量（刻画共性结构）与可学习的低秩潜张量（刻画实例特定结构）。基于该框架构建了多维图像恢复模型，并给出理论分析，同时讨论了PLTD与经典张量分解框架的联系。

Result: 在多维图像恢复任务上的大量实验表明，PLTD相较当前最优方法（SOTA）持续取得更优性能；理论上也论证了该框架在恢复保真度、参数效率与计算/碳开销之间的平衡优势。

Conclusion: PLTD首次将预训练大视觉模型融入经典张量分解，突破了浅层、未训练深度张量分解的局限，实现了恢复保真度、可学习参数数量与碳足迹三者的空前平衡，为多维图像表示与恢复提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recently, tensor decompositions are prevalent for multi-dimensional image representation, which learn the instance-specific structure of each image from scratch. However, tensor decompositions neglect the common structure across different images, leading to limited semantic modeling capability, high computational cost, and a large number of learnable parameters. To address this challenge, we suggest the first pre-trained low-rank tensor decomposition (PLTD) framework, which organically integrates the pre-trained large vision model into the classical tensor decomposition framework. Beyond the shallow and untrained deep tensor decomposition, the suggested PLTD achieves an unprecedented balance among higher recovery fidelity, fewer learnable parameters, and smaller carbon footprint. Specifically, PLTD factorizes the target tensor into a latent tensor and a learnable transform that maps the latent tensor back to the original data domain. The latent tensor consists of two indispensable and complementary terms, i.e., a fixed pre-trained latent tensor and a learnable low-rank latent tensor. The fixed pre-trained latent tensor is distilled from a pre-trained large vision model (i.e., DINOv3) to capture the common structure of the target tensor, while the learnable low-rank latent tensor characterizes the instance-specific structure of the target tensor. To examine the potential of PLTD, we develop the corresponding multi-dimensional image recovery model and theoretically justify the advantages of this framework. Additionally, we discuss the connections between PLTD and classical tensor decomposition frameworks. Extensive experiments on multi-dimensional image recovery demonstrate that PLTD consistently achieves superior performance compared with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [126] [MGAvatar: Mesh-Bound Gaussians for Head Avatar Geometry and Appearance Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12850)
*Lei Shi,Sen Peng,Zhiyang Deng,Zhonggui Chen,Xiaohu Guo,Baorong Yang,Xiao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MGAvatar，一种高斯-网格混合表示，通过两种高斯-网格绑定模式联合建模几何与外观，实现高保真头部头像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯（Gaussian）的头部头像依赖参数化模板（如FLAME）进行高斯初始化与变形，但模板缺乏个性化先验，难以表示头发、衣物等结构。

Method: 采用高斯-网格混合表示，包含两种绑定模式：先引入顶点绑定高斯并约束其可学习参数，通过渐进式网格变形表示复杂头部几何，同时用姿态相关偏移模块处理非刚性变形；几何稳定后切换为面绑定高斯进行外观建模。此外引入视角条件神经颜色场以提升新视角/新姿态下外观一致性，并设计高斯偏移网络在观测空间预测偏移图，增强对面部动态纹理的建模能力。

Result: 在多视角和单目视频上进行大量实验，表明MGAvatar在渲染质量上优于现有方法，能生成具有丰富纹理细节的高保真头部头像。

Conclusion: MGAvatar通过高斯-网格混合表示与分阶段绑定策略，有效解决了参数化模板的个性化与结构表示不足问题，提升了头部头像的渲染质量与外观一致性。

Abstract: Accurate head modeling requires a stable yet expressive geometric representation. Existing Gaussian-based head avatars commonly rely on parametric templates (e.g., FLAME) for Gaussian initialization and deformation, but these templates lack personalized priors and struggle to represent structures such as hair and clothing. To address this issue, we propose MGAvatar, a Gaussian-mesh hybrid representation that jointly models geometry and appearance through two Gaussian-mesh binding modes. Specifically, we introduce vertex-bound Gaussians and constrain their learnable parameters, enabling progressive mesh deformation to represent complex head geometry, while a pose-dependent offset module accounts for non-rigid deformations. Once geometry is stabilized, MGAvatar switches to face-bound Gaussians for appearance modeling. To improve appearance consistency across novel poses and viewpoints, we introduce a view-conditioned neural color field that alleviates artifacts caused by independently optimized Gaussian colors. In addition, we design a Gaussian offset network to predict Gaussian offset maps in the observation space, providing greater flexibility for face-bound Gaussians to capture dynamic facial textures. Extensive experiments on multi-view and monocular videos show that MGAvatar outperforms existing methods in rendering quality, producing high-fidelity head avatars with rich texture details.

</details>


### [127] [3D CT-to-PET Translation via Latent Brownian Bridge Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12860)
*Sarita Mourya,Francesco Di Feola,Pierangelo Veltri,Paolo Soda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于隐空间布朗桥扩散（BBDM）的3D CT到PET合成框架，两阶段：先用对比学习增强的VAE对齐CT-PET隐表征，再在隐空间用BBDM完成跨模态转换，重建3D PET体积，在公开数据集上优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET在肿瘤诊断与治疗计划中提供功能代谢信息，与CT的解剖信息互补，但其广泛应用受限于辐射剂量高、成本昂贵和可及性差。因此希望直接从CT合成PET样信息，但CT与PET之间存在巨大的跨模态差异，精确建模困难。

Method: 提出基于隐空间布朗桥扩散（BBDM）的3D CT-to-PET转换框架，分两阶段：第一阶段在配对的CT-PET图像块上训练变分自编码器（VAE），并引入对比学习以增强解剖表征与代谢表征之间的隐空间对齐；第二阶段在隐空间中训练BBDM，将CT隐表征转换为对应的PET隐表征；随后解码并拼接转换后的PET隐表征以重建最终3D PET体积。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上进行评估，基于图像保真度和病灶级PET专用指标的定量结果表明，该方法性能优于现有竞争方法，具体表现为PET信号保真度提升、临床相关摄取值分布保留更好，并在小病灶代谢活化的保持上表现更优。

Conclusion: 该隐空间BBDM框架有效缓解CT-to-PET跨模态转换难题，在信号保真与小病灶代谢信息保持方面具优势，为虚拟成像应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) and positron emission tomography (PET) provide complementary anatomical and functional information for cancer diagnosis and treatment planning. However, the widespread use of PET is limited by high radiation exposure, elevated costs, and restricted availability. To address these limitations, deep learning-based CT-to-PET translation has emerged as a promising approach for synthesizing PET-like information directly from CT images, although accurately modeling the large cross-modal gap remains challenging. In this work, we propose a 3D CT-to-PET translation framework based on latent Brownian Bridge Diffusion (BBDM). The method consists of two stages. First, a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) is trained on paired CT-PET patches, integrating contrastive learning to improve latent alignment between anatomical and metabolic representations. Second, a BBDM is trained in the latent space to translate CT latent representations into their corresponding PET counterparts. The translated PET latents are then decoded and stitched to reconstruct the final 3D PET volume. We evaluate the proposed approach on two publicly available datasets. Quantitative results based on image fidelity and lesion-level PET-specific metrics demonstrate improved performance compared with competing methods. In particular, the proposed approach improves PET signal fidelity, better preserves clinically relevant uptake patterns, and shows improved performance in preserving small-lesion metabolic activation, paving the way for virtual imaging applications.

</details>


### [128] [VideoTok4D: A 4D-Aware Video Tokenizer for Compact World Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12874)
*Xinyi Chen,Hanxin Zhu,Xijun Wang,Xingrui Wang,Sen Liang,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoTok4D，一种4D感知视频tokenizer，通过时空解耦、轨迹感知动态注意力与扩散先验Co4DGen，实现紧凑的4D世界表征与高效生成，存储需求比稠密4D表征降低最多4个数量级并达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频tokenizer局限于2D视觉范式，将视频视为图像序列而非动态3D世界的观测，导致学习到的token继承"观测中心偏差"，难以紧凑表征真实4D场景。

Method: 三大设计：(1) 时空解耦策略，将视频分解为静态与动态token以建模整体世界；(2) 轨迹感知动态注意力机制，聚合轨迹对齐线索以提升跨视角运动一致性；(3) Co4DGen，在VideoTok4D token空间上学习扩散先验，用于高效4D场景生成。

Result: 实验表明方法达SOTA性能，同时存储需求比稠密4D表征最多降低4个数量级；紧凑token空间显著缩短扩散序列，实现高效生成。

Conclusion: 将视频tokenization从2D观测中心范式推进到4D感知表征，可在极低存储代价下紧凑建模动态3D世界，并为高效4D生成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Video tokenizers have emerged as a cornerstone of modern video modeling, underpinning progress in compression, reconstruction and generation by mapping high-dimensional visual signals into compact latent spaces. However, despite this progress, current tokenization paradigms largely remain within the 2D visual domain, treating videos as image sequences rather than observations of an underlying dynamic 3D world. Consequently, the learned tokens inherit this observation-centric bias, limiting their capacity to compactly represent real-world 4D scenes. To mitigate this issue, we propose VideoTok4D, a novel 4D-aware video tokenizer for compact world representation. Specifically, our approach comprises three key designs: 1) a spatiotemporal disentanglement strategy that factorizes videos into static and dynamic tokens for holistic world modeling; 2) a track-aware dynamic attention mechanism that aggregates trajectory-aligned cues to promote cross-view motion consistency; and 3) Co4DGen, a diffusion prior learned over the resulting VideoTok4D token space for efficient 4D scene generation. Extensive experiments have demonstrated that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while requiring up to 4 orders of magnitude less storage than dense 4D representations. Moreover, the compact token space substantially shortens diffusion sequences, enabling efficient generation.

</details>


### [129] [Learning Sign Language Recognition under Label Noise: A Study of Noise-Robust Losses for Isolated and Continuous Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12885)
*Akihisa Shitara,Yoichi Ochiai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将图像分类中的鲁棒损失（SCE、GCE）应用于手语识别中单标签分类器（ISLR分类损失与CSLR的帧级伪标签辅助分类器），而非CTC。在ASL Citizen注入对称噪声下，鲁棒损失在干净标签上代价≤2.5 pt，在噪声率0.2时六个条件均优于CE 2.9-10.0 pt，但方差显著更大；CSLR（PHOENIX-2014）上无增益，且改进/退化无法与损失权重效应分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISLR的孤立分类损失将单一标签视为真值，连续手语识别（CSLR）方法所添加的帧级伪标签辅助分类器同样如此。风格差异使ISLR标注模糊，CSLR缺乏时间边界而被迫使用伪标签，二者均引入噪声标签。作者希望借鉴图像分类中的鲁棒损失（SCE、GCE）来缓解单标签分类器所受的标签噪声影响，并检验其在手语识别中的有效性与稳定性。

Method: 将对称交叉熵（SCE）与广义交叉熵（GCE）——图像分类中替代交叉熵（CE）的鲁棒损失——应用于CTC之前的单标签分类器，而非CTC本身。实验在ASL Citizen（注入对称噪声，三个骨干网络，ST-GCN用三个随机种子）上评估ISLR；在PHOENIX-2014上评估CSLR的帧级伪标签辅助分类器；并在VAC、CorrNet、SlowFastSign上分析lambda_aux=25下伪标签CE辅助对词错误率的影响及损失的有效梯度差异。

Result: ISLR：干净标签下鲁棒损失代价至多2.5 pt；噪声率0.2时六种条件均优于CE 2.9-10.0 pt（其中一个仅在dev上重新选择q后成立）。GCE增益更大，但其最优q无法跨骨干迁移，而SCE的单一设置适用于全部九个条件；两者的运行间波动比CE大2-11倍，故有利的点估计不足以证明稳定性。CSLR（PHOENIX-2014）无增益，帧级目标带有系统性分配偏差，该研究仅为单一配置的诊断。CSLR辅助：lambda_aux=25时伪标签CE辅助在VAC、CorrNet、SlowFastSign上使WER高于无辅助基线，GCE/SCE比CE提升1.7-3.2 pt（六个条件中三个低于该基线）；但同一lambda_aux下三种损失的有效梯度相差一个数量级以上，匹配初始梯度后差距缩至0.4-0.9 pt，且仅降低CE权重即可超过基线，故退化与改进均无法与权重效应分离。

Conclusion: 鲁棒损失在ISLR噪声条件下可带来一定提升且SCE的超参更易迁移，但方差更大、稳定性不足；GCE虽增益更大但超参不可迁移。CSLR上无增益，帧级伪标签存在系统性分配偏差。CSLR辅助分类器的效果无法与损失权重效应区分，因此报告时应控制有效梯度/权重。研究局限：仅使用对称噪声，多种子评估仅覆盖ST-GCN与VAC的孤立设置。

Abstract: In sign language recognition, the isolated (ISLR) classification loss treats a single label as ground truth, as does the frame-level auxiliary classifier over pseudo-labels we add to continuous (CSLR) methods, which lack one. Stylistic variation blurs ISLR annotation and the lack of temporal boundaries in CSLR forces pseudo-labels; both are noisy. We therefore apply symmetric and generalized cross entropy (SCE, GCE), robust alternatives to cross entropy (CE) from image classification, not to connectionist temporal classification but to the preceding single-label classifier. On ASL Citizen with injected symmetric noise on three backbones (three seeds for ST-GCN), robust losses cost at most 2.5 pt when labels are clean and beat CE by 2.9-10.0 pt in all six conditions at noise rate 0.2, one of which only after q was re-selected on dev. GCE gains more, but its optimal q does not transfer across backbones, whereas one SCE setting works in all nine conditions; both vary 2-11 times more than CE across runs, so a favorable point estimate does not establish stability. For CSLR (PHOENIX-2014) we report no gain; our frame-level targets carry a systematic assignment bias, making that study a diagnosis of a single configuration. At lambda_aux = 25 the pseudo-label CE auxiliary raises word error rate above the no-auxiliary baseline on VAC, CorrNet and SlowFastSign, and GCE/SCE improve on CE by 1.7-3.2 pt (three of six conditions return below that baseline). However, the three losses differ by more than an order of magnitude in effective gradient at a common lambda_aux: matching the initial gradient shrinks the gap to 0.4-0.9 pt, and lowering the CE weight alone already beats that baseline, so neither the degradation nor the improvement can be separated from the effect of the weight. We use only symmetric noise; multi-seed evaluation covers only ST-GCN and VAC isolated.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Accuracy: Uncertainty-Guided Boundary Refinement for Reliable Biomedical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12892)
*Anima Kujur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可靠性感知边界精化网络（RABR-Net），两阶段框架：UNet++ EfficientNet-B4基分割器产生初始概率与logits，融合预测熵、TTA方差、margin不确定性、概率梯度与软边界线索构建边界感知可靠性表示，驱动门控残差精化器选择性修正不确定边界像素；在血液涂片显微图像上Dice由0.9602提升至0.9614，Boundary Dice由0.3448提升至0.3611，HD95由3.0354降至2.8274，统计检验显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分割不仅需要高全局重叠度，还需可靠描绘具有临床意义的边界。血液涂片显微图像中，细胞质与细胞核轮廓是下游形态学分析的结构基础；但深度分割模型即便Dice很高，在模糊边界区域仍可能不确定或过度自信，缺乏可信度与可解释性。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段：UNet++ EfficientNet-B4强基分割器输出初始类别概率与logits。第二阶段：将预测熵、测试时增强（TTA）方差、margin不确定性、概率梯度与软边界线索组合为边界感知可靠性表示，用以引导门控残差精化器，仅选择性修正不确定的边界像素，同时保留基预测中置信区域。评估涵盖重叠精度、类别Dice、Boundary Dice、HD95/ASSD、校准、风险-覆盖率分析、图像扰动鲁棒性、定性修正图与配对统计检验。

Result: 在留出测试集上，相较缓存基预测，Dice由0.9602提升至0.9614，Boundary Dice由0.3448提升至0.3611，HD95由3.0354降至2.8274；统计检验确认Dice、Boundary Dice与HD95改善显著。定性结果显示学习到的门控集中于不确定的细胞质与细胞核边界，修正图确认边界精化具有局部性。但精化后校准未自动改善。

Conclusion: RABR-Net为边界敏感的生物医学图像分割提供了一种可解释、以可靠性为中心的策略，能在保持置信区域的同时有效精化不确定边界，显著提升边界相关指标；局限在于校准性能未随之提升，需后续研究。

Abstract: Accurate biomedical image segmentation requires not only high global overlap but also reliable delineation of clinically meaningful boundaries. In blood-smear microscopy, cytoplasm and nucleus contours provide the structural basis for downstream morphology analysis; however, deep segmentation models may remain uncertain or overconfident near ambiguous boundary regions even when achieving strong Dice scores. This work proposes a Reliability-Aware Boundary Refinement Network (RABR-Net), a two-stage framework for trustworthy image segmentation. A strong UNet++ EfficientNet-B4 base segmenter first produces initial class probabilities and logits. Predictive entropy, test-time augmentation variance, margin uncertainty, probability gradients, and soft boundary cues are then combined into a boundary-aware reliability representation. This representation guides a gated residual refiner that selectively corrects uncertain boundary pixels while preserving confident regions of the base prediction. The framework is evaluated using overlap accuracy, class-wise Dice, Boundary Dice, HD95/ASSD, calibration, risk--coverage analysis, robustness under image perturbations, qualitative correction maps, and paired statistical testing. On the held-out test set, the proposed method improves Dice from 0.9602 to 0.9614, Boundary Dice from 0.3448 to 0.3611, and HD95 from 3.0354 to 2.8274 compared with the cached base prediction. Statistical analysis confirms significant improvements in Dice, Boundary Dice, and HD95. Qualitative results show that the learned gate concentrates around uncertain cytoplasm and nucleus boundaries, and correction maps confirm localized boundary refinement. Although calibration does not automatically improve after refinement, the proposed framework provides an interpretable and reliability-focused strategy for boundary-sensitive biomedical image segmentation.

</details>


### [131] [UniPart: Towards Zero-shot Language-Grounded 3D Part Segmentation for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12898)
*Xinqiang Yu,Zekun qi,Jiawei He,Wenyao Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Guaocai Yao,Li Yi,Zhaoxiang Zhang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniPart，一个文本条件的前馈跨模态 3D Transformer，用于开放词表的细粒度点云部件分割，并构建 LangPart-1M/4K 数据集，实现零样本分割与真实世界语言引导抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度机器人操作需要理解物体部件而非整体。现有 3D 基础模型要么泛化但仅面向整体物体，要么面向部件却局限于封闭类别体系，导致零样本迁移能力受限。

Method: 研究文本条件 3D 部件分割：以自由形式短语在点云上选择功能性部件。提出 UniPart——前馈跨模态 3D Transformer，以 CLIP 文本嵌入作为条件。为扩展监督，利用多视图一致的部件生成构建 LangPart-1M（16 万+ Objaverse 资产、800 万文本-部件对），并人工标注高质量子集 LangPart-4K 用于微调与评测。

Result: UniPart 在开放词表部件基准上取得较强零样本性能，并迁移到真实世界的语言条件部件抓取任务。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态文本条件 Transformer 与大规模语言-部件监督数据，可实现开放词表的细粒度 3D 部件分割，并支撑下游语言引导的机器人操作。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic manipulation depends on understanding parts, not only whole objects. Existing 3D foundation models tend to be either generalized but object-aware, or part-aware but limited to closed-set taxonomies, which weakens zero-shot transfer. We study text-conditioned 3D part segmentation, where a free-form phrase selects a functional part on point cloud. We introduce UniPart, a feed-forward cross-modal 3D Transformer that conditions CLIP text embedding. To scale supervision, we build LangPart-1M with 160K+ Objaverse assets and 8M text to part pairs using multi-view consistent part generation. We further manually label a high-quality subset, LangPart-4K, for fine-tuning and evaluation. UniPart achieves strong zero-shot results on open-vocabulary part benchmarks and transfers to language-conditioned part grasping in real world.

</details>


### [132] [Parallel Training Using a CNN-DNN Architecture for Accelerated Development of Diagnostic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12902)
*Janine Weber-Hamacher,Astha Jaiswal,Philipp Fervers,Dorotya Móré,Athanasios Giannakis,Ricarda Fischbach,Andreas Michael Bucher,Rahil Shahzad,Jonathan Kottlors,Thorsten Persigehl,Axel Klawonn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图像分解与定位的混合CNN-DNN网络，可并行高效训练深度学习模型，用于COVID-19影像诊断，在提升效率的同时保持较高诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模深度模型训练需应对超大数据集及动态增长的疾病数据（如疫情期间），传统训练方式效率低，因此亟需支持并行、高效训练的模型框架以加快模型开发与疫情应对准备。

Method: 回顾性收集德国三个中心的300例COVID-19与非COVID-19肺炎患者CT扫描；构建基于图像分解与定位的混合CNN-DNN网络，天然支持并行高效训练；共训练156个含三种不同架构的模型以捕获不同层级特征，最终形成12个患者级COVID-19诊断模型；测量诊断性能与时间节省情况。

Result: DenseNet121与3D CNN结合并行CNN-DNN方法取得最高精度：DenseNet121在4×4×1子域下训练88.78%、验证76.67%、测试76.03%；3D CNN在4×4×1子域下训练87.72%、验证76.82%、测试74.86%。3D CNN在4×4×2子域下并行训练时间缩短最多，达31倍。

Conclusion: 并行训练方法同时提升了训练效率与模型性能，可实现快速模型开发，对大流行应对准备等场景具有重要意义。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has shown promise in assisting radiologists in imaging-based diagnosis across a wide range of diseases. Efficient training of large deep learning models is essential to cope with extremely large data sets or dynamically growing disease data, like in a pandemic like situation. In this retrospective study, we collected 300 CT scans from COVID-19 and non-COVID-19 pneumonia patients from three different centers in Germany. We investigated a hybrid CNN-DNN network model based on image decomposition and localization that naturally supports parallel and efficient training of deep learning models. In total, 156 models with three different architectures were trained to capture features at different levels resulting in 12 patient-level COVID-19 diagnosis models. Diagnostic performance as well as time saving were measured.
  The highest accuracy was obtained from DenseNet121 and 3D CNN models with a parallel CNN-DNN approach, resulting in $88.78\%$ training, $76.67\%$ validation and $76.03\%$ test accuracy for the DenseNet121 with $4\times4\times1$ subdomains and $87.72\%$ training, $76.82\%$ validation and $74.86\%$ test accuracy, respectively, for the 3D CNN with $4\times4\times1$ subdomains. The strongest reduction in parallel training time by a factor of $31$ was observed for the 3D CNN model and $4\times4\times2$ subdomains.
  Our parallel training approach improves efficiency as well as performance enabling rapid model development, among others crucial for pandemic preparedness.

</details>


### [133] [PA-CDM: Position-Aware Character Detection Matching for Evaluating Handwritten Mathematical Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12917)
*Shiliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出位置感知指标PA-CDM、受控扰动基准StructPerturb v2.0与跨指标一致性协议，在六人标注研究中以Spearman rho=0.9535成为七种自动指标中与人类判断最相关者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写数学表达式识别（HMER）评价指标存在互补盲点：精确匹配与字符串相似度指标对错误发生位置不敏感（下标错位与分数操作数互换的得分与单纯字符错误相同）；基于渲染的字符检测匹配（CDM）虽能稳健对齐字形但位置盲（受控分数操作数互换场景仅得0.8595，而位置感知评分为0.6253）；树编辑指标则把解析器规范化范围之外的改写惩罚为结构错误（0.8552，而渲染类指标为1.0）。即缺乏同时兼顾字形对齐与位置/结构敏感性的评价方法。

Method: PA-CDM：将字符检测匹配与位置森林编码、分歧级加权相结合的位置感知指标；StructPerturb v2.0：包含15个类型—强度单元、1,340对受控扰动样本的冻结基准；跨指标一致性协议：由灵敏度矩阵、人类标注研究（6名标注者、n=990）与LLM评判校准组成；与七种自动指标及一个前沿LLM评判进行对比。

Result: PA-CDM在七种自动指标中与人类判断相关性最高（Spearman rho=0.9535，n=990）；前沿LLM评判相关性略高（rho=0.9613）但成本高、非确定性且依赖API。灵敏度矩阵量化了各指标在位置与结构扰动上的互补盲点（如CDM 0.8595 vs 位置感知0.6253；树编辑0.8552 vs 渲染类1.0）。

Conclusion: PA-CDM以确定性、零边际成本、可诊断的行为逼近LLM评判的评测质量，弥补现有指标的位置盲与结构盲缺陷，配合StructPerturb v2.0与一致性协议为HMER评价提供更可靠、可复现的方案。

Abstract: Handwritten mathematical expression recognition (HMER) is conventionally scored by exact-match rates and string-similarity metrics that are blind to where an error occurs: two predictions with identical token-error counts receive identical scores whether they misplace a subscript or swap the operands of a fraction. Render-based character detection matching (CDM) aligns glyphs robustly but remains position-blind---on controlled fraction-operand swaps it scores 0.8595 where position-aware scoring yields 0.6253. Tree-edit metrics exhibit a complementary blind spot: rewrites outside the parser's normalization coverage are penalized as structural errors (0.8552 where render-based metrics score 1.0). We propose PA-CDM, a position-aware metric that couples character detection matching with position-forest encoding and divergence-level weighting; StructPerturb v2.0, a frozen benchmark of 1,340 controlled perturbation pairs across 15 type--intensity cells; and a cross-metric consistency protocol combining a sensitivity matrix, a human study, and LLM-judge calibration. In a six-annotator study, PA-CDM attains the highest correlation with human judgments among seven automatic metrics (Spearman rho=0.9535, n=990). A frontier LLM judge correlates slightly higher (rho=0.9613) but is costly, nondeterministic, and API-dependent; PA-CDM approaches it at zero marginal cost with deterministic, diagnosable behavior.

</details>


### [134] [Input Resolution Matters: Real-Time Object Detection Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12920)
*Qingyang Zhang,Fumio Machida,Laura Carnevali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文将端到端目标检测总延迟建模为预处理、推理与后处理三个阶段的卷积，且将阶段参数表示为源图像分辨率的函数，并在实测流水线上评估其分布拟合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测流水线的端到端延迟受多阶段与图像分辨率影响，已有模型多采用固定参数，缺乏对分辨率依赖的延迟分布刻画。作者希望在简化独立假设下建立理论化且轻量的延迟分布建模方法。

Method: 在独立近似下，总延迟视为各阶段分布的卷积，其CDF给出端到端检测时间分布。每一阶段采用参数化分布（如指数、Erlang、正态、Gamma），参数表达为源图像分辨率的函数。实验采用COCO2017图像，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上运行YOLOv11n，覆盖多种分辨率，并用KS、AD与Cramér–von Mises统计量对比固定参数基线。

Result: 在实测设置中，分辨率感知的参数化能改善分布近似，尤其对更灵活的正态与Gamma模型效果更明显；但拟合优度仍依赖于具体分布。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种理论有据且轻量的建模框架，用于研究实测目标检测流水线中随分辨率变化的延迟分布。

Abstract: We model total latency as the convolution of preprocessing, inference, and postprocessing distributions under a simplifying independence approximation, with selected stage parameters expressed as functions of source-image resolution. Under this assumption, the probability density of the total latency is the convolution of the stage-wise densities, and its cumulative distribution function (CDF) provides the distribution of end-to-end detection time. Each stage is modeled by a parametric distribution (e.g., Exponential, Erlang, Normal, Gamma), with parameters expressed as functions of the source-image resolution. Experiments with YOLOv11n on NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using COCO2017 images across multiple resolutions assess the proposed models against fixed-parameter baselines using Kolmogorov Smirnov, Anderson Darling, and Cramér von Mises statistics. The results indicate that resolution-aware parameterization can improve distributional approximation in the measured setting, particularly for the more flexible Normal and Gamma models, while the quality of fit remains distribution dependent. Our contribution is a theoretically grounded and lightweight formulation for studying resolution-dependent latency distributions in a measured object detection pipeline.

</details>


### [135] [DementiaCare-Bench: A Modality-Validated Video Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12929)
*Afrouz Sheikholeslami,Yuankai Qi,Xuyun Zhang,Luping Zhou,Amin Beheshti,Quan Z. Sheng,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出DementiaCare-Bench，首个评估视频语言模型（VLM）理解痴呆症行为心理症状（BPSD）前因能力的基准，并揭示现有模型严重依赖语言先验、忽视时序视觉信息，同时通过LoRA微调证明该缺陷可被修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 痴呆症照护中最棘手的是BPSD（激越、游走、抗拒照护、日落综合征等），理解这些行为不仅要看行为本身，更要看其前置诱因，因为同一行为在不同触发下需不同应对。VLM或可辅助照护者，但缺乏评估该能力的基准，故需填补此空白。

Method: 构建DementiaCare-Bench：56个专业照护培训视频切成94个片段，覆盖9类BPSD；通过多智能体流水线生成2023个问题，每个临床论点均锚定逐字转写片段；每个问题在4种视觉条件下探测并按最低需求标注，从而实测而非假设其视觉需求。随后在12个现有VLM上评测，并用轻量LoRA微调得到DemCare-VLM。

Result: 测量结果与设计意图相悖：设计时77.7%的问题要求有序帧，实际仅34.8%如此。12个VLM表现一致：最佳模型总体达85%，但该均值主要由仅靠临床知识的语言模型即可回答的问题撑起；在需要有序片段的问题上准确率平均下降17个百分点，领先开源模型判断照护者回应是否恰当时仅达随机水平。LoRA微调DemCare-VLM将视频依赖从-3.3提升至+4.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 现有VLM对BPSD的理解高度依赖语言先验、未真正利用时序视觉信息。该基准不仅暴露了这一问题，且通过轻量微调证明其可被修复，表明基准揭示的缺陷是可改进的而不仅是可测量的。

Abstract: Dementia affects an estimated 57 million people worldwide, and for most families the hardest part of care is not memory loss but the behavioral and psychological symptoms of dementia (BPSD): agitation, wandering, resistance to care, sundowning. Understanding these symptoms requires more than recognizing the behavior itself; it also requires knowing what happened beforehand. The same behavior may call for a different response depending on its trigger. Video-language models (VLMs) could potentially support caregivers, yet no existing benchmark evaluates this capability. To fill this gap, we present DementiaCare-Bench: 56 professionally produced caregiver training videos segmented into 94 clips across nine BPSD categories, with 2023 questions generated by a multi-agent pipeline that grounds every clinical claim in a verbatim transcript span. Each question is then probed under four visual conditions and labelled by the least it requires, so its visual demand is measured rather than assumed. Measurement contradicts intent: we wrote 77.7% of the questions to require ordered frames, and 34.8% do. Across 12 current VLMs the pattern is uniform. The best reach 85% overall, but that average is carried by questions a language model can answer from clinical knowledge alone; accuracy falls by 17 points on average on questions that require the ordered clip, and a leading open model scores at chance on judging whether a caregiver's response was appropriate. A lightweight LoRA fine-tune, DemCare-VLM, moves video dependence from -3.3 to +4.5 points, so what the benchmark exposes can be repaired and not only measured.

</details>


### [136] [Fast and Faithful: Principled Conditional Flow Matching for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12953)
*Shirin Shoushtari,Edward P. Chandler,Xiao Shi,Ulugbek S. Kamilov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于后验均值参数化的测量条件速度场，将前向模型显式纳入学习框架，实现无需推理期引导的端到端流匹配逆问题求解，以50倍更少函数评估达到最优PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配求解成像逆问题的两种主流方式（条件化输入与推理引导）均未在学习到的条件速度场中显式施加前向模型约束，导致数据一致性处理较松散、推理开销大且需额外引导。

Method: 在线性插值下将测量条件速度 v(x_t,t,y) 表示为后验均值 E[x_1|x_t,y] 的函数，并将该均值刻画为一个具有显式数据一致性项的变分目标的唯一极小值点；证明该速度场构成从源分布到测量条件后验的概率流；对变分目标进行分解，得到含算子相关数据一致性更新的条件速度参数化，并在流匹配目标下端到端训练，推理期无需额外引导。

Result: 在PSNR上达到最优，函数评估次数比最强流匹配基线少50倍；改变采样步数可在不重训练的情况下实现失真-感知权衡的测试时控制。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码前向模型与后验均值结构，所提参数化将数据一致性内化于条件速度场，兼具理论保证（概率流性质）、推理高效性与灵活的质量权衡控制。

Abstract: Flow matching approaches to imaging inverse problems commonly incorporate measurements in two ways. Conditioning-based approaches supply measurement-derived information as a network input, often through concatenation, while inference-guided approaches combine an unconditional velocity field with a separate data-consistency update. In these common formulations, the forward model is not explicitly enforced within the learned conditional velocity field. We propose a principled parametrization of the measurement-conditional velocity field to solve inverse problems. Under linear interpolation, we express the conditional velocity $v(x_t,t,y)$ in terms of the posterior mean $E[x_1 | x_t,y]$, and characterize that mean as the unique minimizer of a variational objective whose data-consistency term is explicit. We further prove that the velocity field defines a probability flow from the source distribution to the measurement-conditioned posterior. Splitting the variational objective yields a conditional velocity parameterization with operator-dependent data-consistency updates, which we train end-to-end under the flow-matching objective, with no additional guidance at inference. Our method achieves state-of-the-art PSNR with $50\times$ fewer function evaluations than the strongest flow baseline. Varying the sampling steps provides test-time control over the distortion-perception trade-off without retraining.

</details>


### [137] [Generative Retrieval for Unsupervised Text-Based Person Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12965)
*Mang Ye,Yucheng Ji,Yang Bai,Min Cao,Siyuan Chai,Bo Du,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督文本行人检索框架GTR+，采用“先生成后检索”两阶段范式，并贡献大规模细粒度数据集LargeFine-Person。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本行人检索（TBPS）方法大多依赖人工标注的图像-文本对进行监督学习，标注成本高、扩展性受限。本文旨在仅利用无标注图像探索无监督TBPS。

Method: GTR+为两阶段框架：生成阶段采用三层级描述生成机制——基础层通过自动问答生成基本视觉属性描述，中间层借助跨样本对比增强细粒度细节，高级层通过风格化扩展提升文本多样性；检索阶段提出自适应置信度加权检索学习框架，用高斯混合模型将图文对建模为干净/噪声两类，并由实时图文相似度与生成阶段的静态文本生成概率共同校准，产生自适应样本权重。同时构建LargeFine-Person大规模TBPS数据集。

Result: 在多个TBPS基准上的实验验证了GTR+与LargeFine-Person的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: GTR+为无监督TBPS提供了可行的生成-检索方案，LargeFine-Person可作为无监督设定下的实用预训练基准，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-based person search (TBPS) aims to retrieve images of a target person from a large image gallery based on a given natural language description. Most existing methods rely on supervised learning with manually annotated image-text pairs. In this paper, we explore unsupervised TBPS, with only unlabeled images. We propose GTR+, a two-stage generation-then-retrieval framework. In the generation stage, we introduce a tiered description generation framework designed to produce fine-grained and stylistically diverse textual descriptions through a three-tier sequential process. The base tier leverages an automated question-and-answer mechanism to generate basic visual attribute descriptions; the intermediate tier enhances fine-grained detail using an inter-sample contrastive mechanism; the advanced tier further enriches textual diversity via a stylized expansion mechanism. In the retrieval stage, to mitigate the impact of noisy pseudo texts, we develop an adaptive confidence-weighted retrieval learning framework. We model image-text pairs as clean or noisy using a Gaussian Mixture Model, calibrated by real-time image-text similarity and static text generation probability from the prior stage, yielding adaptive sample weights during training. Beyond that, we also contribute LargeFine-Person, a large-scale TBPS dataset with high-quality, fine-grained, and diverse textual annotations, enabling a practical and generalizable TBPS pre-training benchmark under unsupervised setting. Experiments on multiple TBPS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of both GTR+ and LargeFine-Person. Code is available at: https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/GTR.

</details>


### [138] [Investigating Temporal Motion Features for Pose-to-Text Indian Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12993)
*Manav Dhamecha,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Pruthwik Mishra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究预训练T5模型规模与显式运动特征对姿态到文本的印度手语翻译(SLT)的影响，发现T5-small在BLEU/ROUGE最优，加入运动特征后BLEU提升最大，最终系统在WSLP 2026 SLT榜单排名第5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练T5模型规模以及显式运动(帧间姿态差分)特征对姿态到文本印度手语翻译性能的影响，并在WSLP 2026共享任务中构建有效系统。

Method: 通过轻量级姿态编码器将姿态序列投影到T5嵌入空间，并端到端微调整个模型生成英文文本；对比T5-small、T5-base、T5-large，并提出在输入表示中加入帧间姿态差分的运动增强变体(T5-small + Motion)。

Result: 在包含5,334条测试样本和5,257条验证样本的数据上，纯空间模型中T5-small的BLEU和ROUGE最佳，T5-large的chrF最高；T5-small + Motion取得研究中最大的单次提升，BLEU显著优于纯空间基线，成为该指标上最强模型；提交系统在官方WSLP 2026 SLT测试榜单排名第5。

Conclusion: 模型规模并非越大越好，T5-small在纯空间设定下表现最佳；显式运动特征带来的增益最为显著，是提升印度手语翻译BLEU的关键因素；代码与模型已在GitHub和HuggingFace公开。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of pretrained T5 model scale and explicit motion features on pose-to-text Indian Sign Language Translation (SLT) for the WSLP 2026 Shared Task. Pose sequences are projected into the embedding space of T5 through a lightweight pose encoder, with the complete model fine-tuned to generate English text. The shared task data used for this work consists of a test set with 5,334 examples and a validation set with 5,257 examples. We compare T5-small, T5-base, and T5-large, and additionally introduce a motion-augmented variant, T5-small + Motion, that adds explicit frame-to-frame pose differences to the input representation. T5-small achieves the best BLEU and ROUGE scores among the spatial-only models, while T5-large obtains the highest chrF score. Augmenting T5-small with motion features yields the largest single improvement observed in our study, substantially improving BLEU over the spatial-only baseline and making it the strongest model overall on this metric. Our submitted system ranked 5th on the official WSLP 2026 SLT testing leaderboard. The source code and trained models are publicly available on GitHub and HuggingFace.

</details>


### [139] [SV-Cine: Diagnosis-Conditioned Segmentation of Single Ventricle Physiology via Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12997)
*Lila Cunge,Yuehong Liu,Hang Xu,Thomas Coudert,Pierangelo Renella,J Paul Finn,William Hsu,Kim-Lien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向单心室生理（SVP）的心脏MRI分割框架：先用SDF4CHD生成合成3D心脏网格与对应合成MRI做数据增强，再提出诊断条件化的基础模型SV-Cine（在CineMA上通过FiLM层注入患者级诊断信息），在心室与心肌分割及射血分数估计上优于nnU-Net等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVP是罕见先天性心脏病亚型，仅具单一功能心室且解剖构型不典型，常规分割方法难以应对；临床数据稀缺且SVP各亚型形态差异大，导致鲁棒分割方法开发困难。作者希望借助基础模型的预训练解剖知识并结合诊断先验，缓解数据不足与形态多样性的问题。

Method: 1）数据增强流程：基于SDF4CHD生成合成3D心脏网格，并通过生成式建模得到相应的合成心脏MRI；2）SV-Cine模型：对基础模型CineMA进行诊断条件化改造，引入Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）层编码患者级诊断信息，实现诊断感知的特征自适应；3）在包含不同SVP亚型的内部队列上评估，并与nnU-Net等基线比较，同时考察外部队列的泛化表现。

Result: SV-Cine在左心室分割上中位Dice为0.89（IQR 0.80–0.91），右心室为0.72（IQR 0.54–0.84），右心室分割较最强基线nnU-Net提升0.39 Dice；主导心室射血分数中位误差为5.55个百分点（IQR 3.41–7.69）。相对内部队列，外部队列的左心室与心肌分割性能下降，而右心室Dice在两队列间相当。

Conclusion: 预训练基础模型可通过引入诊断先验适配到高度专业化的下游任务，同时利用大规模MRI预训练中习得的解剖知识，从而在数据稀缺、形态多样的SVP场景中实现有效分割；提示诊断条件化与合成数据增强是该类罕见病影像分析的有效策略。

Abstract: Single Ventricle Physiology (SVP) is a rare subtype of congenital heart disease characterized by the presence of a single functional cardiac ventricle with atypical anatomic configurations that challenge conventional image segmentation approaches. The scarcity of clinical data and the morphological diversity across SVP subtypes make the development of robust segmentation methods particularly difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a cardiac MRI segmentation framework focused on ventricular chambers and myocardium segmentation tailored for SVP. First, we introduce a data augmentation pipeline that generates synthetic 3D cardiac meshes using SDF4CHD and corresponding synthetic cardiac MRI through generative modeling.
