Second, we introduce SV-Cine, a diagnosis-conditioned adaptation of the foundation model CineMA that incorporates patient-level diagnostic information through Feature-wise Linear Modulation layers, enabling diagnosis-aware feature adaptation during segmentation. We evaluated the framework on an internal cohort with varying SVP subtypes. SV-Cine achieved median Dice scores of 0.89 (IQR: 0.80--0.91) for the left ventricle and 0.72 (IQR: 0.54--0.84) for the right ventricle, outperforming the strongest baseline, nnU-Net, by 0.39 Dice points on right ventricle segmentation. It also yields a median ejection fraction error of 5.55 percentage points (IQR: 3.41--7.69) for the dominant ventricle. Compared with the internal cohort, LV and myocardium segmentation performance was lower for the external cohort; whereas RV Dice scores were comparable for both cohorts. Our findings suggest that a pretrained foundation model can be adapted for highly specialized downstream tasks through usage of diagnosis priors while leveraging anatomic knowledge learned from large-scale MRI datasets during pretraining.

</details>


### [140] [Physics-Aware Video Generation via Agentic Planning and Graph-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13006)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysPlan，一种免训练引导框架，用VLM作为认知模拟器生成多模态物理先验，并通过对象中心梯度路由与动力学强度画像进行测试时优化，显著提升视频扩散模型的物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型虽能生成高保真视频，但缺乏对物理规律的内在理解，常产生结构幻觉与物理上不合理的动力学序列。现有免训练测试时优化方法依赖全局梯度更新与刚性调度启发式，会破坏被动背景且难以建模复杂动态状态变化。

Method: PhysPlan将范式从随机视觉插值转向智能体物理模拟：首先用VLM作为迭代认知模拟器，将多模态输入分解为"视觉思维链"，构建运动学轨迹与3D深度几何的多模态表示；其次以这些信号驱动对象中心的测试时优化，引入对象中心梯度路由以隔离运动学修改并完全锁定被动环境，同时用动力学强度画像动态参数化框架超参数以适应不同程度的物理形变。

Result: 在PhyGenBench与Physics-IQ基准上的大量评估表明，PhysPlan显著优于基础VDM与可控VDM基线，为提升视频生成的物理理解提供了有前景的途径。

Conclusion: 通过引入智能体式物理模拟、对象中心梯度路由与动力学强度画像，PhysPlan在免训练条件下有效改善了视频扩散模型的物理一致性，验证了认知模拟先验在测试时优化中的价值。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity, photorealistic video content. However, they fundamentally lack an intrinsic understanding of physical laws and frequently produce visually appealing but causally illogical sequences characterized by structural hallucinations and physically implausible dynamics. Injecting physical awareness via training-free test-time optimization is a promising alternative, yet existing methods rely on global gradient updates and rigid scheduling heuristics that inadvertently corrupt passive backgrounds and fail to model complex dynamic state changes. To address this, we propose PhysPlan, a novel training-free guidance framework that shifts the paradigm from stochastic visual interpolation to agentic physics simulation. First, a VLM operates as an iterative cognitive simulator, decomposing multimodal inputs into a Chain-of-Visual-Thought to create a multimodal representation of kinematic trajectories and 3D depth geometries. Second, these signals drives an object-centric test-time optimization. Unlike prior training-free methods that rely on global gradients and rigid scheduling heuristics, PhysPlan introduces Object-Centric Gradient Routing to isolate kinematic modifications and completely lock the passive environment. Furthermore, our Kinetic Intensity Profiling dynamically parameterizes framework hyperparameters to accommodate the varying severity of physical deformations. Extensive evaluations on the PhyGenBench and Physics-IQ benchmarks demonstrate that PhysPlan significantly outperforms both foundational and controllable VDM baselines, offering a promising approach for improving the physical understanding of video generation.

</details>


### [141] [Pixel Decodability Is Not a Compression Signal: Causally Evaluating Importance Proxies for Visual KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13012)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉语言模型的视觉KV缓存中保留大量可像素解码信息，但这种保留与任务因果利用脱钩；可像素解码性不是有效的KV压缩信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉KV缓存中"可重构的像素信息保留量"是否与"任务实际因果依赖"相关，并检验其能否作为KV压缩/驱逐的工程信号。

Method: 用学习到的像素反演解码器测量保留度；用单super-patch KV消融及gold-answer对数概率的teacher-forced下降测量因果使用；在图像内进行预注册、符号校准、留出设计的检验；同时对注意力、token粒度与super-patch粒度进行比较。

Result: 保留度与注意力解耦，且在充分统计力下与因果利用无关（零结果）；注意力能弱但显著地追踪因果利用（唯一有效信号，作为阳性对照）；无编码器模型保留量是编码器模型的2.7倍；super-patch粒度下可像素解码保留度的KV驱逐排名不优于随机，token粒度仅在较大预算出现弱反重要性信号，且各预算下均被注意力幅度主导。

Conclusion: 可像素解码保留度是视觉KV缓存中一条与功能轴正交的信息轴；在所测设定与各粒度下，它都不是有竞争力的KV压缩信号。

Abstract: Vision-language models retain a substantial amount of pixel-decodable visual content in their visual key-value cache. We show, in our setting, that this retention is task-inert: across our preregistered tests, how much a unit retains never positively tracks whether the computation that answers the question causally relies on it. We measure retention with a learned pixel-inversion decoder and causal use with single-super-patch KV ablation, the teacher-forced drop in gold-answer log-probability, and relate the two within images under a preregistered, sign-calibrated, held-out design. Retention is decoupled from attention and, in a well-powered null, from causal utilization. Utilization is not inert to every proxy: attention weakly but significantly tracks it, the only signal we find that does and the design's positive control. We characterize pixel-decodable retention as an informational axis of the visual KV cache, orthogonal to the functional one. How much task-inert content a cache holds differs by architecture in our model pair: the encoder-free model retains 2.7 times more than the encoder-based one. The engineering consequence is a controlled negative result. At super-patch granularity, deconfounded pixel-decodable retention ranks KV eviction no better than random; at token granularity it acquires only a weak inverse-importance signal at larger budgets, dominated at every budget by attention magnitude. In our setting, pixel-decodable reconstructability is not a competitive KV-compression signal at any granularity we test.

</details>


### [142] [TileNet: Tile-Based CNN-SVM Architecture for Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Systems Inspection of Flat Roofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13013)
*Samuel Dunthorne,Hashim A. Hashim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无人机（UAS）的实时深度学习框架，通过双高度航拍与轻量级CNN-SVM分类器实现平屋顶缺陷的机载实时检测，测试准确率达94.4%，优于GoogLeNet和AlexNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平屋顶是建筑围护结构中最关键的组成部分之一，同时影响结构性能与热效率，进而直接影响家庭能耗、碳排放与长期环境可持续性。及时检测屋顶缺陷对于减少冷热损失、防止霉菌等湿气导致的劣化、以及支撑国家气候减缓目标至关重要。然而嵌入式无人机硬件的计算与功耗约束限制了复杂模型的实时部署。

Method: 采用双高度多分辨率飞行策略，兼顾细小缺陷与大型结构问题的识别。框架结合分块（tile-based）架构与轻量级CNN-SVM分类器，以实现低延迟机载推理。最终模型包含5个卷积层和4个全连接层，最后一层为线性SVM头。数据为分块与增强后的图像，按照片级划分：43,383张训练、3,869张验证、2,540张测试。实验使用DJI Matrice 350 RTK无人机现场采集的真实影像。

Result: 模型在三组随机种子下的平均测试准确率为94.4%（95%置信区间±0.4%），优于GoogLeNet（89.2%）和AlexNet（79.8%）。

Conclusion: 实验表明该系统支持快速、可重复且安全的屋顶巡检，能够降低人工风险、减少运营成本，并推动更可持续的建筑维护。

Abstract: Flat roofs are among the most influential components of the building envelope, governing both structural performance and thermal efficiency, and thereby contributing directly to household energy consumption, carbon emissions, and long-term environmental sustainability. Timely detection of roof defects is essential for reducing heating and cooling losses, preventing moisture-driven degradation such as mold growth, and supporting national climate-change mitigation goals. This paper presents a real-time, Unmanned Aerial System (UAS)-based deep learning framework that autonomously detects defects using live imagery captured during dual-altitude aerial passes. The multi-resolution flight strategy is designed to aid the identification of both small, fine-scale defects and larger structural issues, enabling more comprehensive assessments. To meet the strict computational and power constraints of embedded UAS hardware, the proposed framework integrates a tile-based architecture with a lightweight Convolution Neural Network-Support Vector Machine (CNN-SVM) classifier designed for low-latency onboard inference. The final model-comprising five convolutional layers and four dense layers, the last a linear SVM head, achieved a mean test accuracy of $94.4\%$ ($95\%$ confidence interval $\pm0.4\%$ over three seeds) on a photo-level split ($43,383$ training, $3,869$ validation, and $2,540$ test tiled and augmented images), outperforming GoogLeNet ($89.2\%$) and AlexNet ($79.8\%$). Experimental evaluations using real UAS imagery collected by onsite visits with DJI Matrice 350 RTK drone demonstrate that the system supports rapid, repeatable, and safe roof inspections while reducing human risk, lowering operational costs, and enabling more sustainable building maintenance.

</details>


### [143] [Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation for 3D-CNNs in Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13024)
*Yanjiang Shi,Peng Zhao,Nan Qi,Guiqin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出标签引导知识蒸馏(LGKD)，通过样本级与类级蒸馏改善3D-CNN视频特征蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数视频分析中的特征蒸馏方法只是简单移植图像分析方法，忽略了视频特征在时间维度上的差异。

Method: LGKD包含两部分：样本级蒸馏利用标签信息与教师概率分布，引导对时间精度影响显著的特征学习并抑制噪声；类级蒸馏借助原型网络捕获同类样本间的关系知识，提升高维语义学习与泛化能力。

Result: 在UCF101与HMDB51两个基准动作识别数据集上进行了全面实验，取得有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: LGKD利用标签引导蒸馏学生模型特征，验证了其在视频特征蒸馏中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: As a key model compression technique, knowledge distillation aims to transfer knowledge from a high-capacity teacher model to a lightweight student model for enhancing the latter's performance. In this work, we reviewed the feature knowledge distillation for 3D-CNNs and observed that most feature distillation methods in video analysis are simple adaptations of those used in image analysis, often neglecting the differences of video features in the temporal dimension. To address this issue, we proposed Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation (LGKD) to guide the distillation of student model features using ground truth labels. Our method entails two components: sample-wise distillation and class-wise distillation, enabling the student model to learn feature representation of the teacher model at two levels. Sample-wise distillation utilizes label information and the teacher's probability distribution to guide the learning of features that significantly impact temporal accuracy while mitigating noise. Meanwhile, class-wise feature distillation employs a prototype network to further capture the relational knowledge among samples within the same category, enhancing the student's ability to learn higher-dimensional semantic information and improving model generalization. To demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method, we conducted comprehensive experiments on two benchmark action recognition datasets, UCF101 and HMDB51, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [144] [Unified CT and MRI Pancreas Segmentation for Label-Efficient Cross-Modality Subregion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13043)
*Ziliang Hong,Hongyi Pan,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Bejar,Elif Keles,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出统一3D胰腺分割框架，用域对抗学习在4604例CT/MRI上学习解剖表征，实现跨模态分割与标签高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割模型跨模态泛化能力差：不同成像模态在表观与强度分布上差异显著，单模态训练模型应用于未见域时性能大幅下降。

Method: 基于nnU-Net的共享编码器-解码器进行整体胰腺分割，引入隐空间域判别器实现CT-MRI特征对齐的域对抗学习，训练数据为4604例异构CT与MRI扫描；随后将所学编码器迁移至胰头-体-尾子区域分割，仅使用有限的MRI子区域标注。

Result: 整体胰腺分割：分布内测试集平均Dice为87.31%，各外部OOD数据集Dice为84.20%~88.09%；下游子区域分割：MRI Dice 80.53%，CT Dice 83.05%，且未使用CT子区域标注。

Conclusion: 统一的解剖表征可同时支持跨模态胰腺分割与标签高效的下游迁移，验证了域对抗学习在异构多模态数据上学习模态不变解剖特征的有效性。

Abstract: Robust medical image segmentation across imaging modalities is challenging because of large differences in appearance and intensity distributions. Models trained on a single modality often show substantial performance drops when applied to unseen domains. In this work, we develop a unified 3D pancreas segmentation framework that applies domain-adversarial learning to 4,604 heterogeneous CT and MRI scans to learn anatomical representations. A shared nnU-Net encoder-decoder is trained for whole-pancreas segmentation, with a latent domain discriminator encouraging CT-MRI feature alignment. The learned encoder is subsequently transferred to pancreatic head-body-tail segmentation using limited MRI-only subregion annotations. An average Dice score of 87.31% on the in-distribution test set and Dice scores ranging from 84.20% to 88.09% across external OOD datasets were achieved in whole pancreas segmentation. Dice scores of 80.53% on MRI and 83.05% on CT were achieved for downstream subregion segmentation, without using CT subregion annotations. These results demonstrate that a unified anatomical representation can support both cross-modality pancreas segmentation and label-efficient downstream transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [ChemMat-AgentSafetyBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Attacks and Defenses in Chemistry and Materials Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11952)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Zhihao Gao,Liang Yin,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出 \bench 基准，用于评测化学与材料智能体是否能被用户输入、工具观测或持久记忆引导至有害终点，涵盖 432 个固定有害案例、八类危害、三种场景外壳、四种工具与记忆环境，以及直接攻击基线和五种在线长程攻击。实验显示智能体在 25.6\% 的运行中输出完整有害合成/制备流程，更换攻击模型后成功率仍为 18.4\%–26.5\%，现有防御仅能将完整路径释放率降至 9.2\%–22.5\%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学与材料智能体已将文献检索、候选生成、性质预测与协议规划整合为连续发现工作流，安全问题随之从"模型是否回答有害问题"转向"智能体是否通过工具中介工作流释放有害协议"。现有评测与防御难以覆盖多入口污染、工具状态与最终产物边界，科学智能体的快速发展与化学材料领域可用的安全评测和防御之间存在日益扩大的差距。

Method: 构建 \bench 基准：包含 432 个固定有害案例规格，覆盖八类危害、三种场景外壳、四种工具与记忆环境，设有单轮直接攻击基线和五种在线长程攻击（意图劫持、工具链式调用、目标漂移、任务注入、记忆投毒）；在线攻击的具体语言由运行时演化轨迹生成，故不计入静态基准规模。主实验采用四模型配置与固定攻击者，并更换攻击模型验证风险稳健性；同时评估源自通用智能体安全的输入级与状态级防御，以及面向化学与材料的候选检查。

Result: 在四模型主实验中，智能体在 25.6\% 的运行中释放完整的危险合成或制备流程；更换攻击模型后平均成功率为 18.4\%–26.5\%，表明风险并非单一攻击者造成的假象。输入级与状态级通用防御及化学材料专用候选检查可减少部分失败，但完整路径释放率仍处于 9.2\%–22.5\% 区间。

Conclusion: 现有防御无法同时覆盖多入口污染、工具状态与最终产物边界三类风险面。科学智能体能力快速提升，而化学与材料社区可用的安全评测与防御明显滞后，二者之间存在持续扩大的差距。

Abstract: Chemistry and materials agents integrate literature retrieval, candidate generation, property prediction, and protocol planning into continuous discovery workflows. Consequently, the relevant safety question is shifting from whether a model answers a hazardous question to whether an agent releases a hazardous protocol through a tool-mediated workflow. We introduce \bench, a benchmark that evaluates whether chemistry and materials agents can be steered toward hazardous endpoints through user input, tool observations, or persistent memory. The benchmark contains 432 fixed harmful case specifications spanning eight hazard classes, three scenario shells, four tool-and-memory environments, a single-turn direct-attack baseline, and five online long-horizon attacks: intent hijacking, tool chaining, objective drifting, task injection, and memory poisoning. The concrete language of each online attack is generated from the evolving trajectory at runtime and is therefore not counted in the static benchmark size. In the four-model main experiment with a fixed attacker, agents release complete hazardous synthesis or preparation procedures in 25.6\% of runs. Replacing the attacker model yields mean success rates from 18.4\% to 26.5\%, indicating that the risk is not an artifact of a single attacker. Input- and state-level defenses adapted from general-purpose agent safety, as well as candidate checks designed for chemistry and materials, reduce some failures but still leave complete-path release rates between 9.2\% and 22.5\%. Existing defenses therefore do not simultaneously cover multi-entry contamination, tool state, and the final artifact boundary. These results highlight a widening gap between the rapid development of scientific agents and the safety evaluation and defenses available to the chemistry and materials community.

</details>


### [146] [A Survey on Quantum-Safe Cryptographic Mechanisms: Building Blocks and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11991)
*Ricardo Parizotto,Preeti Yadav,Marcus Freire,Anderson Tomkelski,Maycon Peixoto,Emmanuel Zambrini Cruzeiro,Israat Haque*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了抗量子密码机制的现状、面向迁移的现有应用与开放挑战，并按安全机制、应用领域（电信、物联网、区块链）对构建模块与迁移案例进行分类，最后总结迁移所需克服的挑战及动因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算机的出现可能使广泛使用的公钥加密与数字签名算法被高效算法破解，对现有基础设施构成威胁，因此需要研发能抵御量子计算网络安全风险的机制。然而向新安全机制的迁移仍在进行中，面临识别受威胁应用、实现敏捷可扩展迁移流程等挑战。

Method: 综述性研究：调研抗量子密码机制的最新技术现状、面向迁移的现有应用及开放挑战；梳理常用于将抗量子机制集成到现有应用的构建模块，并按其所采用的安全机制进行分类；系统回顾现有向抗量子方案的迁移工作，并按应用领域（电信、物联网、区块链）进行分类。

Result: 给出了抗量子密码机制的现状梳理，建立了构建模块按安全机制的分类体系，并按应用领域归纳了现有迁移实践，同时汇总了成功迁移所必须解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 实现向抗量子机制的迁移既面临诸多待解挑战，也存在明确动因；需要持续推进敏捷、可扩展的迁移流程以应对量子计算带来的安全风险。

Abstract: The possible emergence of fault-tolerant quantum computers may enable widely used cryptographic algorithms to be broken. These algorithms for public key encryption and digital signatures could be exposed if an efficient algorithm is employed. This poses a threat to existing infrastructure, motivating the development of mechanisms that can withstand quantum-computer cybersecurity risks. However, the transition to new security mechanisms is still underway and faces many challenges, including identifying which applications are under threat and implementing agile, scalable migration processes. In this work, we survey the state of the art in quantum-safe cryptographic mechanisms, existing applications targeting migration, and open challenges. We examine the building blocks commonly used to integrate
  quantum-safe mechanisms into existing applications and categorize them by the security mechanisms they employ. Next, we systematically review existing migrations into quantum-safe schemes and categorize them by application domain, including Telecommunications, the Internet of Things, and Blockchains. Finally, we summarize the challenges that must be addressed to enable a successful migration to quantum-resistant mechanisms, as well as the motivations for pursuing this migration.

</details>


### [147] [Scan the Skill, Govern the Action: Composing Registry Verdicts with Runtime Consequence Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12001)
*Rohit Taneja,Travis Weber*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 既有扫描器只回答"是否恶意"，无法回答"此刻该操作是否被授权"；作者在 66,192 个技能版本上测得 705 个全扫描器放行的越权技能，并提出零模型决策路径的确定性解析器与信任账本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能注册表的安全扫描器各自回答"该技能是否恶意"，但互不重叠（合并阳性仅重叠 10.4%，81.9% 仅被单一扫描器捕获）。作者认为真正未被回答的问题是授权语义：该操作在当前时点、对当前操作者是否被允许。

Method: 在 66,192 个公开技能版本上做三项测量：检测违反 CIS 2.7 与 NIST CM-11 的指令型技能并做 100 例人工审计；对实时智能体在沙箱中依文档执行的 144 条命令比对后果类别；对 53 个"通行但文档未覆盖"的技能追踪智能体的越界触达与门控拦截。设计上提出无模型参与的确定性解析器，配以按 (资源, 类别) 的信任账本，晋升阈值由操作者声明的风险容忍度导出。

Result: 705 个技能（来自 135 家发布者）被所有扫描器与注册表评审判为干净，却指示了被明令禁止的操作；检测器精确率 92%，未发现恶意意图标记，其中单一发布者贡献 506 个（按计数一并报告分布）。144 条真实命令中 34.7% 产生文档未记载的后果类别。53 个被放行技能中智能体越界 23 次，门控全部拦截。作者还发布 64 例混淆基准，其方案解出 52%。指出 10 次干净批准在 95% 置信度下无法排除 25.9% 的真实失败率。

Conclusion: 注册表安全应从未经校准的"恶意与否"转向可判定的授权问题：确定性解析加逐资源类别的信任账本，并以操作者策略而非单一假阳性率来标价摩擦，同时公开基准与全部记录以支持复现。

Abstract: Agent skill registries screen what they publish. OpenClaw's security team reported that its scanners overlap on at most 10.4% of combined positives, and 81.9% of flagged skills are caught by one scanner alone. We take that as given, and suggest the open question is not which scanner is right but which is being asked. Each answers a form of "is this skill malicious?", which is what it was built for. "Is this action permitted here, by this operator, right now?" is not one it is designed to express.
  We report three measurements over 66,192 public ClawHub skill versions. First, 705 skills across 135 distinct publishers that every scanner and the registry's judge rate clean nonetheless instruct an action prohibited by CIS Control 2.7 and NIST SP 800-53 CM-11. A hand audit of 100 puts our detector at 92% precision and found no marker of malicious intent. One publisher contributes 506 of the 705, so we report the distribution with the count. This is reproducible from public artifacts.
  Second, of 144 commands a live agent executed in a sandbox while following real skill documentation, 34.7% carried a consequence class absent from that document. Third, over 53 cleared skills documenting an action no clean record earns, the agent reached for one in 23 and the gate stopped all 23. The harness and every recorded command are released.
  We offer one design for that gap: a deterministic resolver with no model in the decision path, feeding a per-(resource, class) trust ledger whose promotion thresholds derive from the operator's stated risk tolerance. Ten clean approvals cannot exclude a true failure rate of 25.9% at 95% confidence. We price the gate's interruptions across a spectrum of operator policies rather than quote one false-positive rate, since friction is a property of the policy, not the gate. We release a 64-case obfuscation benchmark; ours resolves 52%.

</details>


### [148] [A Dark Forest First Attack Is Rational Only If the Attacker Accepts It as Its Last](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12003)
*Harvey Dam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文以"未被控制的预警或反击能力"为敌意对象、以攻击者永远无法确知完整的名册为设定，重构黑暗森林论证：先发制人只有在"清除目标的时间收益≥所有获知残余造成的暴露损失"时才理性，而未被发现的残余必然构成"全押注"损失，故先发者等于接受这是其最后一次攻击；信号（METI）让接收者获得对发送者能力的后验并证明其能预警，威胁性接收者必须"为证人付费"，因此信标可延迟敌对行动，其危害仅来自确定击杀、全押注紧迫性、无惧预警的接收者或隐藏开局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑暗森林模型把被探测文明本身当作打击对象，并在攻击者已知的名册上推演打击，因此其"克制"建议所依赖的步骤恰恰在需要它的情况下失效（名册不完整、目标并非唯一威胁）。作者试图纠正这一建模错位。

Method: 概念/博弈建模：把敌意对象重新定义为"剩余的、未被控制的预警或反击能力"，名册对攻击者永远不完整；用"时间收益（清除掉的部分）"与"暴露损失（每个获知残余）"的比较给出首次打击的理性条件；以"残余能否致死攻击者（直接打击，或警告一个能做到的人）"定义全押注失败；把信号建模为接收者对发送者能力的后验加"发送者可预警"的证明，区分敌对与非敌对接收者并分析信标对敌对行动的延迟效应。对METI的评判标准改为"触达范围"与"接收者行动所需信念"。

Result: 首次打击的理性条件是：等待会使移除目标的成本上升约等于攻击者全部押注；换言之，先发者必须接受这一击是其最后一次攻击。发送者一旦暴露，便给接收者关于其能力的后验，而非一个"靶子"，并证明其能预警；敌对接收者面对的是"必须付费的证人"，故信标可延迟任何无法几乎确定清除发送者力量的敌对者。免费信标的危害路径仅有四条：确定击杀、全押注紧迫性、接收者不惧发送者所能警告的对象、以及发送者看不见的隐藏开局。

Conclusion: 黑暗森林式先发制人缺乏普遍理性基础：由于未被发现者构成不可清除的全押注残余，首次打击本质上是"末次打击"。METI不应按"惩罚恐惧"评判，而应按触达与接收者行动门槛评判；信标的威慑机制是"见证"——发送者无需能打击任何人，只需能告诉一个能做到的人。因此信号反而可能通过增加敌对者成本而延迟攻击，免费信标的危害条件被大幅收窄。

Abstract: The Dark Forest argument holds that a civilization that detects another should strike it at once, so detection, and any transmission that invites it, is danger. Existing models make the detected civilization the object of the strike and play the strike on a roster the attacker knows; where they advise restraint, the advice rests on steps that fail in the cases it is for. This paper takes the object of hostility to be remaining uncontrolled capacity to warn or retaliate, on a roster no attacker ever knows to be complete. A first strike is rational only if the timing benefit of what it removes is at least the disclosure loss from every leftover that learns of it. A leftover able to bring about the attacker's destruction, by striking or by warning one who can, costs the attacker its whole stake, and the actors the attacker has never found are such a leftover that no strike removes. A Dark Forest believer that strikes first therefore accepts the strike as the last attack it will ever make; the strike is rational only if waiting would raise the cost of removing the target by about the attacker's whole stake. A transmission that reveals the sender gives a receiver a posterior over the sender's capacity, not a target, and proves that the sender can warn. A receiver need not be hostile; for one that is, the sender is a witness it must pay for, so a beacon delays every hostile that cannot be nearly sure of removing the sender's forces, and a free beacon harms its sender only through sure kill, whole-stake urgency, a receiver that fears no one the sender could warn, or an opening hidden from the sender. Messaging to extraterrestrial intelligence (METI) is judged by reach and by what a receiver must believe to act, not by fear of punishment, and a beacon deters by witnessing: the sender need not be able to strike anyone, only to tell someone who can.

</details>


### [149] [One Click to Leak: Characterizing the Real-World Usage and Threat Impact of MNO-based Single Sign-On Websites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12037)
*Jiasheng Huang,Mingxuan Liu,Pei Chen,Baojun Liu,Yiming Zhang,Geng Hong,Zhenrui Zhang,Hai Yang,Haixin Duan,Hui Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对基于移动运营商的单点登录（MSSO）Web生态进行大规模纵向安全研究，揭示其三阶段工作流中的信任缺陷，并提出"一键泄露"（OCL）攻击；通过层级化检测框架在一年内识别出116,852个URL、729个顶级域名，发现漏洞普遍存在，并追踪到一个已被执法部门查封的、三天内收集1.41万用户手机号的变现地下生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MSSO作为免密认证框架已从移动应用扩展到网站，但Web生态与安全风险此前缺乏系统性研究，其信任模型从传统IdP转向MNO、锚点转向SP可能引入新的攻击面。

Method: 1）分析主流MSSO部署，归纳出三阶段工作流与三类信任缺陷；2）构造OCL攻击，验证单次网页访问即可泄露敏感身份信息；3）与领先安全公司合作，设计基于被动DNS关联与搜索数据URL重构的层级化检测框架，开展为期一年的纵向测量；4）通过脚本分析识别OCL关联网站，并与合作方追踪上游平台。

Result: 一年内识别116,852个网站URL、729个顶级域名；73.6%的URL存在至少一项信任缺陷，其中69.4%暴露开发者凭证，27.1%在用户同意前签发高权限令牌；31.8%的顶级域名依赖经销商，使下游SP对MNO不可见；101个网站与OCL攻击行为强相关；追踪的上游平台已被执法部门查封，后端净化数据显示其三天内收集14,100名用户手机号，并与浏览活动等敏感信息关联。

Conclusion: 该工作系统揭示了基于Web的MSSO部署现状与安全隐患，证实存在可货币化的地下生态，并通过负责任披露帮助提升移动认证生态的安全性。

Abstract: Mobile Network Operator (MNO)-based Single Sign-On (MSSO) is a password-free authentication framework relying on mobile data sessions. Unlike traditional SSO, it shifts the Identity Provider (IdP) to the MNO and the authentication anchor to the Service Provider (SP). MSSO is increasingly deployed and has expanded from mobile apps to websites, yet its web ecosystem and security risks remain largely unexplored. We analyze mainstream MSSO deployments and identify a 3-phase workflow with three trust defects enabling trust hijacking. We further demonstrate One-Click-to-Leak (OCL) attacks, where a single webpage visit can leak sensitive identity information (e.g., phone numbers). With a leading security company, we conduct the first large-scale, longitudinal study of web-based MSSO. We design a hierarchical detection framework using passive DNS correlations and URL reconstruction from search data to identify MSSO-enabled websites. Over one year, we identified 116,852 website URLs across 729 apex domains. Of these URLs, 73.6% exhibit at least one trust defect: 69.4% expose developer credentials, and 27.1% issue high-privilege tokens before user consent, indicating widespread OCL-enabling trust defects. Among the 729 apex domains, 31.8% rely on Resellers, obscuring the downstream SP from the MNO in the analyzed flows. Script analysis identifies 101 websites strongly associated with OCL attack behavior. With our partner, we trace a representative upstream platform subsequently seized by law enforcement and uncover a monetized underground ecosystem. Sanitized backend data shows that it collected 14,100 users' phone numbers within three days and linked them to sensitive information such as browsing activity. Our work provides a comprehensive study of web-based MSSO deployment and security implications. Through responsible disclosure, our work helps secure the mobile authentication ecosystem.

</details>


### [150] [Hardware Fingerprinting FTQC via Quantum Decoder Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12145)
*Friedrich Doku,Jakub Szefer,Kaitlin N. Smith*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文首次在物理量子硬件上证明，容错量子计算中经典解码器的每轮解码耗时构成可被利用的硬件侧信道；仅凭解码时间分布即可重构探测器点火分布、估计逻辑错误率、推断码距，并以最高89%准确率指纹识别具体设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算正迈向容错量子计算（FTQC），架构规模化和主动纠错成为研究重点，但这一转变引入了尚未被充分探索的安全面。容错量子计算机中，经典解码器位于每轮综合征提取的关键路径上，其运行时间可能泄露敏感信息，而此前缺乏针对该侧信道的实证研究。

Method: 利用三台 IBM Heron 处理器在68天窗口内采集的逐次（per-shot）解码器计时数据，对解码时间分布进行统计分析（包括混合双样本 Kolmogorov-Smirnov 检验）；并基于 Google 105量子比特 Willow 处理器公开数据开展含噪声仿真，考察不同芯片位置、9个表面码 patch 的解码时间可区分性。

Result: 被动观察者仅凭解码时间分布即可：(i) 重构逐次探测器点火分布并估计工作负载的逻辑错误率 p_L；(ii) 推断所用码距；(iii) 以最高89%准确率指纹识别特定物理设备（随机猜测为33%），混合双样本 KS 检验确认解码时间分布具有统计显著性差异；在 Willow 仿真中，解码时间还能以81%准确率区分芯片上不同位置的9个表面码 patch，表明该侧信道在另一厂商、另一码族的低于阈值容错硬件上同样存在。

Conclusion: 解码器处理综合征测量与解码轮次的墙钟时间是一种全新的、可被利用的硬件侧信道，可在物理量子硬件上被动泄露逻辑错误率、码距、设备身份及芯片内位置等信息。该发现揭示了FTQC部署中此前被忽视的安全风险，提示需要在解码器设计与系统调度中纳入侧信道防护。

Abstract: As the quantum computing field transitions toward Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing (FTQC), intensive efforts are focused on scaling architectures and realizing active error correction. However, this shift introduces security surfaces that remain largely unexplored. Fault-tolerant quantum computers pair a quantum processor with a classical decoder that sits on the critical path of every syndrome-extraction round. For the first time, this work demonstrates that the wall-clock time each decoder takes to process a syndrome measurement and decoding round constitutes a novel, exploitable hardware side channel on physical quantum hardware. Using per-shot decoder timings from three IBM Heron processors collected over a 68-day window, the decode-time distribution alone allows a passive observer to (i) reconstruct the shot-by-shot detector-firing distribution and estimate the workload's logical error rate $p_L$, (ii) infer the code distance in use, and (iii) fingerprint the specific physical device with up to 89% accuracy (a random guess is 33%), with a pooled two-sample Kolmogorov-Smirnov test confirming the decode-time distributions are statistically distinct. In noisy simulation inspired by public data from Google's 105-qubit Willow processor, decoder timing further distinguishes 9 surface-code patches at different locations on the chip with 81% accuracy, showing the side channel persists on below-threshold fault-tolerant hardware from a different vendor and code family.

</details>


### [151] [Evaluating Practical Enumeration and Blocking Attacks on the Snowflake Circumvention System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12242)
*Linden Chen,Ryan Sangha,Cecylia Bocovich,Ram Sundara Raman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文实证检验了Snowflake抗IP封锁的两大安全假设：代理IP难以枚举、封锁代理会造成不可接受的附带损害。通过48天实测与大规模模拟发现，恶意客户端可枚举大量代理IP，且在自治系统层面封锁可高性价比地削弱系统连通性，但代理流失率显著制约封锁效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Snowflake等基于代理的翻墙工具依赖庞大且动态变化的第三方代理池来抵御IP封锁，其安全性建立在两个假设之上：攻击者难以枚举代理IP，以及封锁这些代理会带来不可接受的附带损害。本文旨在实证检验这两个假设是否成立。

Method: 结合有界且合乎伦理的真实世界测量与大规模仿真。真实测量持续48天（2025年5月至6月），由恶意客户端发起枚举与封锁攻击；仿真则用于评估攻击者规模扩大后的枚举与封锁能力，以及不同代理流失率的影响。

Result: 实测枚举到超过21,000个唯一代理IP，归属于近1,000个自治系统。代理流失限制了枚举的长期有效性，并降低了单个代理被封对客户端的影响。但在网络层面，封锁观测到的前1%自治系统即可阻断超过30%的Snowflake连接，同时仅影响0%的Tranco前100域名和约2.5%的前100万域名。broker的负载感知匹配会在需求高峰期（如2025年6月伊朗审查期间）提前向攻击者暴露稳定、高容量代理，进而暴露对系统连通性贡献过大的网络。仿真显示，攻击者规模增大显著提升枚举与封锁成功率，而更高的代理流失率显著降低封锁有效性。

Conclusion: Snowflake的两大安全假设在现实中并不稳固：代理可被枚举，且自治系统级封锁可造成不对称的连通性损害。代理流失是重要的天然防御因素。作者讨论并评估了若干实用缓解措施，其中部分已被集成到Snowflake中。

Abstract: Proxy-based Internet censorship circumvention tools like Snowflake rely on large, dynamic pools of third-party proxies to resist IP-based blocking. We focus on two assumptions underpinning the security of Snowflake: that adversaries cannot easily enumerate proxy IPs, and that blocking those proxies would incur unacceptable collateral damage. In this paper, we test these assumptions by studying practical enumeration and blocking attacks against Snowflake conducted by malicious clients.
  We combine bounded, ethical real-world measurements with large-scale simulation to evaluate both present-day enumeration and blocking risk and broader attacker capabilities. Over 48 days of real-world measurements from May--June 2025, our attack enumerated over 21,000 unique proxy IP addresses belonging to almost 1,000 autonomous systems. Despite this high number, we find that proxy churn limits the overall effectiveness of enumeration over time, and reduces the impact on clients of individual proxy addresses being blocked. However, at the network level, blocking the top 1% of observed autonomous systems blocks more than 30% of observed Snowflakes while affecting 0% of Tranco Top 100 domains and ~2.5% of Top 1M domains. We discover that the broker's load-aware matching reveals stable, high-capacity proxies to attackers early, especially during periods of elevated demand such as the censorship even in Iran of June 2025, subsequently exposing the networks that contribute disproportionately to system connectivity. In simulation, increasing attacker scale sharply improves both enumeration and blocking success, while higher proxy churn significantly reduces blocking effectiveness. We conclude by discussing and evaluating practical mitigations, some of which have been integrated into Snowflake.

</details>


### [152] [A First-Principles Evaluation of Graph-Based Network Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12263)
*Rui Zhao,Wajih Ul Hassan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出GIDS-Eval统一评测框架，将图入侵检测系统拆解为六个可替换阶段并统一协议，发现现有GIDS的基准指标难以反映可部署性，同时提出轻量无编码器基线GIDS-Lite。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络入侵检测系统（GIDS）虽在基准测试上报告了很强的检测指标，但这些指标对可部署性的说明非常有限。各系统沿用各自不同的预处理、窗口化和阈值化约定，导致性能无法归因到具体阶段，也难以进行受控比较。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，不继承各系统的既有约定，而是明确受控比较所需条件并统一施加。设计GIDS-Eval框架，将GIDS分解为六个可互换阶段，把这些约定转为显式实验变量。调研9个代表性GIDS，在框架内复现其中5个，在4个数据集上以统一匹配协议评测，并对9类反复出现的评测缺口逐一量化。

Result: 识别出九类评测缺口并量化其影响：两条构造边即可对存在可隐藏信息的8个"检测器-数据集"对中的3个实现完全逃逸；仅快照窗口一项就带来平均38.3%的平均精度（AP）相对波动；跨系统对齐预处理可使单个检测器的AP变动高达61.8个百分点；复现的18个检测器-数据集对均无法在事件到达时实时告警。提出的无编码器对照方法GIDS-Lite在4个数据集中的2个上AP排名第一，运行时间最高降低575倍。

Conclusion: 在统一匹配协议下，架构复杂度并非当前基准上检测质量的稳定驱动因素，但确实扩大了运维人员必须防御的运行时间、校准和攻击面。

Abstract: Graph-based network intrusion detection systems (GIDS) report strong benchmark detection metrics, but those metrics establish little about deployability. We approach the problem from first principles: rather than inheriting the preprocessing, windowing, and thresholding conventions of each published system, we ask what a controlled comparison requires and impose it uniformly. The result is GIDS-Eval, an evaluation framework that decomposes a GIDS into six interchangeable stages and turns those conventions into explicit experimental variables, so reported performance can be attributed to individual stages instead of whole pipelines. We survey nine representative GIDS, reimplement five of them within GIDS-Eval, and evaluate them on four datasets under one matched protocol. We identify nine recurring evaluation gaps and quantify the impact of each: two crafted edges achieve full evasion against three of the eight detector-dataset pairs with anything to hide; the snapshot window alone accounts for a mean 38.3% relative swing in average precision (AP); aligning preprocessing across systems moves AP by up to 61.8 percentage points for a single detector; and none of the 18 detector-dataset pairs we replay can alert as events arrive. We introduce GIDS-Lite, an encoder-free control built in the same framework, which ranks first by AP on two of the four datasets at up to 575$\times$ lower runtime. Architectural complexity is therefore not a consistent driver of detection quality under our matched protocol on current benchmarks, but it does enlarge the runtime, calibration, and attack surfaces operators must defend.

</details>


### [153] [Self-Verifying Anomaly Detection using Explainable AI for Cybersecurity of DER Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12305)
*Damilola Popoola,Souradeep Bhattacharya,Manimaran Govindarasu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExCYDER——一个面向分布式能源（DER）网络的 XAI 异常检测框架，通过 LightGBM 与 SHAP 结合的自验证机制，校验告警决策与特征归因证据的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DER 的快速普及扩大了现代电网的网络攻击面，且攻击手段日益复杂，要求异常检测系统（ADS）兼具准确性、可解释性与可靠性。现有基于机器学习的 ADS 虽检测能力强，但其黑箱特性降低了运维人员信任，并限制了安全运营中心（SOC）解读告警与响应的能力，因而需要可解释人工智能（XAI）来保障透明度与运营信心。

Method: ExCYDER 采用自验证机制对 ADS 告警进行校验以支撑可信决策：将 LightGBM 与 SHAP 相结合，检查每次模型决策是否与其特征归因证据保持一致，从而使系统确认其内部推理的连贯性与可靠性。

Result: 在真实 DNP3 数据集上的实验取得超过 98% 的检测准确率，规则—SHAP 平均一致性为 44.6%，每条告警的 SHAP 时延为 14.5 ms，置信度偏差在 5% 以内，表明验证行为稳定且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在不牺牲检测准确率的前提下区分连贯与不一致的告警，证明在基于 XAI 的 ADS 中集成验证机制可提升面向 DER 的 SOC 的可解释性、可审计性与运营稳健性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Distributed Energy Resources (DERs) has significantly expanded the cyber attack surface of modern power grids. Furthermore, increasing sophistication in attack techniques demands anomaly detection systems (ADS) that are accurate, interpretable, and reliable to support DER cybersecurity. While ML-based ADS provide strong detection capabilities, their black-box nature reduces operator trust and limits Security Operation Center's (SOC) ability to effectively interpret alerts and respond, highlighting the need for explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) to ensure transparency and operational confidence. This paper presents an XAI-based anomaly detection framework tailored for DER networks (ExCYDER). The proposed framework uses a self-verifying mechanism that validates ADS alerts to ensure trustworthy decision-making. ExCYDER combines LightGBM with SHAP to check whether each model decision aligns with its feature-attribution evidence, allowing the system to confirm that its internal reasoning is consistent and reliable. Experiments on a realistic DNP3 dataset achieved over 98% detection accuracy, an average rule--SHAP consistency of 44.6%, a SHAP latency of 14.5 ms per alert, and a confidence deviation within 5%, demonstrating stable verification behavior with minimal computational overhead. The framework distinguished between coherent and inconsistent alerts without compromising detection accuracy, demonstrating that integrated verification within XAI-based ADS enhances interpretability, auditability, and operational robustness for DER-focused SOCs.

</details>


### [154] [Function Name Is All You Need to Detect Blockchain Application Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12315)
*Rui Xi,Zehua Wang,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TxLucent框架，通过将交易调用轨迹映射为函数名序列并用Transformer学习语义，在无需源码和人工规则的情况下实现区块链应用攻击的实时检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链dApp中的业务逻辑漏洞攻击造成重大经济损失，但现有检测器依赖人工规则或难以获取的智能合约源码，脆弱且难以实用。

Method: 实证研究表明函数名序列可捕获交易高层语义。基于此，TxLucent从交易调用轨迹中提取应用语义，将调用轨迹映射为函数名序列，并用Transformer从序列中学习攻击语义特征。

Result: 在424起已知事件的14,611笔攻击交易上假阴性率为1.56%；在以太坊超5亿笔良性交易上估计假阳性率约0.0017%；平均每笔交易分析耗时24.90毫秒。

Conclusion: TxLucent不依赖硬编码模式和源码即可有效检测区块链应用攻击，具备低误报率与实时检测能力，适用于主流区块链的实际部署。

Abstract: Blockchain application attacks, targeting business logic bugs in decentralized applications (dApps), have been an increasing concern to their developers and users, causing significant financial loss. Existing attack detectors either rely on handcrafted rules for detection, or need difficult-to-obtain smart contract source code to analyze attack transactions. This makes them brittle and inapplicable in practice. In this paper, we argue that function name sequences suffice to capture the high-level semantics of a transaction, and hence can be used to detect blockchain application attacks. Our empirical study on transactions from 424 real-world attack incidents shows that 98.46% of call traces can be resolved to function names, whereas only 74.78% invoke contracts with available source code. Based on this observation, we propose TxLucent (pronounced "translucent"), an automated framework to detect blockchain application attacks by extracting application semantics from transaction call traces. TxLucent maps call traces to function name sequences and uses a transformer to learn semantics from such sequences. Consequently, TxLucent can detect attacks without relying on hand-coded patterns or source code. Our results show that TxLucent achieves a 1.56% false negative rate on 424 known incidents with 14,611 attack transactions, and an estimated 0.0017% false positive rate for benign transactions from over 500 million transactions on the Ethereum blockchain. Finally, TxLucent takes an average of 24.90 milliseconds to analyze a transaction, thus supporting real-time attack detection on popular blockchains.

</details>


### [155] [An Evidence-First Multi-LLM Framework for Auditable Critical-Infrastructure Dependency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12360)
*Nurjahan,Mst Eshita Khatun,Lamine Noureddine,Aisha Ali-Gombe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种"证据优先"的多LLM框架，从异构基础设施文档中构建基础设施知识库（IKB）与依赖图（IDG），将证据验证、本体对齐、实体消解等环节分离开来，保证过程可审计、不确定性能被逐步解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施知识分散于异构、不完整、弱结构化的证据中，依赖模型难以自动构建且难以取信。LLM虽可从这类证据抽取结构化知识，但直接由LLM生成图存在风险：无支撑的关系、术语不一致、实体身份错误、依赖端点错误。

Method: 多LLM各自独立从规范化证据中抽取候选实体与依赖，随后框架将证据验证、本体接地、实体消解、依赖对齐、验证、融合与人工复核分离为独立环节；证据支持、本体协调、端点消解、模型一致性与人工验证保持为不同状态，溯源与未决案例全程保留；验证后的IKB确定性地投影为IDG，不引入新的LLM生成知识。

Result: 在九个基础设施项目上评估：实体恢复的召回率显著高于完整有向依赖的恢复率；规范端点消解是依赖构建的主要瓶颈；依赖的跨模型重叠度远低于实体，说明模型常产生互不重叠的候选断言而非稳定的多数共识。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种可审计的"证据→IKB→IDG"流程：不确定性被保留并逐步解决，而非被压缩为单一置信度或投票决策。

Abstract: Critical-infrastructure knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous, incomplete, and weakly structured evidence, making dependency models difficult to construct automatically and difficult to trust. Large language models (LLMs) can extract structured knowledge from such evidence, but direct LLM-to-graph generation risks unsupported relationships, inconsistent terminology, incorrect entity identities, and erroneous dependency endpoints. We present an evidence-first multi-LLM framework for constructing Infrastructure Knowledge Bases (IKBs) and Infrastructure Dependency Graphs (IDGs) from heterogeneous infrastructure documentation. Multiple open-weight LLMs independently extract candidate entities and dependencies from normalized evidence, after which the framework separates evidence verification, ontology grounding, entity resolution, dependency alignment, validation, fusion, and human review. Evidence support, ontology reconciliation, endpoint resolution, model agreement, and human validation remain distinct states, while provenance and unresolved cases are preserved throughout. The validated IKB is then projected deterministically into the IDG without introducing new LLM-generated knowledge. Evaluation in nine infrastructure projects shows that entity recovery achieves substantially higher recall than complete directed dependency recovery and that canonical endpoint resolution is a major constraint in dependency construction. Cross-model overlap is also much lower for dependencies than for entities, indicating that the models often produce non-overlapping candidate assertions rather than a stable majority consensus. These findings support an auditable evidence-to-IKB-to-IDG process in which uncertainty is preserved and resolved progressively rather than collapsed into a single confidence or voting decision.

</details>


### [156] [An Open-Source End-to-End FHE Implementation for Privacy-Preserving Llama 3 8B Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12378)
*Yuhang Fan,Yusi Chen,Kanyu Ye,Zhuoran Ji*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Odin是一个面向Llama的FHE推理系统，通过协同设计密文打包与模型执行，在单块H100上实现Llama-3-8B全32层推理，耗时366.4s、峰值显存58.9GiB，较THOR基线加速4.51倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云LLM服务需将prompt发送给模型提供商，存在隐私风险。FHE可让服务器在不解密输入的情况下推理，但密文表示带来存储与计算开销。在CKKS-based LLM推理中，打包方案决定密文数量与线性层同态代价，并约束线性层、注意力与非线性计算间的数据传递方式。随着模型与序列增长，低效布局会累积编码、计算与布局转换开销。

Method: Odin协同设计密文打包与Llama模型执行。以THOR-style基线（瓶颈为权重编码）为起点，采用feature-major跨层布局统一残差连接与层接口，并构建临时的算子内布局用于线性投影与注意力，减少宽投影中权重的冗余明文编码。注意力中QK^T生成的分数可直接被Softmax消费，PV消费产生的概率，避免中间重打包。非线性算子采用带输入范围控制的minimax多项式近似，并以模型质量为导向进行联合误差分配，降低多项式次数与乘法深度。据称是首个开源端到端GPU CKKS的Llama-3实现。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B权重与128-token输入下，Odin在单块NVIDIA H100 80GB GPU上评估全部32个Transformer层。服务端端到端FHE评估耗时366.4s，峰值设备显存58.9GiB。在相同模型、输入、CKKS参数与硬件下，THOR耗时1651.9s，Odin实现4.51倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过密文打包与模型执行的协同设计，Odin有效缓解了CKKS-based LLM推理中布局转换与权重编码开销，在Llama-3-8B上实现显著加速与可接受的显存占用，验证了端到端GPU CKKS LLM推理的可行性。

Abstract: Cloud LLM services typically require users to send prompts to a model provider, creating a privacy risk. Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) lets a server perform inference without decrypting the input, but representing data as ciphertexts adds storage and computational overhead. In CKKS-based LLM inference, the packing scheme maps logical tensors to ciphertexts and slots. It therefore determines the ciphertext count and the homomorphic cost of linear layers, and it constrains how data pass between linear layers, attention, and nonlinear computation. As models and sequences grow, inefficient layouts accumulate encoding, compute, and layout-conversion overhead.
  We present Odin, an FHE inference system that co-designs ciphertext packing and model execution for Llama. Starting from a THOR-style baseline whose bottleneck is weight encoding, Odin uses a feature-major cross-layer layout to unify residual connections and layer interfaces, and builds transient intra-operator layouts for linear projections and attention. This reduces redundant plaintext encoding of weights in wide projections. Within attention, QK^T produces scores that Softmax can consume directly, and PV consumes the resulting probabilities, avoiding intermediate repacking. For nonlinear ops, we use minimax polynomial approximation with input-range control and joint error allocation guided by model quality, reducing polynomial degree and multiplicative depth. To our knowledge, Odin is the first open-source end-to-end GPU CKKS implementation of Llama-3.
  With Llama-3-8B weights and a 128-token input, Odin evaluates all 32 Transformer layers on a single NVIDIA H100 80 GB GPU. Server-side end-to-end FHE evaluation takes 366.4 s and 58.9 GiB peak device memory. Under the same model, input, CKKS parameters, and hardware, THOR takes 1651.9 s, a 4.51x speedup.

</details>


### [157] [IDORacle: Template-Guided SQL-Sink Mediation for Object-Level Authorization in Java Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12426)
*Yuewantong Song,Guanhang Shi,Yin Cai,Changhui Wang,Jin Wei,Ping Chen,Lei Shi,Jiangxing Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IDORacle 是一个模板引导的 SQL 汇聚点拦截与重写框架，用于在运行时阻止 Java SQL 应用中的水平越权（IDOR/BOLA）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Java 数据库应用中控制器或服务层的身份与授权校验与基于资源标识符的 SQL 执行相分离，导致 IDOR/BOLA 漏洞普遍存在；现有工作多侧重检测，缺乏对遗留 Java-SQL 应用的低侵入运行时防护手段。

Method: 通过服务端跟踪标识在 HTTP 请求、异步任务与数据访问边界间传播已认证身份上下文；在 MyBatis/JDBC 边界提取 SQL 模板、计算双重指纹，并进行一次性的模板分析以生成可复用的仲裁计划；运行时结合主体上下文、SQL AST、表元数据与缓存的授权证明，对操作执行放行、重写或阻断。其防护模型支持直接所有权谓词、连接派生所有权、组属资源探测、角色敏感状态转换以及敏感列仲裁。

Result: 基于真实 CVE 报告构建的 Java-SQL 基准测试表明，IDORacle 能阻止所测试的水平越权违规，最坏情况防护延迟为 0.17 ms；冗余感知优化使平均单实例开销降低 90% 以上，热 SQL 模板低至 0.017 ms。

Conclusion: IDORacle 通过在 SQL 汇聚点进行模板引导的运行时拦截与重写，实现了低侵入、低延迟的水平越权防护，适用于遗留 Java-SQL 应用。

Abstract: Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR), often modeled as Broken Object-Level Authorization (BOLA), remains prevalent in Java database applications because identity and authorization checks at the controller or service layer are disconnected from SQL execution based on resource identifiers. Existing work largely detects these vulnerabilities but offers limited low-intrusion runtime protection for legacy Java-SQL applications. We present IDORacle, a template-guided SQL-sink interception and rewriting framework for preventing horizontal privilege escalation at runtime. IDORacle propagates authenticated identity context across HTTP requests, asynchronous tasks, and data-access boundaries through a server-side trace identifier. At the MyBatis/JDBC boundary, it extracts SQL templates, computes dual fingerprints, and performs one-time template analysis to generate reusable mediation plans. During execution, it combines subject context, SQL ASTs, table metadata, and cached authorization proofs to permit, rewrite, or block operations. Its guard model supports direct ownership predicates, join-derived ownership, probes for group-owned resources, role-sensitive state transitions, and sensitive-column mediation. A Java-SQL benchmark grounded in real-world CVE reports shows that IDORacle prevents the tested horizontal authorization violations with a worst-case guard latency of 0.17 ms. Redundancy-aware optimization reduces average per-instance overhead by more than 90%, to 0.017 ms for hot SQL templates.

</details>


### [158] [PDoS: A Profitable Denial-of-Service Attack against Proof-of-Work Blockchain Liveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12450)
*Junjie Hu,Tianzhu Han,Na Ruan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PDoS，一种结合区块头信号威慑与寄生收益提取的混合攻击，可在破坏 PoW 区块链活跃性的同时利用受害者矿池的份额奖励机制补贴攻击成本，首次证明破坏 PoW 活跃性可经济自持甚至盈利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoW 区块链的安全性与活跃性依赖矿工的经济理性。现有激励驱动的拒绝服务攻击（如 BDoS）虽能阻止理性矿工参与，但要求攻击者持续承担巨额经济损失，在高价值网络中难以维持；而此前的渗透型扣块攻击主要用于获利，而非直接破坏链上活跃性。因此，如何在低成本、可持续的条件下破坏 PoW 活跃性是一个尚待解决的问题。

Method: 提出 PDoS 混合攻击：一方面利用区块头信号进行威慑，迫使理性矿工关机；另一方面通过寄生收益提取，利用受害者矿池的份额奖励（share-reward）机制获得收益以补贴攻击成本。基于此机制分析攻击者所需的对抗算力阈值，并构建盈亏平衡模型，讨论区块价值（高手续费/高 MEV）对攻击可持续性的影响。

Result: PDoS 降低了诱导理性矿工关机所需的对抗算力阈值。反直觉的是，在高手续费或高 MEV 环境下，更高的区块价值会通过增加攻击者的寄生收益，将攻击推过盈亏平衡点，进入自持甚至盈利的状态，从而使 PoW 系统反而更不安全。

Conclusion: PDoS 是首个表明破坏 PoW 区块链活跃性可以经济自持甚至盈利的攻击，揭示了高区块价值环境对 PoW 安全性的负面作用，说明单纯的经济理性假设下，链上活跃性可能被低成本、可持续的攻击方式所威胁。

Abstract: The security and liveness of Proof-of-Work (PoW) blockchains fundamentally depend on the economic rationality of miners. Existing incentive-driven denial-of-service attacks, such as BDoS, can deter rational miners from participating, but require the attacker to continuously absorb substantial economic losses and are therefore difficult to sustain in high-value networks. Meanwhile, prior infiltration-based withholding attacks are designed to extract revenue rather than to directly disrupt chain liveness.
  We present PDoS, a hybrid attack that combines block header signal deterrence with parasitic revenue extraction. PDoS disrupts blockchain liveness while exploiting the victim pool's share-reward mechanism to subsidize the attack cost, thereby lowering the adversarial hash-power threshold required to induce rational miners to shut down. We further show a counterintuitive result: in high-fee or high-MEV environments, higher block value can make PoW systems less secure by increasing the attacker's parasitic revenue and pushing the attack across the break-even point into a self-sustaining, or even profitable, regime. To the best of our knowledge, PDoS is the first attack to demonstrate that disrupting PoW blockchain liveness can be economically self-sustaining and even profitable.

</details>


### [159] [Access Control as Verified Parse Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12488)
*Saranachon Iammongkol,Zhiyi Huang,David Eyers*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一类结果：前向无回溯的EverParse验证器是有界有限状态类的经验证识别器，固定偏移字段与有界析取构成的访问控制决策函数属于该类，一次机器检验的证明可覆盖该类中所有策略。将策略决策函数编码为定长字节缓冲区，用SMT求解器一次性验证执行代码（覆盖所有字节取值），证明验证器当且仅当决策函数接受时才接受。在seL4微内核上演示部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业安全网关在网络与策略决策之间的代码路径上反复出现实现漏洞：手写执行逻辑偏离策略作者意图，以及网络边界处临时请求解析器自身引入内存安全缺陷。两类缺陷都在部署的执行代码中，而非策略本身。现有方法要么使执行运行时未经验证，要么仅通过差分测试把形式模型与手写引擎相连。

Method: 刻画一个前向、无回溯的EverParse验证器作为有界有限状态类的经验证识别器，并证明具有固定偏移字段和有界析取的访问控制决策函数属于该类，从而一次机器检验证明即可迁移至该类中的每个策略。将受限策略语言的决策函数编码进定长字节缓冲区，用SMT求解器一次性验证执行代码，覆盖所有字节取值，证明对所有策略、请求和会话，验证器接受当且仅当决策函数接受。在固定端点集上编辑规则内容无需新证明；新增端点需重跑工具链；扩展语言需新证明。验证门与平台无关，仅需EverParse/Z3与C编译器（假设其正确）。

Result: 建立了对策略的忠实执行，而非策略本身的安全性。在seL4微内核上完成部署演示，确保每个请求都经过该验证门，且未验证组件无法破坏经验证的执行链。

Conclusion: 通过将执行代码的验证提升为对整类策略一次成立的结果，避免了逐策略重复证明，并在具备隔离保证的微内核上实现端到端可信执行链的部署。

Abstract: Commercial security gateways repeatedly ship implementation bugs in the code path between the network and the policy decision: hand-written enforcement logic that diverges from the policy author's intent, and ad-hoc request parsers at the network boundary that introduce memory-safety flaws of their own. In both cases the bug is in the deployed enforcement code, not in the policy. Existing approaches either leave the enforcement runtime unverified or connect a formal model to a hand-written engine only by differential testing.
  Our contribution is a class result: a forward-only, backtrack-free EverParse validator is a verified recognizer for a bounded, finite-state class, and access-control decision functions with fixed-offset fields and bounded disjunction belong to it, so one machine-checked proof transfers to every policy in the class rather than being re-established per policy. Concretely, we encode a bounded policy language's decision function into a fixed-size byte buffer and verify the enforcement code once---covering all byte values---with an SMT solver, proving the validator accepts if and only if the decision function accepts, for every policy, request, and session. Editing rule content over a fixed endpoint set then needs no new proof; adding endpoints reruns the toolchain; extending the language needs new proofs. We establish faithful enforcement of a policy, not that a policy is itself secure.
  The verified gate is platform-independent, requiring only EverParse/Z3 and a C compiler, whose correctness we assume. We demonstrate a deployment on the seL4 microkernel, which ensures every request passes through the gate and that unverified components cannot corrupt the verified enforcement chain.

</details>


### [160] [Omniscience for the Masses: New Threats in the Metaverse's Democratized World Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12554)
*Andrea Mengascini,Ryan Aurelio,Jason Polakis,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估元宇宙"世界创作者"的安全与隐私风险，提出五种利用标准创作工具的新型攻击，可实现隐蔽监视与操纵，且现有平台防护不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙平台日益依赖用户生成的虚拟世界（如Roblox、Horizon Worlds、VRChat），这一模式将社交互动与世界行为的控制权交由不受信任的用户，但此前缺乏对该威胁面的系统研究。

Method: 调研25个支持用户创建世界的平台，分析其创作能力；据此设计并实现五种新型攻击，利用创作者提供的工具突破沉浸环境中的空间、视觉与听觉约束；并复现五种已有攻击。

Result: 五种新型攻击无需软件漏洞或开发者级权限即可实现隐蔽用户监视与操纵；五种既有攻击可仅用标准创作功能复现；现有平台审核、运行时防护与创作者政策均不足以缓解恶意行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户隐私期望与世界创作者所获权限之间的根本性错配，呼吁平台重新审视该威胁模型与防护机制。

Abstract: Metaverse platforms increasingly derive their success from user-generated virtual worlds: self-contained social and interactive environments, which can be created by any ordinary user and scale to billions of visits. Platforms such as Roblox, Horizon Worlds, and VRChat now host millions of creator-built worlds that govern how users see, hear, and interact with one another. While this model enables rapid growth and creativity, it fundamentally delegates control over social interactions and world behavior to untrusted users. In this paper, we present the first systematic security and privacy assessment of metaverse world creators. We survey 25 platforms that support user-created worlds and analyze their world-creation capabilities. Guided by this analysis, we design and implement five novel attacks that exploit creator-provided tools to violate spatial, visual, and auditory constraints in immersive environments, enabling covert user surveillance and manipulation without software vulnerabilities or developer-level privileges. We further show that five previously-proposed attacks can be replicated using only standard world-creation features. Finally, we find that existing platform vetting, runtime protections, and creator policies are insufficient to mitigate malicious world-creator behavior, revealing a fundamental mismatch between users' privacy expectations and the powers granted to world creators.

</details>


### [161] [PIA-Bench: Towards Automated Privacy Impact Assessment with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12571)
*Jiamin Zheng,Hao-Ping Lee,Luo Mai,Jingjie Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并构建了首个面向隐私影响评估（PIA）的开源基准 PIA-Bench，基于美国联邦机构 499 份专家 PIA 审计后整理出 73 份结构化 PIA（含 451 项隐私风险与 831 项缓解措施），用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在真实 PIA 场景下识别隐私风险与提出缓解措施的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PIA 是机构在系统上线前主动识别隐私风险并制定缓解策略的关键工具，虽在监管与机构层面被强制要求，但执行 PIA 需要大量隐私与技术专业知识，对缺乏相关资源的团队构成显著障碍。已有研究显示 LLM 可辅助隐私决策，但其自动化 PIA 的准确性与可靠性尚不明确，且缺乏真实场景的评估基准。

Method: 作者首先审计了美国联邦机构发布的 499 份专家撰写的 PIA，筛选并整理出 73 份结构化 PIA，共包含 451 项隐私风险条目与 831 项缓解措施条目，据此构建开源基准 PIA-Bench，用于评测 LLM 对复杂系统进行隐私风险评估与缓解措施生成的能力。

Result: 评测结果显示，现成（off-the-shelf）LLM 能够生成有意义的评估结果，同时也暴露出仍需改进的方向。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁进一步优化面向 LLM 智能体的领域特定工作流、发展可问责的 LLM 基础设施，并为 PIA 设计新的质量标准。

Abstract: Privacy impact assessment (PIA) is a critical instrument for institutions to proactively identify privacy risks and develop mitigation strategies before system deployment. While mandated across regulatory and institutional contexts, executing PIA requires extensive privacy and technical expertise, posing a particular challenge for teams without access to such resources. Prior work shows the potential of leveraging large language models (LLMs) to assist practitioners' privacy decisions, but little is known about how accurately and reliably LLMs can automate PIA. To this end, we develop PIA-Bench, the first open benchmark for evaluating LLMs on real-world PIAs. We first audited 499 expert-authored PIAs published by US federal agencies and curated 73 structured PIAs, comprising a total of 451 privacy risk and 831 mitigation items, to evaluate LLMs' ability to assess privacy risks and propose mitigations of complex systems. Our results show that off-the-shelf LLMs produce meaningful assessments and identify avenues for future improvement. Finally, we call for improving domain-specific workflows for LLM agents, developing accountable LLM infrastructure, and designing new quality standards for PIAs.

</details>


### [162] [MicroHasTEE: Bare-Metal Haskell for Type-Level Peripheral Ownership on Armv8-M](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12580)
*Robert Krook*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MicroHasTEE 是一个多方编程框架，用单一类型化 Haskell 程序统一描述 Armv8-M TrustZone 的 Secure 与 Non-secure 固件，借助类型级能力账本与索引化初始化计算在编译期捕获跨镜像不一致，并在 STM32U5 Nucleo 上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Armv8-M TrustZone 虽在硬件层隔离 Secure 与 Non-secure 软件，但开发者仍需跨两个独立编译的固件镜像协调外设归属、中断路由、初始化与网关接口。这些镜像间的不一致假设可能顺利通过编译，却只在目标设备上以故障形式暴露，缺乏编译期的静态保障。

Method: 提出 MicroHasTEE：将两个固件应用表达为同一类型化 Haskell 程序中的参与方。用类型级能力账本（type-level capability ledgers）表示外设权限；用索引化 setup 计算跟踪资源的获取、配置、转移与终结；用领域特定效应类型将外设操作与中断回调限制在持有相应权限的参与方；用类型化可调用句柄描述 Non-secure 代码可用的 Secure 服务。由 MicroHs 将共享程序编译两次，分别生成裸机 Secure 与 Non-secure 固件镜像。

Result: 在 STM32U5 Nucleo 板上实现，涵盖 TrustZone 配置、外设驱动及跨域 Haskell 调用的序列化网关。对经由该接口表达的程序，MicroHasTEE 能拒绝不一致的资源使用、配置后更改归属、错误域中的回调，以及调用未注册的 Secure 服务。门锁案例研究验证了可行性，固件镜像 flash 占用 232.7 KiB 与 228.4 KiB，每域 SRAM 约 220 KiB。

Conclusion: 通过将多方固件统一到单一类型化程序中，MicroHasTEE 把原本只能在目标设备上暴露的跨镜像不一致，转化为可在编译期检出的类型错误，为 TrustZone 多方固件开发提供了静态保障，并在真实硬件上验证了其可实现性与资源开销。

Abstract: Arm TrustZone for Armv8-M isolates Secure and Non-secure software, but developers must still coordinate peripheral attribution, interrupt routing, initialization, and gateway interfaces across separately built firmware images. Inconsistent assumptions between these images can compile successfully and emerge only as faults on the target device.
  We present MicroHasTEE, a multiparty programming framework that expresses both firmware applications as participants in one typed Haskell program. MicroHasTEE represents peripheral authority with type-level capability ledgers and uses indexed setup computations to track resource acquisition, configuration, transfer, and finalization. Domain-specific effect types restrict peripheral operations and interrupt callbacks to the participant that holds the corresponding authority, while typed callable handles describe the Secure services available to Non-secure code. MicroHs compiles the shared program twice to produce separate bare-metal Secure and Non-secure firmware images.
  We implement MicroHasTEE for an STM32U5 Nucleo board, including TrustZone configuration, peripheral drivers, and a serialized gateway for cross-domain Haskell calls. For programs expressed through its interface, MicroHasTEE rejects inconsistent resource use, attribution changes after configuration, callbacks in the wrong domain, and calls to unregistered Secure services. A door-lock case study demonstrates feasibility, with firmware images occupying 232.7 KiB and 228.4 KiB of flash and approximately 220 KiB of SRAM per domain.

</details>


### [163] [NovaFabric: Tamper-Evident, Replayable Evidence for Autonomous AI Agent Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12582)
*Mohsen Seyedkazemi Ardebili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NovaFabric 将 AI Agent 执行过程录制为可移植、防篡改、可重放的 Run Capsule，并导出为可供第三方用通用工具验证的 Evidence Bundle；其贡献在于系统集成而非新密码学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI Agent 的关键行为缺乏可证明性：现有 Agent 可观测平台捕获的 trace 可变且无痕、缺少重执行方法、也无法证明敏感信息已被移除。同时 EU AI Act、ISO 42001、NIST AI RMF 等监管要求独立第三方可核查的记录，形成监管与工程能力之间的缺口。

Method: 在不修改 Agent 逻辑的前提下，将一次 Agent 运行录制为十五实体模式的 Run Capsule，并以整体 DSSE 签名、RFC 3161 时间戳、Merkle 日志与脱敏证明进行封存；封存后的运行可通过四模式重放协议重新执行，并导出为 Evidence Bundle 供第三方用现成工具验证。技术栈复用 OpenTelemetry、DSSE/in-toto 与 W3C PROV。

Result: 在界定范围内评估八个研究问题：Mocked replay 以 capsule 回放全部模型响应（无实时模型调用，10/10），但仅对模型离线、不对网络离线，仅 2/10 使用工具的负载完成，缺口在于缺少工具响应替换；三类篡改均被拒绝；声明流完整性 0.652（95% CI ±0.064，十个场景）；修复后的规则包对 14/14 凭据类型完成脱敏并保留 9/9 诱饵，diff 定位 140/140 变异；爆炸半径查询 1000 万边 p99 45.5ms（比列式基线快 3.3 倍），1 亿边 167.9ms（1 客户端，n=30）；314 机器、十区域运行中 capsule REST 摄取无损，但受单 worker 串行化限制，上限 61.6 req/s（p99 26.8s）。共发现六个缺陷：四个已修复、一个撤回、一个未解决。

Conclusion: NovaFabric 提供了面向审计级执行证据的端到端集成方案，实现可重放、防篡改与可分享的证据链；但其验证能力以明确声明的可信计算基为前提，且工具调用场景的重放与摄取吞吐仍是待解缺口。

Abstract: When an autonomous AI agent does something consequential, what can be proven about what it did? Agent-observability platforms capture traces, but a trace is mutable: alterable undetected, with no recipe for re-executing it, silent on whether captured secrets were removed. Regulation (EU AI Act, ISO 42001, NIST AI RMF) presumes records an independent party can check.
  We present NovaFabric, producing audit-grade execution evidence: provider-neutral, tamper-evident, replayable, shareable. It records an agent run, without modifying agent logic, into a portable Run Capsule (fifteen-entity schema), sealed with a holistic DSSE signature, RFC 3161 timestamp, Merkle log and redaction attestation. Sealed runs are re-executable under a four-mode replay protocol and exportable as an Evidence Bundle for third-party verification with stock tooling (specified, not evaluated). The contribution is integration, not new cryptography: OpenTelemetry, DSSE/in-toto and W3C PROV.
  We evaluate eight research questions at measured scope. Mocked replay serves every model response from the capsule (no live model call, 10/10) but is offline w.r.t. models, not the network; only 2/10 tool-using workloads completed; the gap is missing tool-response substitution. Tampering is rejected across three tested classes. Declared-stream completeness is 0.652 (95% CI +/-0.064, ten scenarios). A repaired rule pack redacts 14/14 credential types, preserving 9/9 decoys; diff localises 140/140 mutations. Blast-radius queries: 45.5ms p99 over 10M edges (3.3x faster than a columnar baseline), 167.9ms over 100M (1 client, n=30). A 314-machine, ten-region run finds capsule REST ingest lossless but capped at 61.6 req/s (p99 26.8s) by per-worker serialisation. Six defects found in NovaFabric and its evaluation corpus: four fixed, one withdrawn, one open.
  Verification is conditional on a stated trusted computing base.

</details>


### [164] [A Feature-Rich Embedded NIDS with eBPF/XDP: Detector and Architecture Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12605)
*Shiqi Wu,Oleksii Koshovyi,Georgios Pseiridis Pseiras,Victor Morel,Romaric Duvignau*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向传输网络的DDoS检测NIDS（与Ericsson合作），在统计基线上引入Isolation Forest与GoFlowMeter流特征，并用eBPF/XDP在内核层过滤流量；在Raspberry Pi 5上比较单体、Kafka与gRPC三种部署，发现检测质量主要取决于检测器选择而非传输架构，gRPC在低开销下接近单体精度，Kafka异步流水线明显落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDoS攻击对传输网络构成严重威胁，攻击规模已超过30 Tbps，电信行业是主要目标。已有工作尚未研究承载软件架构对网络监控方案的影响，也未评估近期改进攻击检测的算法，因此需要系统性研究检测算法与部署架构的组合效果。

Method: 构建面向传输网络的NIDS：以统计方法为基线，使用在更广泛流特征上训练的Isolation Forest提升检测效果；流特征由开源的GoFlowMeter（CICFlowMeter的Go实现）提取；集成eBPF/XDP实现内核级真实流量过滤。对比三种部署形态——单体、基于Kafka、基于gRPC的微服务——在Raspberry Pi 5测试床上回放CIC-DDoS2019数据集作为真实网络流量。

Result: 检测质量主要由检测器而非传输方式决定：Isolation Forest相较基线提升召回率与F1分数，单体变体实时F1达0.965，并能识别基线遗漏的低流量攻击窗口。传输架构并非中性：gRPC精度几乎与单体持平，每窗口仅增加不到2毫秒传输时间；异步Kafka流水线落后约9个百分点，且每窗口增加约27毫秒。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了在资源受限硬件上部署NIDS时检测质量与架构开销之间的权衡：优先选择更强检测器（如Isolation Forest），并在架构上倾向低开销的gRPC微服务或单体方案，而非异步Kafka流水线。

Abstract: Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks remain a serious threat to transport networks, with recent attack volumes exceeding 30 Tbps, and the telecommunications industry being the main target. Recent work has yet to study the impact of the hosting software architecture on network monitoring solutions, or to assess recent algorithms for improving attack detection. This paper presents a Network Intrusion Detection System (NIDS) for DDoS detection in transport networks, developed in collaboration with Ericsson. Building on a statistical baseline, we improve detection effectiveness with an Isolation Forest trained on a wider set of flow features, extracted by GoFlowMeter, our open-source Go implementation of CICFlowMeter, and we integrate eBPF/XDP so that the NIDS filters real traffic at the kernel level. We further compare three deployments, monolithic, Kafka-based, and gRPC-based microservices, on a Raspberry Pi 5 testbed replaying the CIC-DDoS2019 dataset as real network traffic. Detection quality is governed mainly by the choice of detector rather than by the transport: the Isolation Forest raises recall and F1 score (0.965 live in the monolithic variant) over the baseline by flagging low-volume attack windows that the baseline misses. The transport is not neutral, however: gRPC reaches almost the same accuracy as the monolithic variant while adding less than 2 milliseconds of transport time per window, whereas the asynchronous Kafka pipeline trails by roughly nine percentage points and adds about 27 milliseconds. These findings clarify the trade-off between detection quality and architectural overhead when deploying a NIDS on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [165] [Subgroup Packing for Batched PASTA Transciphering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12624)
*Mugurel Barcau,Vicenţiu Paşol,George C. Ţurcaş*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 改变记录字在密文中的排布方式（交错/子群布局）可将 PASTA-3 转密所需的稠密线性映射实现成本降低约 1.6 倍，但代价是剩余噪声容量减少，形成一种打包依赖的成本—噪声权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 转密（transciphering）中，服务器需在不获知记录与对称密钥的前提下把对称加密记录转换为同态密文。对 PASTA 密码而言，该转换依赖稠密线性映射，其实现开销取决于记录字在密文中的排布方式。本文旨在探究：在固定批量的前提下，能否通过重排布局来降低转换成本。

Method: 提出一种交错（interleaved）布局：不再把每条记录的字存放在连续块中，而是让各记录交错排列，使得循环字移位仍保持每条记录的位置，这些位置构成一个循环子群的陪集。针对"以掩码平移之和直接求值"的方式，刻画所需的位移量，并将其计数（连续布局 255、子群布局 128）与既有的横截差（transversal-difference）不变量相关联。随后在 HElib 中实现三种等批量调度，用于完整 PASTA-3 转换及随后的公开子集和查询。

Result: 在六种同态密钥、十二组配对语料下，全部 24 次直接与子群转换以及全部 48 次后续查询均返回期望值与所需的零值。以直接布局对子群布局的服务器成本中位配对比为 1.60（含新鲜公开生成、转换与两次查询）。但成本下降的同时剩余噪声容量变小。

Conclusion: 重排固定批量可显著降低 PASTA 转密的线性映射成本，且位移计数可由横截差不变量解释；但该收益伴随噪声容量的下降，从而确立了一种打包依赖的成本—噪声权衡关系。

Abstract: With transciphering, a server converts symmetrically encrypted records into homomorphic ciphertexts without learning the records or the symmetric key. For the PASTA cipher, this conversion involves dense linear maps whose implementation depends on how record words are arranged in the ciphertext. We ask whether rearranging a fixed batch can reduce its conversion cost. Instead of storing each record's words in a contiguous block, our layout interleaves records so that cyclic word shifts preserve each record's positions, which form a coset of a cyclic subgroup. For direct evaluation as a sum of masked translations, we characterize the required displacements and relate their counts, 255 for the contiguous layout and 128 for the subgroup layout, to a prior transversal-difference invariant.
  We implement three equally batched schedules for complete PASTA-3 conversion and subsequent public subset-sum queries in HElib. Across twelve paired corpora under six homomorphic keys, all 24 direct and subgroup conversions and all 48 subsequent queries return the expected values and required zeroes. The median paired ratio of direct to subgroup server cost is 1.60, including fresh public generation, conversion and two queries. The reduction comes with less remaining noise capacity. These results establish a packing-dependent cost--noise tradeoff.

</details>


### [166] [Poster: Towards Selecting Threat Appropriate Industrial Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12646)
*Stefan Lenz,Johannes Weidmann,Martin Henze*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于"对抗威胁情报共享"的检测器选择机制，以应对工业控制系统动态威胁，并通过攻击级实验验证不同入侵检测系统性能随攻击场景变化，证明该机制的潜在价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业控制系统面临的威胁态势是动态演变的，而传统安全防护难以对不断变化的威胁做出及时反应，因此需要能够根据当前威胁情况动态调整的检测策略。

Method: 提出一种以对抗威胁情报共享（counter-threat intelligence sharing）为基础的机制，用于为当前环境/情境选择适配的入侵检测器；并对多种入侵检测系统开展攻击级别（attack-level）的性能评估。

Result: 评估结果显示，入侵检测性能随攻击场景的不同而存在明显差异（性能具有场景依赖性）。

Conclusion: 检测性能的场景依赖性凸显了所提情报共享式检测器选择机制对工业控制系统安全的价值与潜力。

Abstract: As the threat landscape against industrial control systems is dynamic, effective security requires detection strategies capable of timely reactions to these evolving threat situations. To address this problem, we propose the idea of a counter-threat intelligence sharing based mechanism to select appropriate detectors for current circumstances. To highlight the potential of this mechanism, we conduct attack-level performance evaluations of various intrusion detection systems. Results show the variance of intrusion detection performance depending on the attack scenario, emphasizing the benefits of such a mechanism for industrial control system security

</details>


### [167] [Bridging the First-Hour Gap: Evaluating AI Reliability and Benchmarking Deficiencies in Cyber Incident Response for Law Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12681)
*Roshin Sleeba C,Hiran V Nath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向网络犯罪一线响应人员的决策支持架构，涵盖playbook、LLM、RAG与Agentic AI四类，指出RAG是较可行的过渡方案，但现有基准不足以覆盖执法场景的安全与法律需求，呼吁建立新的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络事件发生最初一小时内，一线执法人员的操作对调查成败具有决定性影响；微小失误可能因数字证据的易失性导致程序错误、证据流失，进而破坏取证完整性并妨碍对网络犯罪分子的起诉。现实场景中还存在技术能力有限、取证基础设施不统一等约束，而现有研究缺乏对适配这些约束的决策支持架构的系统梳理。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，将面向网络犯罪一线响应人员的决策支持架构划分为四类：处置手册（playbooks）、大语言模型（LLMs）、检索增强生成（RAG）框架以及Agentic AI系统，并批判性地考察其在一线技术能力受限与取证基础设施不一致等实际约束下的适用性；同时回顾现有网络安全基准，评估其是否满足执法场景的安全与法律要求。

Result: 分析表明，RAG类系统凭借自然语言适应能力，是相对可行的中间方案；但其存在显著风险因素，如对提示词敏感以及在法律语境中可能出现高水平自信的幻觉。此外，现有网络安全基准不足以刻画执法部门在案发最初一小时内的特定安全与法律要求。

Conclusion: 有必要构建一个以朴素查询鲁棒性与证据保全为核心关注点的新型评估基准，以确保AI驱动的指导能够满足司法程序的强制性要求。

Abstract: The actions of frontline law enforcement officers in the initial hour of a cyber incident play a vital role in determining the ultimate success of an investigation. The minor mistakes they commit might result in irreversible critical impacts. The integrity of the investigation can be compromised, and the prosecution of cyber criminals can be hindered due to minor mistakes that happen in the initial hour. These are mainly because of the volatile nature of digital artifacts that might lead to procedural errors and evidence attrition. This paper provides a systematic survey of decision-support architectures designed to assist first responders of a cybercrime, categorizing them into playbooks, Large Language Models (LLMs), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) frameworks, and Agentic AI systems. The survey critically considers the constraints of limited technical proficiency and inconsistent forensic infrastructure in a practical scenario. Our analysis identifies RAG-based systems as a relatively viable intermediate solution due to their natural language adaptability. However, significant risk factors like prompt sensitivity and the potential for confident hallucinations in legal contexts pose a major challenge. Furthermore, we review current benchmarks in cybersecurity and demonstrate that they are not sufficient to capture the specific safety and legal requirements of law enforcement, focusing on the initial hour of the cybercrime. We conclude by arguing for the necessity of a new evaluation benchmark focused on naive query robustness and evidence preservation, so as to ensure that AI-driven guidance aligns with the mandatory demands of judicial proceedings.

</details>


### [168] [Fresh-Challenge VDF Attestations for Model-Relative Response Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12727)
*Ansar Yesmukhanov,Aruzhan Tlessova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种将VDF与不可预测公开挑战绑定的协议组合FCLA，用于在有限验证者模型下获得关于顺序计算响应延迟的公开、模型相对证据，并给出公开参考实现的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个VDF证明对选定消息而言可能被预先计算，因此不足以证明延迟发生在某个公开挑战之后。作者希望让有限验证者能够获得可公开审计的、关于顺序计算响应延迟的模型相对证据。

Method: 提出FCLA协议组合：将VDF与不可预测的公开挑战、消息以及可独立审计的发布与接收记录绑定；在关于VDF顺序性、挑战来源、见证日志以及对手顺序评估速率校准上界的显式假设下进行分析；并对公开参考实现进行基准测试。

Result: 在所设定的有界模型中，被接受的FCLA转录与对手在挑战后生成相矛盾；基准测试在单一记录机器上确认了评估与验证之间预期的经验性分离。该结果既不指认具名的声明者，也不排除中继、外包或更快的未建模机器。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是协议/设计分析与带基准测试的参考实现层，而非新的VDF构造或密码学原语；其为顺序计算延迟的公开可验证证据提供了一种模型相对但不完全排除外部因素的方法。

Abstract: Can a finite verifier obtain public, model-relative evidence about response latency for sequential computation? Verifiable delay functions (VDFs) make this possible in principle: evaluation requires T sequential steps, whereas verification is efficient in the security parameter and polylogarithmic in the numerical value of T for standard constructions. Thus a delay can be astronomically large to evaluate yet succinctly represented and feasibly checked. A VDF proof for a chosen message alone is insufficient because it may be precomputed. We specify and analyze Fresh-Challenge VDF Attestations (FCLA), a protocol composition that binds a VDF to an unpredictable public challenge, a message, and independently auditable release and receipt records. Under explicit assumptions about VDF sequentiality, the challenge source, witness logs, and a calibrated upper bound on an adversary's sequential evaluation rate, an accepted FCLA transcript is inconsistent with post-challenge generation by an adversary in that bounded model. The result neither identifies a named claimant nor excludes relaying, outsourcing, or a faster unmodeled machine. A benchmark of the public reference implementation confirms the expected empirical separation between evaluation and verification on one documented machine. Our contribution is a protocol/design analysis and benchmarked reference implementation layer, not a new VDF construction or cryptographic primitive.

</details>


### [169] [Batten the Hatches: Cybersecurity with Military Mariners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12810)
*Ryan Von Brock,Anna Raymaker,Animesh Chhotaray,Frank Li,Saman Zonouz,Raheem Beyah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对20名美国海军与海岸警卫队船员的半结构化访谈，初步揭示了军方人员如何理解、识别与应对网络风险，指出舰上网络安全在组织层面高度抽象，船员多依赖非正式经验构建风险模型，并以安全导向的事件响应方式提升韧性，但可能延误归因与遏制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络战已成为当代地缘政治冲突的关键组成部分，但关于军事组织及其人员如何开展网络安全工作的系统性研究极为有限。军事情境具有独特性：资源极度充裕、指挥链严格、行动后果异常严重，使其网络安全成为一个独立却研究不足的议题。

Method: 聚焦海上军种，在充分考虑组织性障碍的基础上设计了一项非保密研究，对美国海军与海岸警卫队舰艇的20名军事船员进行半结构化访谈，考察其对网络风险的理解、识别与响应方式。

Result: 识别出军事系统被攻陷的独特后果，包括武器被接管与蓄意的地缘政治升级；发现舰上网络安全在组织层面是抽象的，船员的风险模型主要源于非正式经验而非正式训练；船员通过识别作战影响将网络安全付诸行动，并采用安全导向的事件响应模式，从而形成韧性，但可能延误网络归因与遏制。

Conclusion: 研究提出了可操作的建议，帮助军事操作人员框定网络威胁、将悠久的海事条令与现代系统相结合，并将军事经验应用于民用领域，以保障更广泛的海上环境安全。

Abstract: Cyberwarfare has become a key component of contemporary geopolitical conflict. However, there has been extremely limited systematic investigation into how cybersecurity is handled by military organizations and personnel. The military context is unique compared to other operational ones, with immense resource availability (U.S. military spending approached 1 trillion dollars in 2024), a rigid chain of command, and extraordinary consequences for its actions. Thus, military cybersecurity is a distinct yet understudied topic.
  In this paper, we take an early step at understanding military cybersecurity by investigating how service members understand, recognize, and respond to cyber risk. We focus on maritime services and carefully consider organizational barriers to design an unclassified study and conduct semi-structured interviews with 20 military mariners from U.S. Navy and Coast Guard vessels. Through our investigation, we identify unique consequences of compromising military systems, including weapon takeover and purposeful geopolitical escalation. We find that cybersecurity is organizationally abstract on ships, so mariners build cyber risk models from informal experience rather than formal instruction. They nonetheless make cybersecurity actionable by recognizing operational impacts and responding with a safety-oriented incident-response model that creates resilience but may delay cyber attribution and containment. These findings inform actionable recommendations to help military operators frame cyber threats, merge longstanding nautical doctrine with modern systems, and apply military insights to the civilian sector, all to secure the broader maritime environment.

</details>


### [170] [Evaluating Context Segmentation in Locally Deployable SLMs for Cybersecurity CTF Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12839)
*Sebastiano Nordio,Michele Lotto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"context segmentation"两级智能体框架，将复杂漏洞利用任务拆分为上下文隔离的子问题，缓解SLM智能体在长程探索任务中的上下文膨胀与认知退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源小语言模型（SLM）普及了高级网络攻防能力，本地部署可绕过专有API的护栏，安全风险上升。但SLM作为自主智能体执行CTF等长程探索任务时，受限于累积工具调用输出导致的上下文膨胀与认知退化，表现不佳。研究动机在于理解并缓解这一安全威胁。

Method: 提出context segmentation两级智能体框架，将复杂漏洞利用任务分解为可管理、上下文相互隔离的子问题。在picoCTF数据集上，使用内存受限的gemma-4模型进行评估。

Result: 对E4B模型，该策略表现为一种智能搜索，相比暴力重试能以更优的token效率取得有竞争力的奖励，并成功解决了标准智能体执行无法完成的18.52%的任务。

Conclusion: 上下文分段可有效缓解SLM智能体在长程攻防任务中的上下文膨胀与认知退化，在token效率与任务成功率上均优于暴力重试，为理解和应对开源SLM带来的安全威胁提供了可行思路。

Abstract: The proliferation of highly capable open-weight Small Language Models (SLMs) democratizes access to advanced cybersecurity capabilities, posing a escalating risk as these models can bypass proprietary API guardrails when deployed locally. However, SLMs deployed as autonomous agents often struggle with long-horizon, exploratory tasks like cybersecurity Capture The Flag (CTF) challenges due to context bloat and cognitive degradation from accumulated tool-call outputs. To understand and mitigate this cybersecurity threat, we introduce \textit{context segmentation}, a two-level agentic framework that divides complex exploitation tasks into manageable, contextually isolated sub-problems. Evaluating on the \texttt{picoCTF} dataset using memory-constrained \texttt{gemma-4} models, we demonstrate that for the E4B model, our strategy acts as an intelligent search, achieving competitive rewards with superior token efficiency compared to brute-force retries, and successfully solving 18.52\% of tasks that standard agentic execution fails to complete. Code is available at https://github.com/9xeb/context-segmentation.

</details>


### [171] [A Graph-Based Approach for Mapping Kernel-Level Telemetry to MITRE ATT&CK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12841)
*Matteo Lupinacci,Luigi Arena,Francesco Blefari,Angelo Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Trace2ATT&CK端到端流水线：用eBPF采集内核级事件，构建溯源图并生成紧凑图表示，结合LLM与RAG映射到MITRE ATT&CK，在347个Linux Atomic Red Team测试上验证，RAG优于纯提示、溯源图优于原始遥测，证明基于本地开源模型的自动化映射具备可操作性且不损害数据机密性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将观测到的系统行为映射到MITRE ATT&CK等标准框架是威胁知情防御的关键，但目前主要依赖人工。现有自动化方法依赖CTI报告，只能提供事后的攻击描述；而内核级系统调用等低层遥测虽能直接反映对手行为，却因数据量大、复杂度高而难以用于自动映射。

Method: 通过eBPF采集内核级事件，将攻击者命令关联为溯源图（provenance graph），并派生出适合LLM推理的紧凑图表示；随后使用纯LLM提示与基于ATT&CK知识库的检索增强生成（RAG）两种方式，将表示映射到MITRE ATT&CK，输出带支持理由的技术候选排序。该方法实现为端到端流水线Trace2ATT&CK。

Result: 在347个Linux Atomic Red Team测试上，使用本地部署的开源权重LLM评估：RAG相比纯提示持续提升ATT&CK映射性能；溯源图表示显著优于原始遥测数据。

Conclusion: 基于图化行为描述的本地推理能够使从内核级遥测到ATT&CK的自动映射在运行上可行，并且无需牺牲数据机密性。

Abstract: Mapping observed system behavior to standardized frameworks like MITRE ATT&CK is essential for threat-informed defense, but remains largely manual. Existing automated methods depend on Cyber Threat Intelligence reports, which offer only retrospective accounts of attacks. Low-level telemetry, i.e. kernel-level system calls, instead provides evidence of adversary behavior, yet its volume and complexity have limited its use for automated mapping. We present a methodology that collects kernel-level events via eBPF, correlates attacker commands into a provenance graph, and derives compact graph representations suitable for LLM-based reasoning.
