These representations are mapped to the MITRE ATT&CK framework using both pure LLM prompting and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounded in the ATT&CK knowledge base, producing ranked technique candidates along with supporting rationales. We implement this methodology as an end-to-end pipeline, named Trace2ATT&CK and evaluate it on 347 Linux Atomic Red Team tests using locally deployed open-weights LLMs. RAG consistently improves ATT&CK mapping performance over pure prompting, while provenance graph substantially outperforms raw telemetry. These results show that local inference over graph-based behavioral descriptions can make automated ATT&CK mapping from kernel-level telemetry operationally viable, without compromising data confidentiality.

</details>


### [172] [Forging Tree-Ring: Reproducing and Instrumenting Black-Box Semantic Watermark Forgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12909)
*Saifur Rahman Tamim,Md Taslimul Hasan Toufique,A. M. Tayeful Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在低显存双 T4 环境下复现了 Müller 等人针对 Tree-Ring 语义水印的 Reprompt 伪造攻击，攻击成功复现（伪造 5/6、真实 6/6、干净 0/6），并给出三项受约束运行下的附加结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tree-Ring 等语义水印方案本应在扩散模型初始噪声隐变量中嵌入可检测模式，但近期工作表明这类水印不仅可被移除还可被伪造——攻击者无需接触水印密钥即可生成能被真实检测器接受的图像。作者希望在远低于原研究的硬件条件下（免费层双 T4、每卡约 14.6 GB 可用显存，而原始研究使用 A40）验证该伪造攻击的可复现性，并借此审视检测器的实现细节。

Method: 使用作者发布的代码，在 Stable Diffusion XL 上复现 Müller 等人的 Reprompt 伪造攻击，运行于免费层双 T4 GPU（每卡 14.6 GB 可用显存）。实验共设三组（arms）、每组六次试验，统计真实图像、干净图像与伪造图像的检测情况与单次攻击耗时。此外在受限条件下开展三项工作：(1) 检测器仅返回非中心 χ² 统计量的 CDF，作者恢复被丢弃的统计量并基于其构造两种自然得分；(2) 半精度运行 SDXL 需修补流水线中直接调用自编码器的路径，并通过对照探针验证修补不影响检测器统计量；(3) 依据检测器源码做出预测，再用实测结果进行检验。

Result: 攻击成功复现：三组六次试验中，真实图像检测为 6/6，干净图像为 0/6，伪造图像为 5/6，单次攻击耗时 325–332 秒。恢复的非中心 χ² 统计量与官方检测器完全一致，由其构造的两种得分在同样十八个观测上区分伪造组与干净零假设的 AUC 分别为 0.861 和 0.972。对照探针确认半精度修补路径不改变检测器统计量。此外，作者基于源码阅读所做的一项预测被实测结果所否证。

Conclusion: 该研究在远低于原研究的显存条件下独立复现了针对 Tree-Ring 的 Reprompt 伪造攻击，证实其可复现性与实用性（伪造 5/6）。工作同时指出检测器实现中可恢复的被丢弃统计量具有更强的判别力，验证了半精度修补的统计无害性，并报告了一次被实验否证的源码推断，随论文一并公开了 notebook、固定版本分叉与全部测量产物。

Abstract: Semantic watermarking schemes such as Tree-Ring hide a detectable pattern in the initial noise latent of a diffusion model. Recent work shows these watermarks are not only removable but forgeable: an attacker who never sees the watermarking key can still produce images the genuine detector accepts. We reproduce the Reprompt forgery attack of Müller et al. against Tree-Ring on Stable Diffusion XL, using the authors' released code, on free-tier dual T4 GPUs with 14.6 GB of usable memory per device, substantially less per-GPU memory than the A40 hardware used in the original study. The attack reproduces. Over six trials of three arms we detect genuine images 6/6, clean images 0/6, and forged images 5/6, at 325-332 s per attack. Three further results came out of running it under constraint. The released detector computes a non-central $χ^2$ statistic and hands back only its CDF, so we recovered the discarded statistic; our recovery matches the released detector exactly, and two natural scores built from it separate the forged arm from the clean null at AUC 0.861 and 0.972 on the same eighteen observations. Running SDXL in half precision requires patching the pipeline's direct autoencoder calls, and a controlled probe confirms the patched path leaves the detector statistic unchanged. Finally, we report a prediction we made from reading the detector source that our measurements then contradicted. The notebook, the pinned fork and every measurement artifact are released with the paper.

</details>


### [173] [Shuffling is Not Enough: Breaking Permutation-Based Model Confidentiality in Hybrid FHE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12911)
*Jiseung Kim,Hyung Tae Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明混合FHE推理中基于噪声输出置换与shuffle-model差分隐私的模型保密方案在正确性约束下失效：$d+1$个查询即可精确恢复$d$输入线性层的置换不变摘要，从而完全区分模型，并在ResNet-20等网络上实现零误差端到端恢复所有线性层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合FHE推理通过服务器同态计算线性层、客户端解密并处理非线性来提升隐私推理的实用性。近期方案试图用带噪、输出置换的响应并借助shuffle-model差分隐私保护模型机密性，但作者怀疑这种保护在混合FHE所需的正确性约束下并不成立，因此需要检验其实际安全性。

Method: 作者从理论层面分析：对$d$输入线性层，证明$d+1$个可接受查询足以精确恢复置换不变的层摘要，进而实现完美模型区分；同时论证输入DP与模型保密性正交，且正确性有界噪声下shuffle放大所需的局部DP前提无法成立。实验上，使用每层$d+1$个查询、共$5{,}712$个直接查询，从TFHE转录中零误差恢复SAFHIRE风格ResNet-20的所有线性层，并在预训练ImageNet规模CNN与ViT-B/16上验证逐层精确恢复。

Result: 理论上，$d+1$个查询即可精确恢复置换不变层摘要，实现完美模型区分；输入DP与模型保密性正交，正确性有界噪声下局部DP前提不成立。实验中，以每层$d+1$个查询、总计$5{,}712$个直接查询，从TFHE转录中零误差恢复ResNet-20全部线性层，并在ImageNet规模CNN和ViT-B/16上确认逐层精确恢复。泄露的谱可用于指纹识别、谱系归属和更好的logit提取，而抑制这些谱会破坏推理效用。

Conclusion: 混合FHE推理中依赖噪声输出置换与shuffle-model DP的模型保密方案在正确性约束下不成立，攻击者仅需少量查询即可精确恢复线性层并区分模型；泄露的谱信息具有额外利用价值，而对其进行抑制会牺牲推理效用，表明模型保密性与推理效用之间存在根本冲突。

Abstract: Hybrid fully homomorphic encryption~(FHE) inference improves the practicality of private inference by letting the server evaluate linear layers homomorphically while the client decrypts and applies nonlinearities. Recent schemes attempt to protect model confidentiality by returning noisy, output-permuted responses and appealing to shuffle-model differential privacy~(DP). We show that this protection fails in the correctness regime required by hybrid FHE systems. For a $d$-input linear layer, $d+1$ admissible queries suffice for exact recovery of a permutation-invariant layer summary, hence for perfect model distinguishability. We further show that input DP is orthogonal to model confidentiality and that the local-DP premise required for shuffle amplification cannot hold under correctness-bounded noise. We recover all linear layers of a \safhire{}-style ResNet-20 end-to-end from TFHE transcripts with zero error, using $d+1$ queries per layer for a total of $5{,}712$ direct queries. Under the same query model, we also confirm exact per-layer recovery on pretrained ImageNet-scale CNNs and ViT-B/16. The leaked spectra enable fingerprinting, lineage attribution, and improved logit-based extraction, while suppressing them destroys inference utility.

</details>


### [174] [IntentFuzz: A Protocol-Aware Fuzzer for Automated Invariant Violation Detection in Intent-Based Cross-Chain Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13004)
*André Augusto,Christof Ferreira Torres,André Vasconcelos,Miguel Correia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出意图桥漏洞分类taxonomy与协议感知模糊测试器IntentFuzz，可直接从无标注Solidity源码恢复意图结构并合成多步测试序列，在基准、真实部署与植入漏洞合约上均取得高召回高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥用于在区块链间转移价值，其中意图桥由solver先行完成用户声明的结果、再由链下结算层与存款对账。现有智能合约模糊测试器与静态分析器只能识别已知坏模式，或依赖协议特定的手写断言，无法处理意图桥中"本地不变量"与"合法委托给链下结算的风险敞口"之间的区别，因此缺乏协议感知的通用检测手段。

Method: 1) 形式化taxonomy，区分合约必须在本地强制的不变量违规与合法委托给链下结算层的结算敞口；2) 提出IntentFuzz：从无标注Solidity源码中恢复桥的意图结构及deposit/fill函数角色；3) 合成多步模糊测试序列，并引入基于LLM的fallback辅助构建调用参数；4) 在基准协议、人工标注合约语料、植入bug变异体和真实部署上进行评估。

Result: 在9/9基准协议中恢复正确意图结构；deposit与fill函数分类召回率100%、综合精度82%；77个手工标注合约上桥分类精度79.5%、召回97.2%，确认桥中struct选择精度与召回均为88.6%，deposit和fill分类各达100%召回；23个植入bug变异体上100%召回与精度，执行273个模板（507笔交易，每模板中位14ms）；24个真实部署中，纯启发式输入生成确认17个真实不变量违规，启用LLM辅助层后升至22个，覆盖8个易受攻击的GitHub仓库，且每个发现均可对公开部署字节码复现。

Conclusion: IntentFuzz通过协议感知地恢复意图结构并结合启发式与LLM辅助输入生成，能够在不依赖手写断言的情况下有效发现意图桥中的真实不变量违规，且发现可对公开部署字节码复现，验证了该方法的实用性与可复现性。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges move value between blockchains. Intent-based bridges are a variant where a solver fulfills a user's declared outcome and an off-chain settlement layer later reconciles the fill against the deposit. Existing smart-contract fuzzers and static analyzers only flag known-bad code patterns or require protocol-specific hand-written assertions. This work formalizes a taxonomy separating invariant violations, safety properties a contract must enforce locally, from settlement exposures legitimately delegated to the off-chain settlement layer, and proposes IntentFuzz: a protocol-aware fuzzer that recovers a bridge's intent structure and deposit/fill function roles directly from unannotated Solidity source, then synthesizes multi-step fuzz sequences using an LLM-based fallback to help build call arguments. IntentFuzz recovers the correct intent structure in 9/9 benchmark protocols and classifies deposit and fill functions with 100% recall and 82% combined precision; across a corpus of 77 manually labeled contracts, it reaches 79.5% bridge-classification precision and 97.2% recall, and among confirmed bridges, struct selection reaches 88.6% precision and recall while deposit and fill classification each reach 100% recall. On 23 planted-bug mutants, IntentFuzz attains 100% recall and 100% precision, executing 273 templates (507 transactions in a median of 14ms per template). Across 24 real-world deployments, it confirms 17 genuine invariant violations under heuristic-only input generation, rising to 22 with its LLM-assisted tier enabled, spanning eight vulnerable GitHub repositories, each finding reproducible against public, deployed bytecode.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Learning Interaction Kernels from Collective Steady States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12004)
*Baoli Hao,Mauro Maggioni,Ming Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从粒子系统集体行为的单快照观测中识别相互作用规律的学习方法，通过基于经验分布的正则化策略求解原本不适定的逆问题，并在多种稳态与准稳态模型中实现了对交互机制的稳定、准确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统辨识方法依赖对轨迹的观测，而在许多实际场景中仅能获得集体行为的单快照观测（不同、未观测的初始条件下生成的构型）。这种设定构成根本性的不适定逆问题，需要新的求解思路。

Method: 提出一种针对相互作用粒子系统进行系统辨识的学习流程，仅使用集体行为的单快照观测而非轨迹数据；采用基于观测构型经验分布的正则化策略来求解该不适定逆问题，其中观测构型来自不同的、未观测的初始条件。

Result: 在多种具有稳态和准稳态模式的代表性模型上验证，其中集体行为隐含了关于交互机制的信息；实验表明该方法能够稳定且准确地恢复底层相互作用规律，忠实复现集体行为，并且在许多情况下甚至能复现导致该行为的动力学过程。

Conclusion: 单快照观测虽导致不适定逆问题，但借助基于经验分布的正则化仍可有效辨识粒子系统的交互规律，为缺乏轨迹数据场景下的系统辨识提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a learning procedure for system identification in interacting particle systems from single-snapshot observations of collective behaviors, unlike existing approaches that rely on observations of trajectories. This setting leads to a fundamentally ill-posed inverse problem, which we solve by using a regularization strategy based on the empirical distribution of observed configurations, drawn from different, unobserved initial conditions. We test our learning procedure on a variety of representative models with steady-state and quasi-stationary patterns, where collective behaviors encode implicit information about the interaction mechanisms, demonstrating that our approach enables stable and accurate recovery of the underlying interaction laws, leading to faithful reproduction of the collective behavior, and in many cases even of the dynamics leading up to it.

</details>


### [176] [Membership Inference via Pairwise Likelihood Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12367)
*Shengjie Niu,Zebin Yun,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出PL-MIA方法，通过高斯似然比统计量、群体校准与Cauchy组合检验统一整合模型输出的统计信号，在低假阳性区间将TPR提升超过25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成员推断攻击（MIA）是审计机器学习模型隐私风险的标准工具，需依赖模型输出（置信度、logits、中间特征）暴露的统计信号。但现有方法难以高效地汇总与组合这些统计信号，导致攻击效力受限。

Method: 提出Pairwise Likelihood MIA（PL-MIA）：将高斯似然比（GLR）统计量与群体校准（population calibration）及Cauchy组合检验统一。理论上刻画GLR如何保留方差收缩信号，并建立群体校准与Cauchy组合提升攻击力的条件。对查询点与未参与训练的参考点进行成对比较获得p值，再用Cauchy组合检验聚合这些连续信号，保留被二值化投票所丢弃的证据强度。

Result: 大量实验表明PL-MIA优于强基线，在关键的低假阳性区间TPR提升超过25%，验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 统计原理可将噪声较大的模型输出转化为更强有力、经过校准且可复现的成员隐私审计证据。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are the standard tool for auditing the privacy risks of machine learning models. Given a query point, an MIA aims to determine whether that point was used to train the target model. In practice, such inference must rely on the statistical signals exposed by the model's outputs, such as confidence scores, logits, and intermediate feature representations. However, existing methods often fail to efficiently summarize and combine these statistical signals. To address this limitation, we propose Pairwise Likelihood MIA (PL-MIA), a unified method that combines a Gaussian likelihood-ratio (GLR) statistic with population calibration and the Cauchy combination test. We characterize theoretically how the GLR retains variance-contraction signals and establish conditions under which population calibration and Cauchy combination improve attack power. We obtain $p$-values from pairwise comparisons between the query point and reference points not used for training, and aggregate these continuous signals using the Cauchy combination test. This preserves the evidence strength that is discarded when each pairwise comparison is reduced to a binary vote. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PL-MIA outperforms strong baselines, improving the true positive rate (TPR) by over 25\% in the critical low-false-positive regime, corroborating our theoretical findings. These results demonstrate how statistical principles can turn noisy model outputs into more powerful, calibrated, and reproducible evidence for membership privacy auditing.

</details>


### [177] [Inference for Newton Methods with Accelerated Sketch-and-Project via Random Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12421)
*Xinchen Du,Elizaveta Rebrova,Michał Dereziński,Sen Na*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究基于广义加速草图投影求解器（GAS）的在线草图牛顿法，建立平均迭代的渐近正态性与泛函中心极限定理，实现无需显式协方差估计的在线推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二阶方法（牛顿法）存在计算瓶颈。草图牛顿法虽可缓解这一瓶颈，但既有的普通（未加速）草图投影求解器收敛速度有限，且缺乏对加速版本迭代量的统计推断理论，无法在在线场景下进行有效的置信区间构造与假设检验。

Method: 采用最先进的广义加速草图投影求解器（GAS）近似每步牛顿方向：通过 Nesterov 动量更新实现加速收敛，并引入灵活投影度量以进一步降低计算成本。理论分析上，推导平均草图牛顿迭代的渐近正态性并刻画其极限协方差矩阵；进一步建立牛顿迭代量的泛函中心极限定理，通过随机缩放构造枢轴检验统计量。

Result: 所得到的极限协方差矩阵在特定加速参数选择下可退化为未加速草图牛顿法的协方差；一般情形下在草图步数意义下更快收敛到极小极大最优协方差，且小于加速方法末次迭代的协方差。基于泛函中心极限定理构造的检验统计量其极限分布不含未知参数，可绕过显式协方差估计；数值实验证明所提推断方法性能优越。

Conclusion: 加速草图牛顿法在保持计算效率的同时具有更优的统计性质，其在线推断可通过随机缩放实现渐近有效且无需估计未知协方差，为大规模在线二阶优化提供了可行的推断框架。

Abstract: We study an online sketched Newton method that approximates the Newton direction at each step via a state-of-the-art sketching solver, called the generalized accelerated sketch-and-project solver (GAS), thereby mitigating the computational bottleneck of classical second-order methods. The GAS solver improves upon vanilla, unaccelerated sketch-and-project solvers by achieving accelerated convergence through Nesterov momentum updates, and accommodates a flexible projection metric whose proper choice further reduces computational cost. Building on this design, we establish asymptotic normality of the averaged sketched Newton iterates and characterize their limiting covariance matrix. The resulting covariance recovers that of the unaccelerated sketched Newton method under a specific choice of acceleration parameters, converges more rapidly (in the number of sketching steps) to the minimax-optimal covariance in general, and is smaller than that of the last iterate produced by the accelerated method. Finally, we strengthen these results by establishing a functional central limit theorem for the Newton iterates, which allows us to bypass explicit covariance estimation and develop an online inference procedure based on random scaling. Specifically, we construct a pivotal test statistic by appropriately rescaling the averaged iterates, so that its limiting distribution is free of any unknown parameters, enabling asymptotically valid online inference. Numerical experiments demonstrate superior performance of the proposed inference procedure.

</details>


### [178] [A Splitting Method for SDE Terminal-Law Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12513)
*Rushil Gupta,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在固定模拟预算下，用分裂路径树代替独立同分布路径采样来生成随机微分方程终端分布样本的可行性。以Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离为度量，推导了经验分布的极限误差，刻画了渐近最优分裂策略，实现在多种设置下平均误差较i.i.d.采样改善10-25%，在CIFAR-10上最大均值差异降低8-13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成AI等场景中，目标是从终端分布精确采样，通常通过生成独立同分布的扩散路径实现。在固定模拟预算约束下，通过适当分裂部分路径形成路径树可能提升效率，但引入的路径间相关性（依赖注入）是否损害精度尚不明确，因此需要系统研究这一问题。

Method: 以Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离作为精度度量，推导模拟预算趋于无穷时相关经验分布的极限误差；将分裂策略选择表述为渐近优化问题，刻画其最优解并揭示问题的内在结构；实现上分两阶段：初始估计阶段与最终推断阶段。

Result: 在多数设置下，相对独立同分布采样，平均误差改善10-25%；在探索性的CIFAR-10实验中，最大均值差异（MMD）降低8-13%。

Conclusion: 路径树分裂采样在引入相关性后仍可通过渐近最优分裂策略获得优于i.i.d.采样的效率，理论分析揭示了问题的优雅结构，并在合成与图像生成任务中获得实证验证。

Abstract: In many settings involving stochastic differential equations, including in diffusion based generative AI, our aim is to accurately generate samples from a terminal distribution. Typically, this is done by generating i.i.d. samples of diffusion paths. Given a fixed simulation budget, a reasonable way to gain efficiency may be to instead generate a tree of paths through appropriately split partial paths. This suggests improved performance, but one worries about the injected dependence. In this paper, we study this issue comprehensively. With Kolmogorov-Smirnov distance as a measure of accuracy, we identify the limiting errors of the associated empirical distributions as the simulation budget increases to infinity. We characterize a splitting strategy motivated by a corresponding asymptotic optimization problem. The theoretical results bring out the elegant underlying structure in the problem. Practical implementation involves two phases, an initial estimation phase and a final inference phase. Overall, we observe a 10-25% improvement in mean error over i.i.d. samples in many settings. In an exploratory CIFAR-10 study, our method reduces the maximum mean discrepancy by 8-13%.

</details>

### [179] [A Sharp Signal-to-Noise Threshold for Quasi-Maximum Likelihood Breakpoint Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12271)
*Hubeyb Gurdogan,Georg Menz*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为多元时间序列二阶矩结构中的主导断点建立了QML估计的严格路径式信噪比判据：当机制间对比度以显式因子超过机制内波动时估计量相合，低于阈值则全局恢复可能失败。路径式框架与对数行列式目标的岭正则可弱化相合性条件，并应用于维数随样本量发散的普遍因子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列二阶矩结构中断点的估计通常依赖对数据施加随机模型假设，且需对候选断点集进行修剪以处理端点边界层，条件难以放宽。本文旨在建立不依赖随机模型的路径式信噪比判据，明确相合与失效的阈值，并放宽相合性所需条件。

Method: 采用路径式（pathwise）框架，不对数据施加任何随机模型；对QML的对数行列式目标引入加性岭正则化，消除端点边界层，从而无需对候选集修剪，且通过调节岭参数弱化相合性条件；据此推导显式的信噪比阈值以判定相合与全局恢复失败。

Result: 得到尖锐的路径式信噪比判据：机制间对比度以显式因子超过机制内波动时QML估计量相合，低于该阈值时全局恢复可能失败。同时证明在维数随样本量发散的普遍因子模型中，对完全一般的误差项（无需独立、特异或随机），QML断点估计相合。

Conclusion: 该工作以路径式框架绕开随机模型假设，以岭正则在去除修剪需求的同时弱化相合条件，给出了断点QML估计相合的清晰阈值，并将结果推广至维数发散的普遍因子模型与一般误差结构。

Abstract: We establish a sharp pathwise signal-to-noise criterion for quasi-maximum-likelihood (QML) estimation of a dominant breakpoint in the second-moment structure of a multivariate time series: the QML estimator is consistent whenever the between-regime contrast exceeds the within-regime fluctuation by an explicit factor, and below this threshold global recovery can fail. Two innovations drive the result. First, the framework is pathwise: no stochastic model is imposed on the data. Second, the log-determinant objective carries an additive ridge regularization: it removes the endpoint boundary layers, so no trimming of the candidate set is required, and tuning the ridge weakens the consistency condition. As an application of the main theorem, we establish consistency of QML breakpoint estimation in pervasive factor models whose dimension diverges with the sample size, for completely general error terms -- not necessarily independent, idiosyncratic, or even random.

</details>


### [180] [PDE-constrained inverse problems at the $\sqrt{n}$ rate via debiased physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12301)
*Yves Atchade,Debarghya Mukherjee*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 论文针对PINN求解PDE约束反问题中参数估计收敛慢、推断有偏的问题，提出基于影响函数的去偏估计与去偏拟似然贝叶斯推断，实现√n一致渐近正态且无需欠平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在PDE约束反问题中虽经验成功，但估计量继承了神经网络解的非参数慢收敛率，导致对有限维目标参数的推断有偏且统计效率低。需在不对神经网络组件做欠平滑的前提下获得有效统计推断。

Method: 提出两步去偏估计：先以PINN做非参数估计，再用影响函数（influence function）做偏差校正，消除估计量对冗余函数误差的一阶敏感性；进一步用去偏拟似然替代原似然进行贝叶斯推断。作为副产品，建立了神经网络在Sobolev空间中估计非参数回归函数及其导数的近似极小极大最优收敛率。

Result: 所提估计量达到√n一致且渐近正态，无需对神经网络欠平滑；贝叶斯版本满足Bernstein-von Mises定理，后验收缩率为√n且渐近协方差与频率派估计量一致。数值实验验证了理论结果，并显示去偏步骤对PDE约束反问题中有效统计推断的必要性。

Conclusion: 去偏程序可在PINN框架下实现高效、有效的参数推断，兼顾非参数估计的灵活性与√n参数收敛率，为PDE约束反问题提供了统计上可靠的频率派与贝叶斯推断途径。

Abstract: We study the problem of estimating unknown parameters in PDE-constrained inverse problems from noisy observations, where the PDE solution is approximated using Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs). While PINNs have demonstrated remarkable empirical success, existing estimators often inherit the slow nonparametric convergence rate of the neural-network solution, leading to biased and statistically inefficient inference for the finite-dimensional parameters of interest. To address this, we propose a two-step debiased estimation procedure that combines neural-network-based nonparametric estimation with an influence-function-based bias correction. By eliminating the first-order sensitivity of the estimator to errors in the nuisance function, our procedure yields a $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal estimator without requiring undersmoothing of the neural network component. We further extend this framework to Bayesian inference by replacing the original likelihood with a debiased quasi-likelihood and establish a Bernstein-von Mises theorem showing that the resulting posterior contracts at the $\sqrt{n}$-rate with an asymptotic covariance matching that of the frequentist estimator. As a by-product of our analysis, we establish near-minimax optimal convergence rates for estimating a nonparametric regression function and its derivatives in Sobolev spaces using neural networks. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate our theoretical findings and demonstrate the necessity of the proposed debiasing procedure for valid statistical inference in PDE-constrained inverse problems.

</details>


### [181] [Tight Sampling Complexity with stochastic gradient oracles in Fixed Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12590)
*Weiming Ou,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在任意固定欧几里得维度下，采样光滑强对数凹分布的随机梯度查询复杂度，并给出了关于条件数和精度同时紧的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机梯度oracle下采样光滑强对数凹分布的查询复杂度是采样理论中的核心问题，已有结果往往未能在条件数与精度上同时达到紧性，因此需要建立统一且紧的复杂度刻画。

Method: 考虑势函数为μ-强凸且L-光滑、众数位于原点半径μ^{-1/2}球内的设定，访问方差不超过σ^2的无偏随机oracle；针对任意σ^2≥0与TV精度0<ε≤1/10，通过上下界匹配分析证明采样到ε-TV距离内分布的紧复杂度。

Result: 证明紧复杂度为N*_TV=Θ(log(1+κ)+σ^2/(με))，其中κ=L/μ为条件数；该界关于条件数κ与精度ε同时紧，并自适应退化为无噪声情形σ=0时的N*_TV=Θ(log(1+κ))。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了随机梯度采样光滑强对数凹分布的TV精度下查询复杂度的紧刻画，统一了条件数依赖与噪声-精度依赖，并自然涵盖无噪声极限。

Abstract: We investigate the stochastic-gradient query complexity of sampling smooth strongly log-concave distributions in any fixed Euclidean dimension. The potential is $μ$-strongly convex and $L$-smooth, with an unknown mode in the ball of radius $μ^{-1/2}$ about the origin. We have access to unbiased stochastic oracles with the variance at most $σ^2$. For every $σ^2\ge0$ and total variation (TV) accuracy $0<\varepsilon\le1/10$, we prove that the tight complexity of sampling a distribution within $ε$-TV distance from the target distribution is \[
  N^\star_{\text{TV}}=Θ\!\left(\log(1+κ)+
  \frac{σ^2}{με}\right), \] where $κ:=\frac Lμ$ is the condition number.
  Note that this complexity bound is simultaneously tight for the condition number $κ$ and accuracy $ε$. Besides, our tight complexity bound is adaptive to noiseless setting $σ=0$, which is $ N^\star_{\text{TV}}=Θ\!\left(\log(1+κ)\right)$.

</details>


### [182] [Closed-form estimation of the exponential scale under two-sided informative random censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12601)
*Dilshod R. Mansurov,Sukhrob B. Bozorov,Azizbek B. Oltiyev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文在双侧随机删失、且删失律为生存函数幂（Koziol–Green比例风险模型的双侧推广）的信息性模型下估计指数寿命的尺度参数，提出闭式伪极大似然估计量，无需迭代与数值优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双侧删失估计通常依赖删失深度受限或删失机制可忽略的假设；本文希望在没有删失深度限制的信息性删失模型下构造既Fisher一致又强一致、且渐近正态的尺度估计量。

Method: 将经验分布函数代入似然方程，得到闭式伪极大似然估计量，仅需对次序统计量作一遍扫描；利用digamma恒等式证明估计方程精确（而非仅渐近）Fisher一致；通过一个恒等式重写底层L统计量以去除无界得分函数，从而在无删失深度限制下证明强相合性与渐近正态性；给出由多伽马函数显式表达的影响函数与渐近方差。

Result: 在考察的设计中效率超过98.6%，在广泛扫描范围内保持高于96.2%；置信区间无需数值积分；估计量仅需模型入口半部分，即无论右删失律为何都保持精确Fisher一致与强相合，并证明指数性正是这一自由度的精确代价；构造可径直推广至已知基线的比例风险类；三个数据集（含一个真实左删失队列）验证了方法。

Conclusion: 该闭式伪极大似然估计在双侧信息性删失下实现精确Fisher一致、强相合、渐近正态且接近信息界的高效率，且对右删失律具有稳健性、可推广至已知基线的比例风险类。

Abstract: We estimate the scale of an exponential lifetime from observations censored randomly from both sides, under an informative model in which each censoring law is a power of the lifetime survival function -- a two-sided generalization of the Koziol--Green proportional hazards model. Substituting the empirical distribution function into the likelihood equation yields a closed-form pseudo-maximum-likelihood estimator of the scale that is computed in one pass over the order statistics and needs neither iteration nor numerical optimization. A digamma identity shows the estimating equation to be \emph{exactly}, not merely asymptotically, Fisher-consistent. Strong consistency and asymptotic normality are established with \emph{no restriction on the censoring depth}; the key is an identity that rewrites the underlying $L$-statistic without its unbounded score function and so removes the condition that classical limit theorems would impose. The influence function and the asymptotic variance are explicit in polygamma functions, so confidence intervals require no numerical integration. Against the information bound of the observed-data model the efficiency exceeds $98.6\%$ in the designs considered and stays above $96.2\%$ over a wide sweep. Only the entry half of the model is needed: the estimator remains exactly Fisher-consistent and strongly consistent whatever the right-censoring law, and exponentiality is shown to be the precise price of that freedom. The construction extends verbatim to a proportional hazards class with known baseline. Three data sets illustrate the procedure, including a cohort in which left censoring is genuine.

</details>


### [183] [Efficiency Optimality without Pathwise Differentiability: A Variational Theory for Marginal-Integral Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12707)
*Shuoxun Xu,Xinzhou Guo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将半参数有效性问题重构为变分问题：在稳健无偏约束下最小化估计函数的方差，针对边际积分泛函（不要求路径可微）发展了该理论，并将条件方差最小化刻画为方差下确界，对处理特异条件均值仿射函数的最大值给出了显式最优权重与交叉拟合估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典半参数效率理论依赖路径可微性，难以处理非光滑（如最大值、上确界型）目标泛函；同时实际推断中通常需要估计函数对单个干扰成分误设定保持稳健（稳健无偏性），而现有理论对这一稳健性与最优效率之间的关系刻画不足。

Method: 将最优效率及其可达性问题形式化为变分问题：在稳健无偏性约束（任一指定干扰成分误设、其余正确时期望仍等于目标）下最小化估计函数方差；针对边际积分泛函，无需路径可微性，通过条件方差最小化刻画方差下确界；对处理特异条件均值仿射函数的最大值，推导显式最优权重并构造交叉拟合估计量；对基于联合观测量的类似最大值，最优权重使用完整条件协方差矩阵；并给出方差界与经典卷积界一致的条件。

Result: 给出了仿射函数最大值情形的最优权重解析解及在干扰估计与近似并列概率条件下的交叉拟合估计量；联合观测量情形的最优权重由完整条件协方差矩阵给出；识别出方差界与保持并列（一阶）参数扰动卷积界一致的条件；应用实例涵盖最优策略价值、L^1 校准误差、Balke--Pearl 界与中介参数。在 NLSY 青年男性数据应用中，协方差加权相较等权使交叉拟合估计函数方差中位数在下 Balke--Pearl 端点降低 12.9%、上端点降低 16.5%，且全部 20 次重复交叉拟合划分均有改善。

Conclusion: 该变分视角为半参数效率提供了不依赖路径可微性的统一框架，稳健无偏约束下的条件方差最小化可导出最优权重与可达效率界；协方差加权在实践中对非光滑泛函（如 Balke--Pearl 界）显著优于等权，验证了理论的实用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a new perspective on semiparametric efficiency theory. In particular, we reformulate the questions of optimal efficiency and its attainment as a variational problem: minimize variance over estimating functions subject to robust unbiasedness constraints. We develop this theory for marginal-integral functionals without requiring pathwise differentiability. We study estimating functions whose expectations remain equal to the target when any one specified nuisance component is misspecified and the others are correct. We characterize the infimum of their variances through conditional variance minimization. For maxima of affine functions of treatment-specific conditional means, we obtain explicit optimal weights and construct cross-fitted estimators that attain the bound under conditions on nuisance estimation and the probability of near ties. For analogous maxima based on jointly observed quantities, the optimal weights use the full conditional covariance matrix. We also identify conditions under which the variance bound agrees with a classical convolution bound for parametric perturbations that preserve ties to first order. Examples include optimal policy values, $L^1$ calibration error, Balke--Pearl bounds, and mediation parameters. In an application to the National Longitudinal Survey of Young Men, covariance weighting reduces the median estimated variance of the cross-fitted estimating function relative to equal weighting by 12.9% for the lower Balke--Pearl endpoint and 16.5% for the upper, with improvements in all 20 repeated cross-fitting splits.

</details>


### [184] [A class of nonparametric homogeneity tests on the circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13019)
*Alberto Fernández-de-Marcos,Eduardo García-Portugués*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文构建圆圈上一元 $c$-样本齐性检验的统一框架，提出 $c$-样本 Sobolev 检验，并嵌入一般合并函数的聚合框架，涵盖均值型、最大值型及插值族，且无需重采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有圆圈上多样本齐性检验方法较为分散，缺乏统一构造与分布理论；且缺少针对圆形数据的多模态偏离检验及 Anderson-Darling 型检验。

Method: 提出 $c$-样本 Sobolev 检验类，推广基于均匀得分的两样本 Sobolev 检验，并将样本级 Sobolev 分量通过一般合并函数聚合；引入首个圆形数据 Anderson-Darling 型齐性检验，以及基于 softmax 核和 Poisson 核的两个多模态检验。推导零分布渐近分布，证明对广泛固定备择的一致性，并得到移位型局部备择下的渐近分布。

Result: 检验为分布自由，无需重采样。模拟显示 Anderson-Darling 型检验在多种情形下优于 Cramér-von Mises 型竞争者，softmax 与 Poisson 检验在若干备择下有效。工具箱应用于北极熊哺乳模式分析。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为圆形 $c$-样本齐性检验提供了灵活且理论完备的工具，兼具高功效与无需重采样的实用性。

Abstract: We develop a unified framework for $c$-sample homogeneity testing on the circle. The proposed class of $c$-sample Sobolev tests generalizes the two-sample Sobolev tests based on uniform scores and encompasses the existing multisample tests as particular cases. We further embed this class into a broader aggregation framework, where the samplewise Sobolev components are combined through general merging functions, including average-type, maximum-type, and interpolating families. Within this class, we introduce the first Anderson-Darling-type homogeneity test for circular data, and we further propose two new tests designed to detect multimodal departures from homogeneity, constructed from softmax and Poisson kernels. We derive the asymptotic null distribution of the class, prove its consistency against a broad family of fixed alternatives, and obtain the asymptotic distribution under shift-type local alternatives. The tests are distribution-free and therefore do not require resampling. A comprehensive simulation study demonstrates the strong power of the Anderson-Darling-type test compared with Cramér-von Mises-type competitors across several scenarios, and the effectiveness of the softmax and Poisson tests under several alternatives. The testing toolbox is applied to analyze the nursing patterns of polar bears.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Fundamental Dynamical Units for Physics-Informed Structural Inference from Perturbation Time-Series in Networked Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11934)
*Nima Nouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基本动力学单元（FDU）——以带符号的三节点交互模式作为可组合原语，将交互假设空间转化为有限、可构造且可处理的表示，并嵌入物理信息神经常微分方程（ODE）框架，实现交互结构与扰动解析轨迹的联合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络动力系统中，符号化的交互结构是最核心的机制参数，但从扰动时间序列中恢复该结构面临三大耦合障碍：交互架构的组合复杂性、有限干预下的因果归因歧义，以及状态依赖动力学对结构推断的干扰。作者认为这些障碍本质上是结构性的，因而需要结构性解决方案。

Method: 采用还原论思路，引入基本动力学单元（FDU），即带符号的三节点交互模式，作为可组合原语，将交互假设空间转化为有限、可构造、可处理的表示；并证明局部交互结构决定了区分直接影响与中继影响所需的扰动条件，使干预设计成为 FDU 表示的结构性推论。进一步将 FDU 正则化的结构推断嵌入物理信息神经常微分方程，以控制方程约束把结构假设转化为可验证的动力学预测。

Result: 在具有已知真值的合成基准上验证，该框架可联合恢复交互结构与扰动解析轨迹，并支持结构承诺（通过 FDU 原语表达）、基序规定的干预设计以及物理信息学习。

Conclusion: 结构承诺——以 FDU 原语、基序驱动的干预设计与物理信息学习为体现——可作为网络动力系统中机制可解释推断的原则性基础。

Abstract: In networked dynamical systems, the parameter of primary mechanistic interest is signed interaction structure. Recovering this structure from perturbation time-series data is a fundamental identification problem, compounded by three coupled obstacles: the combinatorial complexity of interaction architectures, ambiguity of causal attribution under limited interventions, and state-dependent dynamics that confound structural inference. Each obstacle is structural in origin and calls for a structural solution. We address these challenges by adopting a reductionist approach, introducing Fundamental Dynamical Units (FDUs): signed three-node interaction patterns as composable primitives that convert the interaction hypothesis space into a finite, constructive, and tractable representation. We show that local interaction structure determines the perturbation conditions required to disentangle direct from relayed influence, making intervention design a structural consequence of the FDU representation. We embed FDU-regularized structural inference within a physics-informed neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) whose governing-equation constraint transforms structural hypotheses into verifiable dynamical predictions, enabling joint recovery of interaction structure and perturbation-resolved trajectories. Validated on synthetic benchmarks with known ground truth, the framework supports structural commitment, expressed through FDU primitives, motif-prescribed intervention design, and physics-informed learning, as a principled basis for mechanistically interpretable inference in networked dynamical systems.

</details>


### [186] [Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction: Embedding PDE Consistency into Distribution-Free Uncertainty Quantification for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11935)
*Michael Chin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PI-CP框架，将PDE残差嵌入分裂共形预测的非一致性评分，获得分布无关且具覆盖保证的不确定性区间；并揭示FNO平移等变性对Dirichlet边界条件的近似瓶颈，坐标通道可缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子（如FNO）在求解PDE时精度高，但缺乏严格的、有理论保证的不确定性量化方法，这是当前未解决的关键挑战。此外，FNO架构本身的对称性可能导致对特定边界条件问题的近似障碍。

Method: 提出Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction（PI-CP）：把PDE残差融入分裂共形预测（split conformal prediction）的非一致性评分，从而生成预测区间；理论上证明FNO的平移等变性对带Dirichlet边界条件的PDE构成基本近似障碍，并引入坐标通道（coordinate channels）加以解决；在六类物理场景（2D/3D热传导、2D/3D结构力学、Darcy流、Navier-Stokes）上验证。

Result: PI-CP对四种共形方法均给出一致的89-91%覆盖率，而MC Dropout与Deep Ensembles不稳定（82-100%）；预测区间在物理满足良好处更紧、被违反处更宽，具空间自适应性；坐标通道带来最高63倍误差降低；FNO较CNN和DeepONet精度高出10-12倍。

Conclusion: PI-CP提供分布无关、可证明覆盖且空间自适应于物理残差的不确定性估计，适用于多种PDE场景；同时表明坐标通道能有效突破FNO在Dirichlet边界下的平移等变性近似瓶颈，FNO在多类物理任务中显著优于CNN与DeepONet。

Abstract: Neural operators such as the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) achieve remarkable accuracy in approximating solutions to partial differential equations (PDEs). However, providing rigorous uncertainty estimates remains an open challenge. We propose Physics-Informed Conformal Prediction (PI-CP), a framework that embeds PDE residuals into the nonconformity score of split conformal prediction, producing prediction intervals that are (i) distribution-free with provable coverage guarantees, and (ii) spatially adaptive when the PDE residual correlates with prediction error -- tighter where physics is well-satisfied, wider where it is violated. Additionally, we prove that FNO's translation equivariance creates a fundamental approximation barrier for PDEs with Dirichlet boundary conditions, and show that coordinate channels resolve this with up to 63x error reduction. We validate PI-CP across six physics scenarios -- heat conduction (2D/3D), structural mechanics (2D/3D), Darcy flow, and Navier-Stokes -- demonstrating consistent 89-91% coverage for all four Conformal methods, while MC Dropout and Deep Ensembles are unstable (82-100%). FNO outperforms CNN and DeepONet by 10-12x.

</details>


### [187] [Fed-Equilibrium Framework for Topological Pareto Control in Robust and Fair Clinical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11937)
*Ting Xu,Henry Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fed-Equilibrium框架，通过两阶段梯度控制级联（余弦相似度漏斗滤除恶意噪声 + Pareto拐点调制已验证贡献），将联邦学习从"防御"推向"拓扑均衡"，在加拿大CNODES与美国SyntheticMass的双国仿真中，使占比不足3%的少数节点达到与数据丰富中心相当的深度收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在多中心临床网络部署时面临"知识支配"问题：高数据量中心节点天然压制少数群体节点，将其独特临床模式隐含地视为异常值。现有几何防御虽提供安全基线，但未能解决效率—公平性困境。

Method: Fed-Equilibrium采用顺序架构协同的两阶段梯度控制级联。阶段I（几何质量保证）：通过余弦相似度漏斗强制方向一致性，过滤恶意噪声，构建稳定流形；阶段II（拓扑Pareto控制）：通过识别最优Pareto拐点，主动调制已验证的贡献。

Result: 在整合加拿大CNODES与美国SyntheticMass登记库的双国仿真中，系统在抵御对抗性发散的同时容纳了代表性不足的信号；代表数据量不足3%的少数美国分支节点实现了与数据丰富的加拿大中心节点相当的深度收敛。

Conclusion: Fed-Equilibrium有效对抗"知识支配"，建立起真正的"知识共享池"（knowledge commons），使全局泛化能力不以牺牲本地临床代表性为代价。

Abstract: The deployment of Federated Learning (FL) in multi-center clinical networks faces the challenge of "knowledge dominance," where high-volume hubs naturally overwhelm minority community nodes, implicitly treating the distinct clinical patterns of smaller cohorts as outliers. Existing geometric defenses provide a security baseline but leave this efficiency-fairness dilemma unresolved. To bridge this gap, we propose Fed-Equilibrium, a framework that advances the paradigm from simple defense to topological equilibrium. Unlike traditional aggregators, Fed-Equilibrium implements a sequential architectural synergy. It utilizes a two-stage gradient control cascade: Stage I (geometric quality assurance) enforces directional consistency via a cosine similarity funnel to filter malicious noise, creating a stabilized manifold; Stage II (topological Pareto control) then actively modulates verified contributions by identifying the optimal Pareto knee point. We validated this framework on a bi-national simulation integrating Canadian (CNODES) and U.S. (SyntheticMass) registries. Experimental results demonstrate that the system simultaneously secures the network against adversarial divergence while accommodating underrepresented signals. Notably, the minority U.S. spoke (representing less than 3% of data volume) achieved deep convergence comparable to the data-rich Canadian hub. This confirms that Fed-Equilibrium effectively counters "knowledge dominance," establishing a true "knowledge commons" where global generalizability does not come at the cost of local clinical representation.

</details>


### [188] [Efficient AI Model Deployment Using Quantization Analysis Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11954)
*Dwith Chenna,Kanishka Macherla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Quantization Analysis Tool，一个基于 ONNX 的实用量化分析系统，提供逐层敏感度分析、权重/激活分布可视化与精度选择指导，帮助开发者在模型大小、延迟与精度间权衡，实验表明其能提升量化精度并支持高效边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型日益部署于资源受限设备，边缘与低功耗平台的有效部署需要在不显著损失精度的前提下降低模型规模与计算开销，因此需要高效的模型优化（量化）技术及配套分析工具。

Method: 构建 Quantization Analysis Tool：基于 ONNX 框架以实现广泛互操作性；提供逐层敏感度分析、权重与激活分布可视化，以及面向精度选择的分析洞见，识别对低精度敏感或鲁棒的层，辅助开发者进行使模型大小、延迟、精度之间的取舍决策。

Result: 在多种神经网络架构上的实验评估表明，该工具有效提升量化后的精度，并在真实部署场景中带来效率改进；同时为开发者提供关于量化对模型及其精度影响的有价值洞见。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了该工具的能力与实际应用，以及其通过稳健量化分析支持高效 AI 模型部署的作用。

Abstract: As deep learning models are increasingly deployed on resource constrained devices, the demand for efficient model optimization techniques continues to grow. Effective deployment of AI models on edge and low power platforms requires optimization methods that reduce model size and computational cost while maintaining high accuracy. This paper presents Quantization Analysis Tool, a practical system designed to streamline quantization workflows and support performance efficient model deployment. Built on the ONNX framework for broad interoperability, the tool provides detailed layer-wise sensitivity analysis, visualization of weight and activation distributions, and insights to guide precision selection. By identifying layers that are resilient or sensitive to reduced precision, the tool enables developers to make informed trade-offs between model size, latency, and accuracy. Experimental evaluations across multiple neural network architectures demonstrate that the tool effectively improves the quantized accuracy, leading to improved efficiency in real-world deployment scenarios. The tool also provides developers valuable insights into the effects on quantization on the model and its accuracy. This work highlights the tools capabilities, practical applications, and its role in enabling efficient AI model deployment through robust quantization analysis

</details>


### [189] [Performance, Efficiency and Collapse -- Advantages and Challenges in Offline Post-training of Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11956)
*Abhinav Anand,Sanjana Reddy Pachika,Shweta Verma,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文探讨能否完全离线地进行基于强化学习的代码生成LLM后训练，而非依赖在线采样，从而降低算力与通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RL的后训练对代码生成LLM保证指令遵循和功能正确性至关重要，但通常需要大量代码样本生成和可观的GPU-CPU通信用于序列验证，计算成本高昂。

Method: 利用已有数据集而非生成新样本，进行完全离线的RL后训练。

Result: 仅需数小时训练，即可在无在线采样的情况下显著提升LLM的零样本代码生成性能；离线RL在0.5B至7B参数的模型上均带来性能增益，但提升幅度因模型家族而异。

Conclusion: 离线RL后训练无需在线采样即可有效提升代码生成能力，为降低RL后训练计算成本提供了可行路径，但效果存在模型家族差异。

Abstract: Post-training with reinforcement learning (RL) is a critical phase in the development of code-generating large language models (LLMs), as it ensures adherence to instructions and the production of functionally correct code. This process typically requires computationally intensive code sample generation from Transformer-based LLMs and substantial GPU-CPU communication for sequence verification. To address these computational challenges, this work examines whether RL-based post-training can be performed entirely offline by leveraging existing datasets rather than generating new samples. The findings indicate that, with only a few hours of training, zero-shot code generation performance of LLMs can be substantially improved without online sampling. Additionally, offline RL produces performance gains across models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters, although the extent of improvement varies among model families.

</details>


### [190] [Look Before You Leap: Pre-Action Verification for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11957)
*Asaad Althoubi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在动作执行前进行廉价的确定性校验，作为LLM智能体监督的有效手段，并在shell命令与代码编辑两种动作模态上验证：静态验证器可高召回、低误报地捕获无效命令；内容锚定编辑格式可安全失败，而位置锚定格式会静默失败；采用“不确定时拒答”策略可将静默失败转为可恢复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过发出动作（如shell命令、代码编辑）作用于世界，错误的动作不一定显式失败，可能产生貌似合理但实际错误的效果且不报错。因此需要一种廉价、确定性的执行前检查机制，以避免静默失败，并允许验证器在不确定时弃权而非猜测。

Method: 论文在一个统一框架下跨两种动作模态研究执行前确定性校验。核心思想是在任何执行器运行前，通过构造方式固定动作的正确效果，从而直接度量静默失败。对shell命令，构建静态验证器，包含语法检查、二进制检查与标志检查，在9930条命令、482个工具上评估。对代码编辑，构建包含224个文件上640次编辑的基准，隔离apply步骤，对比内容锚定格式（search/replace、diff）与位置锚定格式（行号、函数名）的失败行为，并提出选择性grounding与anchor-and-verify应用器、refuse-when-unsure策略。

Result: shell命令静态验证器在10.0%误报率下捕获95.8%的无效命令；语法与二进制检查达到oracle-exact，零误报并捕获一半错误；标志检查受help-text覆盖率限制且贡献全部误报。代码编辑方面，内容锚定格式干净失败，位置锚定格式静默失败：一行偏移使99.1%文件被行号编辑破坏，函数名编辑12.7%命中错误函数。拒绝不确定策略将静默失败转为可恢复失败：选择性grounding达0.958召回、7.0%误报；anchor-and-verify应用器在8320次试验中仅1次静默误用（0.01%）。论文发布了两个基准、验证器和防护器。

Conclusion: 执行前廉价确定性检查是有效且尚未充分利用的智能体监督形式。通过构造性固定动作正确效果，可度量并缓解静默失败；内容锚定编辑天然更安全，而位置锚定格式需要额外验证；refuse-when-unsure策略以可调适用性成本换取静默失败的可恢复性。

Abstract: An LLM agent acts on the world by emitting actions: shell commands to run, edits to apply. A wrong action does not always fail loudly; it can fail silently, producing a plausible but incorrect effect that raises no error. We argue that a cheap deterministic check, run before an action takes effect, is an effective and underused form of agent oversight, and we study it across two action modalities in one framework. The idea is to fix an action's correct effect by construction, before any executor runs, so that silent failure is measured directly and the verifier may abstain rather than guess. For shell commands, a static verifier over 9930 commands and 482 tools catches 95.8% of invalid commands at a 10.0% false-positive rate. Its syntax and binary checks are oracle-exact, giving zero false positives while catching half of all errors; the flag check is bounded only by help-text coverage and accounts for every false positive. For code edits, a benchmark of 640 edits over 224 files isolating the apply step exposes a sharp split. Content-anchored formats such as search/replace and diff fail cleanly, whereas location-anchored formats fail silently: line numbers corrupt 99.1% of files under a one-line shift, and function-name edits hit the wrong function 12.7% of the time. In both settings a refuse-when-unsure policy turns silent failures into recoverable ones at a tunable cost in applicability: selective grounding reaches 0.958 recall at 7.0% false positives, and an anchor-and-verify applier records one silent misapplication in 8320 trials (0.01%). We release both benchmarks, the verifiers, and the guards.

</details>


### [191] [Decoding Mixture Perception through Computational Modeling of Component Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11958)
*Fei Wang,Xiaoya Xie,Junfei Liu,Huihao Wang,Yixiao Wang,Yintao Wang,Yi Li,Hao Dong,Xing Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种仿生深度学习框架，用于多分子混合物的嗅觉感知识别，通过构建分子-受体神经响应曲线并融合注意力加权的多受体与浓度依赖多分子曲线，实现从化学混合到感知形成的完整计算路径，识别准确率达92.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嗅觉在人类进化与文明中不可或缺，至今仍是危险判别、情绪体验与记忆形成的重要通道。然而自然界中大多数物质以多分子混合物形式存在，混合物成分复杂，加上浓度依赖的饱和效应与受体特异性激活阈值，使得嗅觉特征识别面临巨大挑战。

Method: 提出一种受生物启发的深度学习框架：稳健构建分子-受体相互作用的神经响应曲线；设计融合策略，将注意力加权的多受体曲线与浓度依赖的多分子曲线相结合，模拟混合物成分的竞争性激活与协同整合；通过比较响应曲线模式的一致性，将分子关联的语义丰富空间知识迁移至混合物感知特征识别，从而建立从化学混合、神经编码到感知形成的完整计算路径。

Result: 综合评估结果显示该框架具有显著优越性，识别准确率达到92.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作为长期存在的混合物感知难题提供了可泛化的解决方案，并可集成到具身认知系统中，增强智能体在复杂场景下的感知与交互能力。

Abstract: Olfaction played an indispensable role throughout human evolution and civilization. Even in the contemporary era of advanced technology, olfaction remains a critical channel for person to conduct danger discrimination, emotional experience, and memory formation. However, most substances in nature exist as multi-molecule mixtures. The complexity of mixture compositions, as well as concentration dependent saturation effects and receptor specific activation thresholds, pose substantial challenges in identifying olfactory characteristics. In this study, we proposed a novel bio inspired deep learning framework for accurate odor perception recognition of mixtures. We robustly constructed neural response curves for molecule-receptor interactions, and developed a fusion strategy that integrates attention-weighted multi-receptor curves with concentration-dependent multi-molecule curves, replicating the competitive activation and synergistic integration of mixture components. Furthermore, by comparing the consistency of response curve patterns, the model can transfer knowledge from the semantically rich space of molecular associations to guide recognition of mixture perception characteristics. Therefore, we established a complete computational pathway from chemical blending, neural encoding, to perceptual formation. Finally, we conducted comprehensive evaluation, and results demonstrated exceptional superiority, achieving an accuracy of 92.2%. Consequently, our work provides a generalizable solution to the long standing mixture perception challenge. More importantly, it can be integrated into embodied cognitive systems to enhance the agents perceptual and interactive capabilities in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [Space as an Interventional Invariant: Cross-Modal Predictive Geometry for Stratified Cities and Em-Spaced Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11959)
*Tao Yang,Xuhui Lin,Kunyao Li,Haijiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将“空间”定义为干预不变结构（interventional invariant），提出跨模态预测几何框架，并用层论表示扩展到分层城市系统，理论上证明在联合点分离、等变性与干预忠实性下隐空间可辨识至干预群中心化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学、物理、空间认知、城市科学与具身智能等领域对空间结构的处理存在分歧：或视为共享几何容器，或视为互不相连的表征集合。这难以解释异质感官与城市过程如何在度量或表征不共享时共同揭示同一空间结构。

Method: 定义空间为干预不变：在可容许动作下保持局部兼容性与未来观测条件律的最小关系结构。构建跨模态预测几何，整合局部状态空间、模态特定观测映射、动作群胚与规范预测状态商，并给出识别干预结构而非仅观测结构的因果条件。进一步用层值表示扩展到分层城市系统，使几何、物理、移动性、社会与经济层共存而不归约为单一度量。

Result: 核心理论结果为：在联合点分离、等变性与干预忠实性条件下，隐空间可辨识至干预群的中心化子，从而将表征歧义归约为残余坐标自由度。在含噪合成实验中评估了等变性、预测充分性、完整性、限制映射恢复、跨尺度一致性与语境饱和等性质。

Conclusion: 该框架为空间认知、城市科学、具身AI与em-spaced intelligence提供了统一且可证伪的基础。

Abstract: Space is a foundational concept across mathematics, physics, spatial cognition, urban science, and embodied intelligence, yet these fields often treat spatial structure either as a shared geometric container or as a collection of disconnected representations. Such approaches struggle to explain how heterogeneous sensory and urban processes can jointly reveal a common spatial structure, particularly when different modalities do not share the same metric or representation. This paper addresses this gap by defining space as an interventional invariant: the minimal relational structure that preserves local compatibility and the conditional laws of future observations under admissible actions. We develop a cross-modal predictive geometry that integrates local state spaces, modality-specific observation maps, an action groupoid, and a canonical predictive-state quotient, with explicit causal conditions for identifying interventional rather than merely observational structure. The key theoretical result shows that, under joint point separation, equivariance, and interventional faithfulness, the latent space is identifiable up to the centraliser of the intervention group, thereby reducing representational ambiguity to residual coordinate freedom. The framework is further extended to stratified urban systems using sheaf-valued representations, allowing geometric, physical, mobility, social, and economic layers to coexist without being reduced to a single metric. Synthetic experiments under noise evaluate equivariance, predictive sufficiency, holonomy, restriction-map recovery, cross-scale consistency, and context saturation. The resulting framework provides a unified and falsifiable foundation for spatial cognition, urban science, embodied AI, and em-spaced intelligence.

</details>


### [193] [On-Device Language Models for Privacy-Preserving Stress Prediction: A Multimodal Evaluation on Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11961)
*Ibukunoluwa Soyebo,Alyssa Donawa,Rodrigo Aguilar Barrios,Brice Patchou,Corey E. Baker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估端侧语言模型（ODLM）在多模态压力预测中的可行性：零样本提示下，2B以下轻量模型可实现低延迟、可预测资源开销，客观传感器特征平均略优于主观自评。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压力是心理健康的关键决定因素与移动健康干预靶点；端侧语言模型可提供无需云端的隐私保护推理，但其在移动资源约束下用于健康预测的可行性尚缺乏研究。

Method: 采用零样本提示（zero-shot prompting）对端侧语言模型进行多模态压力预测评估，同时测量预测准确率、延迟与吞吐量，并比较客观传感器特征与主观自评报告的表现。

Result: 客观传感器特征平均略优于主观自评；轻量级（<2B参数）模型实现低延迟且资源占用可预测。

Conclusion: 研究同时揭示了端侧语言模型用于移动心理健康的潜力与切实存在的资源约束。

Abstract: Stress is a pervasive determinant of mental health and a key target for mobile health interventions. On-device language models (ODLMs) offer privacy-preserving inference without cloud dependency, yet their feasibility for health prediction under mobile resource constraints remains underexplored. We evaluate ODLMs for multi-modal stress prediction using zero-shot prompting, measuring predictive accuracy alongside latency and throughput. Our results show that objective sensor features marginally outperform subjective self-reports on average, and that lightweight sub-2B models achieve low latency with predictable resource usage. Our findings highlight both the promise and the practical constraints of ODLMs for mobile mental health.

</details>


### [194] [FINESSE: An Agent-Based Simulator and Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Financial Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11993)
*Tyler Farnan,Benjamin Eng,Adam Abate,Xirui Hou,Rizal Fathony,Nam H. Nguyen,Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FINESSE——一个面向金融服务的事件序列仿真环境，可生成多流耦合的合成数据集，并发布配套基准FINESSE-Bench及四类任务的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务领域的机器学习研究受限于代表性开源数据集的稀缺性；现有资源多局限于单一模态或单一任务，难以体现金融服务问题中固有的结构化、多模态与动态特性。

Method: 提出FINESSE（金融事件序列仿真环境），一个基于智能体的仿真框架，用于生成由多个相互依赖事件流组成的合成结构化数据集。各事件流对应不同金融行为（交易、支付、账户状态变化、政策干预），具有各自的动作空间、模式与变量类型；这些事件流通过智能体的潜在演化状态相互耦合，从而支持时间维度上丰富的交互仿真。基于该仿真器构建基准数据集FINESSE-Bench。

Result: FINESSE-Bench支持四类代表性任务：余额预测、交易欺诈检测、漏缴预测与下一事件预测；并使用时间序列预测、事件序列建模、时序图与时间点过程等方法给出了基线结果。

Conclusion: 作者开源FINESSE框架（含仿真器与数据集），以推动金融服务中结构化、多模态事件序列建模相关挑战的研究。

Abstract: Machine learning research in financial services is limited by the scarcity of representative open-source datasets. Existing resources are often narrowly focused on a single modality or task and fail to reflect the structured, multimodal, and dynamic nature inherent to many problems in financial services.
  In this paper, we introduce FINESSE, a Financial Event Sequence Simulation Environment, an agent-based simulation framework for generating synthetic, structured datasets composed of multiple interdependent event streams. Each stream corresponds to a distinct financial behavior such as transactions, payments, account status changes, and policy interventions, each with unique action spaces, schemas and variable types. These streams are coupled through agents' latent evolving states, enabling the simulation of temporally rich interactions.
  We also introduce FINESSE-Bench, a benchmark dataset generated by the simulator, supporting four representative tasks: balance forecasting, transaction fraud detection, missed payment prediction, and next event prediction. We report baseline results using methods from time series forecasting, event sequence modeling, temporal graphs, and temporal point processes. We release the FINESSE framework, including the simulator and dataset to accelerate research on structured, multimodal event sequence modeling challenges in financial services.

</details>


### [195] [Explainable Prediction from Mobile Sensing Data through LLM-guided Concept Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11995)
*Yuning Wang,Iman Azimi,Amir M. Rahmani,Pasi Liljeberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概念集成Transformer（CIT），利用预训练大语言模型生成基线感知的概念异常目标与置信度权重，实现小样本移动感知数据的可解释预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动感知可纵向监测日常行为与生理模式，但在小队列健康感知研究中，任务特定标签稀缺而传感数据异质性强，准确预测困难；同时模型输出需具备可解释性，应反映有意义的行为与生理模式，而非仅给出预测分数。

Method: 构建概念集成Transformer（CIT），并引入LLM引导的概念监督。使用预训练大语言模型自动生成基线感知的概念异常目标及对应置信度权重，无需人工概念标注，将概念层信息融入Transformer预测框架。

Result: 在两个纵向数据集上，CIT在AFFECT上取得最高F1（0.756），在PHQ-9数据集上并列最高（0.765）。学到的概念分数揭示了可解释的行为与生理模式；在AFFECT中，睡眠时长与睡眠质量在高、低负面情绪组间差异最明显。

Conclusion: LLM引导的概念集成有助于在小队列移动感知研究中实现准确且可解释的预测，说明概念级监督可缓解标签稀缺并提升模型透明度。

Abstract: Mobile sensing enables longitudinal monitoring of behavioral and physiological patterns in everyday settings. However, accurate prediction remains challenging in small-cohort health-sensing studies, where task-specific outcome supervision is limited relative to heterogeneous sensing data. Interpretability is also important, as model outputs should reflect meaningful behavioral and physiological patterns rather than predictive scores alone. We develop a Concept-Integrated Transformer (CIT) with LLM-guided concept supervision for explainable prediction from mobile sensing data. CIT uses a pretrained large language model to generate baseline-aware concept abnormality targets with confidence weights without manual concept annotation. Across two longitudinal datasets, CIT achieves the highest F1 score on AFFECT (0.756) and ties for the highest on a PHQ-9 dataset (0.765). The learned concept scores also reveal interpretable behavioral and physiological patterns; in AFFECT, sleep quantity and quality show the clearest difference between high and low negative affect groups. These findings support LLM-guided concept integration for accurate and interpretable prediction in small-cohort mobile sensing studies.

</details>


### [196] [DCRA: Diffusion-Conditioned Representation Alignment for Robust Time-Series Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11997)
*Wenrui Xu,Anas Enanaa,Keshab K. Parhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCRA框架，将扩散前向过程作为结构化噪声调度器，结合特征级一致性目标，实现噪声与分布偏移下时间序列的鲁棒表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列（如EEG、ECG）在噪声和分布偏移下的鲁棒表征学习仍具挑战；传统增强与一致性方法依赖独立采样的扰动，缺乏结构化、可控的噪声演化机制，难以在潜在空间中保持语义一致与类别判别性。

Method: DCRA复用扩散前向过程作为结构化腐蚀调度器，沿噪声水平构建连续、可控的表征演化轨迹；引入特征级一致性目标，在不同噪声水平间对齐表征并保持类别判别结构，从而促进保结构一致性。框架与编码器无关，可兼容状态空间模型与Transformer。

Result: 在CHB-MIT EEG癫痫检测数据集上，DCRA在多种噪声条件下持续提升性能，并在低假阳性率下取得更高灵敏度；分析表明其表征比基线及仅扩散模型更均衡、更具结构。

Conclusion: 将结构化腐蚀与表征对齐相结合，可为鲁棒时间序列学习带来显著收益，且该框架具备通用性与跨架构适用性。

Abstract: Learning robust representations for time-series signals under noise and distribution shifts remains challenging, especially in clinical applications such as electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. We propose Diffusion-Conditioned Representation Alignment (DCRA), a training framework that repurposes the forward diffusion process as a structured corruption scheduler for representation learning. Different from conventional augmentation and consistency-based methods that rely on independently sampled perturbations, DCRA introduces a structured corruption trajectory via the diffusion forward process, which enables continuous and controlled representation evolution across noise levels. We introduce a feature-level consistency objective that aligns representations across noise levels while preserving class-discriminative structure. This mechanism promotes structure-preserving consistency, which enables smooth and semantically coherent feature trajectories in latent space. The proposed framework is encoder-agnostic and can be integrated with state space models and Transformer architectures. The seizure detection experiments on the CHB-MIT EEG dataset show that DCRA consistently improves performance under multiple noise conditions and achieves higher sensitivity at low false-positive rates. Analysis reveals that DCRA produces more balanced and structured representations compared to baseline and diffusion-only models. These findings highlight the benefit of combining structured corruption with representation alignment for robust time-series learning.

</details>


### [197] [Fixed State, Long Reach: What a Constant-Size Cache Buys Block Diffusion at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11998)
*Vaibhav Singh,Pierre-André Noël,Torsten Scholak,Eugene Belilovsky,Oleksiy Ostapenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在大规模（30亿参数、3000亿token）上预训练三种块扩散去噪器（注意力、Mamba、混合），并统一通过单一缓存接口解码。结果显示：只有状态空间缓存的内存与单步延迟在任意上下文长度下保持恒定（O(1)），在256k token时相比注意力缓存实现4.3倍低延迟、11倍低内存、2.6倍单流吞吐，且可随批处理扩展至14倍聚合吞吐；同时Mamba与混合骨干在8–16倍训练长度外仍能检索，而注意力在2倍处即崩溃，且无质量损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽可并行解码token，但其双向去噪器无法使用自回归推理中朴素的KV缓存。块扩散通过逐块解码恢复缓存能力，但现有块缓存依赖注意力机制，内存为O(L)，且作为免训练改造仅是模型计算的近似。论文动机在于同时克服内存与精确性两大限制：利用能将已定稿块汇总为可复用状态的序列混合器支持块缓存，并通过相应的块因果训练目标使缓存变得精确。

Method: 在单一前沿目标下，于3000亿token上预训练三个30亿参数的块扩散去噪器（注意力、Mamba、混合），并通过单一缓存接口对三者进行解码。采用支持块缓存的序列混合器，将已定稿块汇总为可复用状态，配合块因果训练目标，使缓存精确而非近似。

Result: 仅状态空间缓存的内存在序列长度上为O(1)：其内存与单步延迟在任意上下文长度下保持恒定，而注意力缓存为O(L)。在256k token时（注意力已增至82GB、29 ms/step），Mamba缓存实现4.3倍低延迟、11倍少内存、2.6倍高单流吞吐；且因其占用恒定，可随批处理扩展，达到14倍聚合吞吐，而注意力无法超过单流运行。相同的线性状态偏置使Mamba与混合骨干在训练长度的8–16倍外仍保持检索能力，而注意力的检索在2倍处即崩溃，且无实测质量损失。

Conclusion: 基于状态空间（序列混合器）的块缓存相较注意力缓存更具优势：内存与延迟恒定、可随批处理扩展、长上下文检索能力强，且不带来质量损失。这为块扩散模型的快速推理与长上下文部署提供了可扩展的缓存方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models decode tokens in parallel, but their bidirectional denoiser rules out the naive key--value (KV) cache behind fast autoregressive inference. Block diffusion restores caching by decoding block-by-block, and the block caches deployed on it so far are tied to attention: O(L)in memory and, if used as training-free retrofits, only an approximation of the model's computation. Both constraints can be overcome: sequence mixers that summarize finalized blocks into a reusable state support block caching, and the corresponding block-causal training objective makes the cache exact. We study this recipe at scale, pretraining three 3B block-diffusion denoisers (attention, mamba, and hybrid) on 300B tokens under one single-frontier objective and decoding all three through a single cached interface. Only the state-space cache is O(1) in sequence length: its memory and per-step latency stay constant at any context length, while an attention cache remains O(L). At 256k tokens (where attention has grown to 82GB and 29 ms/step), the Mamba cache delivers 4.3x lower latency, 11x less memory, and 2.6x higher single-stream throughput; and because that footprint is constant it scales with batch as well, reaching 14x the aggregate throughput, where attention cannot run beyond a single stream. The same linear-state bias lets the Mamba and hybrid backbones keep retrieving out to 8-16x their training length, whereas attention's retrieval collapses at 2x, at no measured quality cost.

</details>


### [198] [Can We Trust LLM Judges: A Study of Capability-Dependent Biases and Multi-Judge Ensemble for Bias Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12002)
*Gemma Zhang,Prachi Badarayani,Asmi Kumar,Sadid Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究绝对评分场景下LLM作为评判者的系统性偏差，发现模型任务准确率可预测其评判准确率与偏差，但准确率无法保证公平性；提出无标签的校准加权多数投票（WMV）集成方法，在仿真实验中接近完美误差率知识的oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用作自动化评判者，但单个评判者存在系统性偏差，损害评估可靠性。已有工作多研究成对比较设定下的偏差，而绝对评分设定更贴近真实用例，却缺乏深入研究。此外，能力更强的被评模型系统性地获得更宽松的评判，仅靠准确率无法保证公平评估。

Method: 在4个基准、6个模型（36个评判-被评对）上开展实验，考察任务准确率与评判准确率、方向性偏差之间的关系。提出校准加权多数投票（WMV），依据在线估计的假阳性率和假阴性率对多个LLM评判者加权聚合；并引入基于分歧的估计器，仅从评判者间一致性模式推导误差率，无需真值标签或任务元数据。

Result: 任务准确率强烈预测评判准确率（多数模型上Pearson r≥0.90），并与方向性偏差负相关（r≤-0.83）；但更优秀的被评模型持续受到所有评判者更宽松的评判（r≥0.83）。在任务分布变化的仿真实验中，无标签WMV平均与具备完美误差率知识的oracle相差不超过0.5个百分点，优于单个评判者和未加权多数投票。

Conclusion: 有原则的多评判者校准能够在无需标注数据的情况下同时提升准确率并纠正系统性宽松偏差，为模型能力不断增强时实现可扩展、可靠的自动化评估提供了路径。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used as automated judges for model training and evaluation, yet individual judges exhibit systematic biases that undermine reliability. Much of prior work has studied biases in pairwise LLM-as-a-judge settings; in this paper, we focus on absolute scoring tasks, which mirror more realistic use cases. Across four benchmarks and six models (36 judge-examinee pairs), we show that a model's task accuracy strongly predicts its judging accuracy (Pearson $r \geq 0.90$ on most models) and inversely predicts its directional bias ($r \leq -0.83$), but that accuracy alone does not ensure fair evaluation: more capable examinee models consistently receive more lenient judgments from all judges ($r \geq 0.83$). To address this, we propose calibrated weighted majority voting (WMV), an ensemble evaluation method that aggregates multiple LLM judges weighted by online estimates of their false-positive and false-negative rates. We introduce a disagreement-based estimator that derives these error rates purely from inter-judge agreement patterns, requiring no ground-truth labels or task metadata. In a simulated experiment with shifting task distributions, our label-free WMV tracks an oracle with perfect error-rate knowledge to within 0.5 percentage points on average, outperforming both individual judges and unweighted majority voting. These results demonstrate that principled multi-judge calibration can simultaneously improve accuracy and correct for systematic leniency without requiring labeled data, offering a scalable path to reliable automated evaluation as model capabilities increase.

</details>


### [199] [QTrans: A Quantum Transformer for Sentiment Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12011)
*Ren-Xin Zhao,Xinjie Huang,Yahong Liu,Maoyu Ye,Jinjing Shi,Shi Wang,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QTrans模型，用参数化量子电路构建Q/K/V特征，以量子测量间的高斯距离计算注意力系数，并结合量子前馈网络、残差连接与层归一化，形成端到端可训练的量子-经典混合情感分类框架，在MR、CR、MPQA上分别达72.13%、69.51%、63.45%，较最优经典基线提升2.88、3.17、3.79个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模二分类情感分类中，否定、转折与跨词依赖等因素导致情感线索非线性耦合，传统轻量模型难以充分捕捉词元间的上下文关系。

Method: QTrans使用参数化量子电路构造query、key、value特征，由量子测量之间的高斯距离推导注意力系数；并整合量子前馈神经网络、残差连接与层归一化，构建端到端可训练的量子-经典混合框架。

Result: 在MR、CR、MPQA数据集上测试准确率分别为72.13%、69.51%、63.45%，较各数据集最优经典基线分别提升2.88、3.17、3.79个百分点。

Conclusion: QTrans拓展了参数化量子电路在轻量情感分析中的应用，并为后续量子多头自注意力建模文本关系的研究奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: In small-scale binary sentiment classification scenarios, factors such as negation, contrastive shifts, and cross-word dependencies lead to the non-linear coupling of sentiment cues, making it difficult for conventional lightweight models to fully capture the contextual relationships between tokens. To address this issue, we propose a model named QTrans, which uses parameterized quantum circuits to construct query, key, and value features and derives attention coefficients from Gaussian distances between quantum measurements. By further integrating a quantum feed-forward neural network, residual connections, and layer normalization, the model establishes an end-to-end trainable quantum-classical hybrid framework for sentiment classification. Experimental results on the MR, CR, and MPQA datasets show that QTrans achieves test accuracies of 72.13\%, 69.51\%, and 63.45\%, respectively, representing improvements of 2.88, 3.17, and 3.79 percentage points over the best-performing classical baselines for each dataset. Overall, QTrans expands the application of parameterized quantum circuits in lightweight sentiment analysis and lays an experimental foundation for further research into quantum multi-head self-attention for modeling textual relationships.

</details>


### [200] [Certified Safety Curation: Distribution-Free Guarantees for Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12014)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"认证安全筛选"(Certified Safety Curation),一种"先筛选后克隆"的离线强化学习安全流程:仅凭片段比较训练状态价值函数对整条轨迹打分,再用Learn-then-Test校准阈值,以无分布(α, δ)界认证所选训练集的不安全比例,最后进行行为克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全离线强化学习假设每条transition都有代价函数,但现实中往往只能通过比较短片段、偶尔询问回合是否超预算来判断安全性,细粒度逐transition标签不可得。作者希望在该弱监督设定下界定"仍能做到什么"。

Method: 采用filter-then-clone流水线:(1)用片段比较数据训练仅含状态的价值函数,对整条轨迹打分;(2)通过Learn-then-Test校准选择阈值,在无分布假设下给出所选子集不安全比例的(α, δ)认证界;(3)对认证后的子集做行为克隆。用oracle控件验证设计合理性。

Result: 在DSRL的15个任务中,策略有11个满足代价预算,仅比克隆真实安全子集(需逐轨迹标签)少一个;未认证变体达到12个。用认证筛选集重训后,最强的全标签方法在其中变得安全,而该方法自调代价目标无法达到同样效果。拒绝行为可预测:证书概率对池纯度有闭式表达。

Conclusion: 逐transition重加权即便拥有精确价值函数也会失败,因此应由价值函数筛选整条轨迹。据作者所知,这是首个对离线RL或模仿学习训练集构成进行认证的工作。

Abstract: Safe offline reinforcement learning assumes a cost function on every transition. We ask what remains possible when safety can be judged only by comparing short clips and occasionally asking whether an episode exceeded its budget. Certified safety curation answers with a filter-then-clone pipeline: a state-only value trained from segment comparisons scores whole trajectories, Learn-then-Test calibration certifies a selection threshold under a distribution-free $(α, δ)$ bound on the unsafe fraction of the selection, and behavior cloning follows. We are not aware of prior work certifying the composition of a training set for offline RL or imitation. Oracle controls justify the design: reweighting individual transitions fails even with an exact value, so the value selects whole trajectories. The policies satisfy the cost budget on eleven of fifteen DSRL tasks, one short of cloning the ground-truth safe subset, which needs a label on every trajectory; the uncertified variant reaches twelve. Retrained on the certified selection, the strongest full-label method becomes safe where no setting of its own cost target rescues it. Refusal is predictable: the certificate's probability has a closed form in the purity the pool attains, which the calibration sample estimates and the scorer enters only through.

</details>


### [201] [Inverting Self-Triggered Control: Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Sparse Denial-of-Service Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12016)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Tristan Schuler,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自触发强化学习控制（RL-STC）的稀疏控制调度问题反向转化为对抗性RL问题：学习最稀疏的干扰/DoS调度以破坏闭环稳定性，给出最小阻塞次数下界并验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自触发控制（STC）旨在学习保持Lyapunov衰减稳定性的最稀疏控制调度，但针对STC控制器的DoS调度攻击理论分析尚属空白。已有DoS计数预算分析仅限于周期性和线性时不变控制器，无法覆盖更复杂的学习型STC控制器。

Method: 将防御者的安全证书镜像为Lyapunov增大的可容许性谓词，构建对抗RL智能体学习最稀疏的干扰/DoS调度。理论上证明在满足Lyapunov契约的STC控制器下，迫使崩溃所需最小阻塞次数的被控对象属性下界，并作为推论恢复连续分组最优性的证书级类比。

Result: 在Pendulum、CartPole、Quadrotor2D上针对四个固定防御者（1个LQR和3个RL-STC）训练，所学对抗者是唯一能在所有被控对象上以100%成功率击溃所有防御者的方法：贪婪法在Quadrotor2D LQR上有42%回合失败，周期性方法在Pendulum LQR上有97%回合失败。在"每次失效阻塞时间"指标上超越基线达2.8倍，在Quadrotor2D LQR上绝对裕度最大。

Conclusion: 该工作将DoS调度计数预算分析从周期性和线性时不变控制器推广到STC控制器，理论与实验均表明学习型稀疏DoS调度在破坏稳定性方面显著优于贪婪和周期性基线，并在高斯观测噪声和仅位置观测等鲁棒性消融下保持优势。

Abstract: Self-triggered reinforcement learning control (RL-STC) learns the sparsest control schedule that preserves Lyapunov-decreasing stability under a Run-Time Assurance (RTA) override. We invert this: an adversarial RL agent learns the sparsest jamming or Denial-of-Service (DoS) schedule that destabilizes the closed loop, with a Lyapunov-increase admissibility predicate mirroring the defender's safety certificate. We prove a plant-property lower bound on the minimum jam count required for an immediate hold-last medium-access-control adversary to force a crash against a self-triggered controller (STC) satisfying a Lyapunov contract, and recover a certificate-level analog of the consecutive-grouping optimality of prior count-budget DoS scheduling as a corollary. This extends the DoS-scheduling count-budget analysis from periodic and linear-time-invariant to STC controllers. Empirically, we train against four fixed defenders per plant (one Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) and three RL-STC) on Pendulum, CartPole, and Quadrotor2D. The learned adversary is the only adversary that crashes every defender on every plant at $100\%$: greedy misses Quadrotor2D LQR on $42\%$ of episodes and periodic misses Pendulum LQR on $97\%$. On jam-time-per-failure it beats baselines by up to $2.8\times$, and shows its widest absolute margin on Quadrotor2D LQR. Robustness ablations show that Gaussian observation noise exceeding the initial-state magnitude and position-only observation both preserve $100\%$ failure rate and keep the learned adversary strictly ahead of both baselines on jam-time-per-failure.

</details>


### [202] [Toward Reliable Railway-Bogie Response Prediction Using Multifidelity TDNN and Physics-Informed Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12018)
*Gyeolhee Lee,Moosun Kim,Taewook Kwon,Jaehun Kim,Dongjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多保真度铁路转向架响应修正方法，将多体仿真视为低保真信息、滚振试验台测量视为高保真证据，结合试验锚定的保真度分配与物理信息差异学习，对多通道转向架响应历程进行修正，并在385 km/h留出工况上取得较高预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路工程需要能预测多种运行工况下车辆响应的仿真模型，但无法穷尽试验。与代表性测量吻合虽能提供关键证据，但仅在有限工况下标定并不能保证其他工况的准确性，因此需要一种能在有限试验数据下提升仿真外推能力的方法。

Method: 方法将多体仿真历程作为低保真信息、滚振试验台测量作为高保真证据，采用试验锚定的保真度分配与物理信息差异学习。用时延神经网络（TDNN）表示随工况变化的仿真趋势，通过基于开发集拟合的幅值对齐定义低保真基线；再用残差修正网络建模基线未解释的可复现响应分量并叠加到基线。引入有效动态平衡方程约束所学差异，以表征仿真与物理系统在惯性、阻尼、刚度及外部激励上的差异。训练目标结合该约束、残差匹配、时间平滑性以及选择性施加的位移-加速度一致性项。

Result: 在评估的重构案例中，修正后的响应取得平均决定系数0.8197、平均归一化均方根误差（NRMSE）4.6055%、平均归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）1.9297%，为在385 km/h留出工况下的准确响应预测提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 该方法通过多保真度融合与物理约束的差异学习，在有限试验条件下提升了铁路转向架响应预测的准确性，并在385 km/h留出工况上得到验证，表明其具备向未测工况外推的潜力。

Abstract: Railway engineers need simulation models that predict vehicle responses across operating scenarios that cannot be tested exhaustively. Agreement with representative measurements provides essential evidence, but calibration at a limited set of conditions does not guarantee accuracy elsewhere. We present a multifidelity railway-bogie response-correction method that treats multibody simulation histories as low-fidelity information and roller-rig measurements as high-fidelity evidence. This method combines an experiment-anchored fidelity assignment with physics-informed discrepancy learning for multichannel bogie-response histories. A time-delay neural network (TDNN) represents the condition-dependent simulation trend, and development-fitted amplitude alignment defines the low-fidelity baseline. A residual-correction network then models the reproducible response component not explained by this baseline and adds it to the baseline. An effective dynamic-balance equation constrains the learned discrepancy by representing differences in inertia, damping, stiffness, and external forcing between the simulated and physical systems. The training objective combines this constraint with residual matching, temporal smoothness, and selectively applied displacement-acceleration consistency terms. For the evaluated reconstruction case, the corrected response gives a mean coefficient of determination of 0.8197, a mean normalized root-mean-square error (NRMSE) of 4.6055 %, and a mean normalized mean absolute error (NMAE) of 1.9297 %. These results provide initial evidence of accurate response prediction at the held-out 385 km/h condition.

</details>


### [203] [Score-based Outlier Generation via Controlling the Radon-Nikodym Derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12113)
*Amartya Mukherjee,Tristan Milne,Kry Yik-Chau Lui,Stephanie Hazlewood,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于测度论的离群点定义（借助对数似然分布），推导似然重加权对扩散得分函数的影响，从而在无需重训练的情况下可控地生成低似然样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离群点对算法压力测试和理解系统在稀有条件下的行为至关重要，但其通常被描述为低似然事件，而现有生成方法很少显式控制似然度，缺乏对稀有事件生成概率的精确调控手段。

Method: 首先基于对数似然值分布给出测度论的离群点定义，保证以可指定的幅度将更高概率质量分配给低似然事件；进而推导似然重加权如何修改扩散得分函数，得到含控制项的得分缩放关系，该控制项来自似然分布的Radon-Nikodym导数；利用扩散模型内在的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群，设计指数插值控制器近似真实控制，无需重新训练扩散模型即可更新反向时间动力学。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够可控地生成低似然样本，且生成结果与数据几何结构保持一致。

Conclusion: 通过似然重加权与得分函数修正的理论联系，结合Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群驱动的指数插值控制器，可在不重训练扩散模型的前提下实现可控的离群点（低似然样本）生成，为稀有事件建模与算法压力测试提供有效工具。

Abstract: Outliers are important for stress-testing algorithms and understanding system behaviour under rare conditions. Despite being commonly described as low-likelihood events, existing generative approaches rarely control likelihood explicitly. In this work, we introduce a measure-theoretic notion of outliers based on the distribution of log-likelihood values, which is guaranteed to assign higher probability mass to low-likelihood events with a specifiable magnitude. Building on this formulation, we derive how likelihood reweighting modifies the diffusion score and use this relation to motivate a controlled modification of the reverse-time dynamics. In particular, likelihood reweighting implies a scaling of the score function with a control term derived from the Radon-Nikodym derivative of the likelihood distributions. Correspondingly, the updated score function can be obtained with no retraining of the diffusion model. We exploit the Ornstein-Uhlenbeck semigroup underlying diffusion models to motivate an exponentially interpolated controller which approximates the true control. Experiments demonstrate controlled generation of low-likelihood samples while remaining consistent with the data geometry.

</details>


### [204] [Reinforcement Learning for Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12020)
*John Zhuoyang Ye,Aarav Pabla,Jens Palsberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合强化学习与重要性采样，自动搜索量子纠错中低逻辑错误率的综合征提取实现，性能优于AlphaSyndrome与PropHunt。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 综合征提取实现的可能方案随综合征规模指数增长，不同方案的容错性差异巨大，因此需要寻找低逻辑错误率的实现；已有工作牺牲了解质量或可扩展性。

Method: 使用强化学习与重要性采样，在多个规模下自动搜索/调度综合征提取实现。

Result: 相比AlphaSyndrome和PropHunt，平均逻辑错误率分别降低25.9%和71.7%；在距离为15的表面码上最高降低97.8%。

Conclusion: RL加重要性采样方法在各个规模上均优于现有自动调度工具，展示了较好的可扩展性与解质量。

Abstract: A key subtask of quantum error correction is to extract a syndrome that, if nontrivial, signals an error. The number of possible ways to extract a syndrome grows exponentially with the syndrome size, and these implementations vary greatly in fault tolerance, as measured by their logical error rates. This creates a natural search problem: find an implementation with a low logical error rate. Previous work solves this problem but sacrifices either solution quality or scalability. In this paper, we use reinforcement learning and importance sampling to outperform previous work at all scales. Compared with the state of the art automatic scheduling tools AlphaSyndrome and PropHunt, our tool reduces the logical error rate by 25.9\% and 71.7\% on average, respectively, culminating with a reduction of 97.8\% for a surface code with distance 15.

</details>


### [205] [Rank-Efficient LoRA via Joint Tangent-Space Optimization under Isotropic Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12123)
*Zihan Zhu,Zhehang Du,Xuyang Chen,Tim Tsz-Kit Lau,Jiayuan Wu,X. Y. Han,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出LoRA的秩不仅决定表征容量，优化器还决定该容量被实际利用的程度（有效秩），并提出ISO-LoRA通过谱下降耦合LoRA因子更新以提升秩利用率与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常以名义秩（rank）控制LoRA适配器的参数预算与表征容量，但作者认为这一观点不完整：名义秩只决定容量上限，优化器决定了诱导出的权重空间更新实际使用了多少容量。以GPT-2适配为例，AdamW在相同名义秩下产生的每步更新奇异谱高度集中、有效秩低；Muon则使用更丰富的方向，并从增大LoRA秩中获益更一致。因此动机是揭示"秩利用率"这一被忽视的关键因素，并通过优化器设计提升参数高效适配的效果。

Method: 提出ISO-LoRA：通过权重空间中诱导切向扰动的谱下降（spectral descent）来耦合LoRA两个因子的更新，使更新能量更均匀地分布于奇异方向，从而提升秩利用率，同时保持与LoRA参数化的兼容性。并给出理论保证：在风格化的spiked-gradient模型下，通过单步分析证明ISO-LoRA相比标准逐因子优化器可获得更高的有效秩。

Result: 在0.1B–7B参数语言模型适配任务上验证，ISO-LoRA提升了有效秩与下游性能，在中等至较大LoRA秩下增益最强。

Conclusion: 秩利用率是LoRA优化的关键因素，优化器设计是通往更强参数高效适配的重要途径。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is an effective approach for adapting large pretrained models by learning low-rank weight updates. In practice, the LoRA rank is used to control an adapter's parameter budget and representational capacity. We show that this view is incomplete: while the nominal rank determines the representational capacity, the optimizer shapes how much of that capacity is used in the induced weight-space updates. In a case study of GPT-2 adaptation with LoRA, we observe a strong rank-dependent optimizer effect. Despite using the same nominal rank, AdamW often produces per-step updates with concentrated singular spectra and low effective rank, whereas Muon uses a richer set of directions and benefits more consistently from increasing LoRA rank. These observations motivate ISO-LoRA, an optimizer that couples the LoRA factor updates through spectral descent on the induced tangent perturbation in weight space. ISO-LoRA promotes updates that distribute energy more evenly across singular directions, improving rank utilization while preserving compatibility with the LoRA parameterization. We complement this design with theoretical guarantees showing that ISO-LoRA can achieve higher effective rank than standard factor-wise optimizers through a one-step analysis under a stylized spiked-gradient model. We validate this design on language-model adaptation across 0.1B-7B-parameter models, where ISO-LoRA improves effective rank and downstream performance, with the strongest gains at moderate-to-large LoRA ranks. Our results highlight rank utilization as a key factor in LoRA optimization and suggest that optimizer design offers an important path toward stronger parameter-efficient adaptation.

</details>


### [206] [Scalable Discrete-to-Continuous Channel Simulation for Compression and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12067)
*Joseph Rowan,Buu Phan,Ashish J. Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种离散到连续信道的信道模拟方案，使用固定数量的随机样本，运行时间与信道及输入无关，并通过极化与多级编码扩展到长块长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用信道模拟算法计算成本高、停止时间随机，最坏情况下需生成无限多个共享随机样本，限制了其在机器学习压缩系统中的应用。

Method: 从每个潜在目标分布生成一个（或固定数量）样本，而非从提议分布生成独立序列；对样本施加潜在置换，再用指数竞赛进行样本选择。利用极化和多级编码在O(n log n)时间内处理长块长度。

Result: 方案支持生成样本数与压缩速率之间的灵活权衡，达到O(n log n)时间复杂度，降低每符号开销。

Conclusion: 该方案适用于随机VQ-VAE的可变速率压缩，以及通过高斯机制的精确模拟实现通信高效的差分隐私分布式均值估计。

Abstract: Channel simulation has recently emerged as a useful component in machine learning systems where samples from a prescribed probability distribution are to be compressed. Yet, general channel simulation algorithms often suffer from high computational costs, random stopping times or, in the worst case, can require generating an infinite number of shared random samples. We introduce a scheme for both exact and approximate simulation of discrete-to-continuous channels which conversely uses a fixed number of random samples, and therefore has a runtime independent of the channel and the input. Unlike existing channel simulation schemes which generate a sequence of independent samples from a proposal distribution, our approach generates one sample, or alternatively a fixed number of samples, from each potential target distribution. We then apply a latent permutation to the samples before performing sample selection using an exponential race. Our scheme provides a flexible tradeoff between the number of generated samples and the compression rate. Using polar and multilevel coding, we scale our approach to handle long blocklengths in $O(n \log n)$ time in order to benefit from reduced per-symbol overhead.
